@@ -46,15 +56,48 @@ struct Move {
+#if FORGE_NOZERO_MOVE_BUFFERS
+// Storage for N objects of type T that is not initialised when its owner is constructed. A
+// default-initialised std::array<Move, N> is zero-filled because Move has default member
+// initializers; the move buffers below never read an element they have not written, so that fill
+// is wasted work on every construction. The std::byte array implicitly creates the T[N] array when
+// its lifetime begins ([intro.object], C++20, P0593R6) and std::launder yields a pointer to its
+// first element, so element access and pointer arithmetic are well defined. Owners must write an
+// element before reading it and must not value-initialise the storage (no `{}` initializer).
+template <typename T, std::size_t N>
+struct UninitArray {
+    static_assert(std::is_trivially_copyable_v<T> && std::is_trivially_destructible_v<T>);
+    alignas(T) std::byte bytes[N * sizeof(T)];
+
+    [[nodiscard]] T* data() { return std::launder(reinterpret_cast<T*>(bytes)); }
+    [[nodiscard]] const T* data() const { return std::launder(reinterpret_cast<const T*>(bytes)); }
+    [[nodiscard]] T* begin() { return data(); }
+    [[nodiscard]] const T* begin() const { return data(); }
+    [[nodiscard]] T& operator[](std::size_t index) { return data()[index]; }
+    [[nodiscard]] const T& operator[](std::size_t index) const { return data()[index]; }
+    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr std::size_t size() { return N; }
+};
+#endif
+
+#if FORGE_NOZERO_MOVE_BUFFERS
+    using iterator = Move*;
+    using const_iterator = const Move*;
+#else
+#endif
+#if FORGE_NOZERO_MOVE_BUFFERS
+    // Shrink only: elements at or past size_ have never been written.
+    void resize(std::size_t size) { assert(size <= size_); size_ = size; }
+#else
+#endif

+    UninitArray<Move, Capacity> data_;  // only [0, size_) is ever read
+#else
+#endif

+// Cuckoo table behind Board::upcoming_repetition (UCI CuckooRep). It holds one
+// entry per reversible move of a knight, bishop, rook, queen or king of either
+// colour between two squares {a, b} (a < b) that the piece connects on an
+// empty board: 2 x (168 + 280 + 448 + 728 + 210) = 3668 entries. The key of an
+// entry is the Zobrist difference of the two positions the move connects:
+// piece-on-a ^ piece-on-b ^ side-to-move. The table is a constant built at
+// compile time; these accessors exist for tests and diagnostics.
+namespace cuckoo {
+
+inline constexpr std::size_t Slots = 8192;
+
+struct Entry {
+    Piece piece = Empty;
+    int a = 0;  // the lower square
+    int b = 0;  // the higher square
+};
+
+// Number of stored entries (3668).
+[[nodiscard, gnu::cold]] std::size_t entry_count();
+// Zobrist key difference of `piece` moving between squares a and b.
+[[nodiscard, gnu::cold]] std::uint64_t move_key(Piece piece, int a, int b);
+// Exact lookup: the entry whose key is `key`, if any.
+[[nodiscard, gnu::cold]] std::optional<Entry> lookup(std::uint64_t key);
+// Slot contents for table-level tests (key 0 = empty slot).
+[[nodiscard, gnu::cold]] std::uint64_t slot_key(std::size_t slot);
+
+} // namespace cuckoo
+

@@ -0,0 +1,70 @@
+#pragma once
+
+#include "forge/search_tune.hpp"
+#include "forge/search_types.hpp"
+
+#include <algorithm>
+#include <cstdlib>
+
+// UCI CorrExt: correction-history extensions (test plan G-C5).
+//
+// The correction history (src/search/evaluation.inc) learns how far search
+// results have differed from the static evaluation for this pawn structure and
+// material; the size of the correction applied at a node,
+//     |correction| = |ss->static_eval - ss->raw_eval|   (centipawns),
+// says how little the static evaluation can be trusted there. With CorrExt on,
+// the main search (the second instance of src/search/alphabeta.inc,
+// Worker::search_corr_ext) extends singular moves more at such nodes:
+//   - the double-extension margin (AL1 s198: 24 cp below the singular beta)
+//     shrinks with |correction|;
+//   - a triple extension is added for nodes with a large correction whose TT
+//     move is singular by a wide margin;
+// and every extension beyond one ply is paid from a per-path budget.
+//
+// Per-path budget. SearchStack::double_extensions counts the extra plies
+// (extension - 1 for extensions above 1) granted by double and triple
+// extensions on the path to a node. AL1 s198 allows a double extension while
+// at most 6 have been granted, so a path holds at most 7 extra plies; CorrExt
+// grants an extension of e > 1 only while the count plus e - 1 stays within
+// tune::CorrExtBudget (default 7, the AL1 s198 allowance). With
+// |correction| = 0 (or every CorrExt weight 0) the rule is the AL1 s198 rule.
+namespace forge::corr_ext {
+
+// |correction| at a node; 0 when the node is in check (no static evaluation).
+// (The same measure as UCI CorrMargins, branch opus-O5.)
+[[nodiscard]] inline int correction_size(bool in_check, int static_eval, int raw_eval) {
+    if (in_check || static_eval == ValueNone || raw_eval == ValueNone) return 0;
+    return std::abs(static_eval - raw_eval);
+}
+
+// Distance below the singular beta that earns a double extension.
+// AL1 s198: 24; minus CorrExtDoublePct percent of |correction|, at least 0.
+[[nodiscard]] inline int double_margin(int correction) {
+    return std::max(0, 24 - correction * tune::CorrExtDoublePct / 100);
+}
+
+// Whether a triple extension is possible at this node: CorrExtTripleCorr > 0
+// and |correction| >= CorrExtTripleCorr.
+[[nodiscard]] inline bool triple_allowed(int correction) {
+    return tune::CorrExtTripleCorr > 0 && correction >= tune::CorrExtTripleCorr;
+}
+
+// Extension of a singular TT move (singular_score < singular_beta) at a node
+// whose path already holds `path_extra` extra plies: 1, 2 (double) or 3
+// (triple). PV nodes get 1, as in AL1 s198.
+[[nodiscard]] inline int singular_extension(bool pv_node, int singular_score, int singular_beta, int correction,
+                                            int path_extra) {
+    if (pv_node) return 1;
+    const int room = tune::CorrExtBudget - path_extra;  // extra plies still allowed on this path
+    if (room >= 2 && triple_allowed(correction) && singular_score < singular_beta - tune::CorrExtTripleMargin) return 3;
+    if (room >= 1 && singular_score < singular_beta - double_margin(correction)) return 2;
+    return 1;
+}
+
+// Extra plies on the path after a move searched with `extension`.
+// AL1 s198: path_extra + (extension == 2).
+[[nodiscard]] inline int path_extra_after(int path_extra, int extension) {
+    return path_extra + std::max(0, extension - 1);
+}
+
+} // namespace forge::corr_ext
diff --git a/include/forge/corr_margins.hpp b/include/forge/corr_margins.hpp
@@ -0,0 +1,61 @@
+#pragma once
+
+#include "forge/search_tune.hpp"
+#include "forge/search_types.hpp"
+
+#include <algorithm>
+#include <cstdlib>
+
+// UCI CorrMargins: correction-size pruning margins (test plan G-B3).
+//
+// The correction history (src/search/evaluation.inc) learns how far search
+// results have differed from the static evaluation for this pawn structure and
+// material. The size of the correction applied at a node,
+//     |correction| = |ss->static_eval - ss->raw_eval|   (centipawns),
+// is therefore a measure of how little the static evaluation can be trusted
+// there. With CorrMargins on, the main search (the second instance of
+// src/search/alphabeta.inc, Worker::search_corr_margins) widens the static-eval
+// margins of reverse futility pruning, futility pruning and null-move pruning
+// and cuts the late-move reduction by a share that grows with |correction|:
+// the larger the correction, the less it prunes and reduces.
+//
+// Every function here is the AL1 s198 formula plus a term that is 0 at
+// |correction| = 0, monotonic in |correction|, and 0 for every |correction|
+// when its tunable is 0 (tests/corr_margins_tests.cpp checks all three).
+// With the default weights |correction| is at most 64 cp (pawn table +-32 cp
+// plus material table +-32 cp); MaterialCorrectionWeight 300 allows 128 cp.
+namespace forge::corr_margins {
+
+// |correction| at a node; 0 when the node is in check (no static evaluation).
+[[nodiscard]] inline int correction_size(bool in_check, int static_eval, int raw_eval) {
+    if (in_check || static_eval == ValueNone || raw_eval == ValueNone) return 0;
+    return std::abs(static_eval - raw_eval);
+}
+
+// Reverse futility pruning: the node returns eval when
+// eval - rfp_margin(...) >= beta. AL1 s198: RfpMargin * depth - RfpImproving * improving.
+[[nodiscard]] inline int rfp_margin(int depth, bool improving, int correction) {
+    return tune::RfpMargin * depth - tune::RfpImproving * improving + correction * tune::CorrRfpPct / 100;
+}
+
+// Futility pruning of quiet moves: the move is skipped when
+// static_eval + futility_margin(...) <= alpha. AL1 s198: FutilityBase + FutilityMultiplier * lmr_depth.
+[[nodiscard]] inline int futility_margin(int lmr_depth, int correction) {
+    return tune::FutilityBase + tune::FutilityMultiplier * lmr_depth + correction * tune::CorrFutilityPct / 100;
+}
+
+// Null-move pruning is tried only when static_eval >= null_move_threshold(...).
+// AL1 s198: beta - 24 * depth + 180.
+[[nodiscard]] inline int null_move_threshold(int beta, int depth, int correction) {
+    return beta - 24 * depth + 180 + correction * tune::CorrNullMovePct / 100;
+}
+
+// Late-move reduction after all AL1 s198 adjustments (r) minus a share of it:
+// CorrLmrPct percent of r per 64 cp of |correction|, rounded down, at most r.
+// Reductions of 0 or less are returned unchanged.
+[[nodiscard]] inline int lmr_reduction(int r, int correction) {
+    if (r <= 0) return r;
+    return r - std::min(r, r * correction * tune::CorrLmrPct / 6400);
+}
+
+} // namespace forge::corr_margins
diff --git a/include/forge/correction_plus.hpp b/include/forge/correction_plus.hpp
@@ -0,0 +1,146 @@
+#pragma once
+
+// Extended correction history (UCI option CorrHistPlus, default 0 = off).
+//
+// AL1 s198 corrects the static evaluation with two learned tables, keyed by the
+// pawn structure and by the material signature (src/search/evaluation.inc).
+// CorrHistPlus adds three more keys, one option bit each, so that every key can
+// be gated on its own:
+//   1  Continuation: the previous two moves (our move two plies ago and the
+//      opponent's reply) as (piece type, destination) pairs        -> gate G-B1
+//   2  MinorPiece:   placement of both sides' knights, bishops and kings -> G-B2
+//   4  NonPawn:      placement of one side's non-pawn pieces incl. its king;
+//                    one table per side, both read at every node
+// This header holds everything that does not depend on search state: the keys
+// (hashes of piece bitboards computed on demand, so forge::Board is unchanged),
+// the table layout and the fixed-point update and blend rules. The search glue
+// is src/search/correction_plus.inc. tests/correction_history_tests.cpp checks
+// these functions against independent reference implementations.
+
+#include "forge/board.hpp"
+#include "forge/history.hpp"
+
+#include <algorithm>
+#include <array>
+#include <cstddef>
+#include <cstdint>
+
+namespace forge::corrplus {
+
+// Bits of forge::options::corr_hist_plus.
+constexpr int Continuation = 1;
+constexpr int MinorPiece = 2;
+constexpr int NonPawn = 4;
+constexpr int AllKeys = Continuation | MinorPiece | NonPawn;
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------- keys
+// One odd multiplier per piece role (own random draw), summed, then avalanched
+// so that the low bits used as the table index depend on every input bit.
+inline constexpr std::array<std::uint64_t, 6> KeyMultipliers{
+    0xccc786391d0cc457ULL, 0xac43abac3a456cc1ULL, 0xd2773bee220bddebULL,
+    0x678b09c87c6dca51ULL, 0x16efdac28abd75dbULL, 0xd508421d0238c1a9ULL,
+};
+
+// xor-shift-multiply finaliser (the output step of the public-domain SplitMix64 generator).
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr std::uint64_t avalanche(std::uint64_t x) {
+    x = (x ^ (x >> 30U)) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+    x = (x ^ (x >> 27U)) * 0x94d049bb133111ebULL;
+    return x ^ (x >> 31U);
+}
+
+// Knights, bishops and kings of both sides.
+[[gnu::always_inline]] [[nodiscard]] inline std::uint64_t minor_piece_key(const Board& board) {
+    return avalanche(board.pieces(WN) * KeyMultipliers[0] + board.pieces(WB) * KeyMultipliers[1] +
+                     board.pieces(BN) * KeyMultipliers[2] + board.pieces(BB) * KeyMultipliers[3] +
+                     board.pieces(WK) * KeyMultipliers[4] + board.pieces(BK) * KeyMultipliers[5]);
+}
+
+// Knights, bishops, rooks, queens and the king of one side.
+[[gnu::always_inline]] [[nodiscard]] inline std::uint64_t non_pawn_key(const Board& board, Color color) {
+    return avalanche(board.pieces(color, Knight) * KeyMultipliers[0] + board.pieces(color, Bishop) * KeyMultipliers[1] +
+                     board.pieces(color, Rook) * KeyMultipliers[2] + board.pieces(color, Queen) * KeyMultipliers[3] +
+                     board.pieces(color, King) * KeyMultipliers[4]);
+}
+
+// Slot of a hashed key in a CorrectionSize table (same rule as the pawn table).
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr std::size_t key_index(std::uint64_t key) {
+    return static_cast<std::size_t>(key & (CorrectionSize - 1U));
+}
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------- tables
+// Continuation table, directly indexed:
+// [side to move][type of our move two plies ago][its destination]
+//               [type of the opponent's last move][its destination].
+// The colours of both moved pieces follow from the side to move (a null move
+// in between gives no key), so piece types (1..6) suffice.
+constexpr std::size_t PieceTypes = 6;
+constexpr std::size_t ContinuationSize = 2 * PieceTypes * 64 * PieceTypes * 64;
+
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr std::size_t continuation_index(Color side, int our_type, int our_to, int their_type, int their_to) {
+    std::size_t index = static_cast<std::size_t>(side);
+    index = index * PieceTypes + static_cast<std::size_t>(our_type - 1);
+    index = index * 64 + static_cast<std::size_t>(our_to);
+    index = index * PieceTypes + static_cast<std::size_t>(their_type - 1);
+    return index * 64 + static_cast<std::size_t>(their_to);
+}
+
+// Entries are CorrectionGrain fixed point like the pawn table and never leave
+// +/-CorrectionLimit (8192), so 16 bits suffice: 768 KiB per search thread.
+using Entry = std::int16_t;
+struct Tables {
+    std::array<std::array<Entry, CorrectionSize>, 2> minor;                    // [side to move][key]
+    std::array<std::array<std::array<Entry, CorrectionSize>, 2>, 2> non_pawn;  // [key's colour][side to move][key]
+    std::array<Entry, ContinuationSize> continuation;                          // continuation_index()
+};
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------- division
+// The search glue (src/search/correction_plus.inc) is [[gnu::cold]], i.e.
+// optimised for size, and there GCC divides by a constant with idiv, even by a
+// power of two. These helpers give exactly the C++ quotient (rounded toward
+// zero) with the multiply / shift sequences GCC uses in hot code; the tests
+// check them against the / operator over the whole reachable range.
+
+// x / 2^Shift, for |x| < 2^31 - 2^Shift.
+template <int Shift>
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr int div_pow2(int x) {
+    return (x + ((x >> 31) & ((1 << Shift) - 1))) >> Shift;
+}
+
+// x / 100, for every 32-bit x.
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr int div100(int x) {
+    const long long scaled = static_cast<long long>(x) * 1374389535LL;  // ceil(2^37 / 100)
+    return static_cast<int>(scaled >> 37) + (x < 0 ? 1 : 0);
+}
+
+static_assert(CorrectionGrain == 1 << 8 && CorrectionLimit == 1 << 13, "div_pow2 shifts below assume these values");
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------- update
+// Step: the residual and depth weight of the pawn/material update
+// (Worker::update_correction): (search score - corrected static eval) in
+// CorrectionGrain units, clipped to +/-CorrectionLimit/4, times min(depth+1,16)/256.
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr int step(int best_score, int static_eval, int depth) {
+    const int residual = std::clamp((best_score - static_eval) * CorrectionGrain, -CorrectionLimit / 4, CorrectionLimit / 4);
+    return div_pow2<8>(residual * std::min(depth + 1, 16));
+}
+
+// One entry: add the step and decay the entry toward 0 by entry * |step| /
+// CorrectionLimit (a saturated entry moves less in its own direction and is
+// unlearned faster; at zero residual nothing changes), then clamp. The decay
+// keeps |entry| <= CorrectionLimit for |step| <= CorrectionLimit; the clamp
+// is the guarantee.
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr int apply(int entry, int step) {
+    const int magnitude = step < 0 ? -step : step;
+    return std::clamp(entry + step - div_pow2<13>(entry * magnitude), -CorrectionLimit, CorrectionLimit);
+}
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------- blend
+// Contribution of the enabled keys: sum of entry * weight (weights x0.01,
+// tune::Corr*Weight), in CorrectionGrain units, clamped to +/-TotalLimit
+// (64 cp) before it is added to the pawn + material correction.
+constexpr int TotalLimit = 2 * CorrectionLimit;
+
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr int blend(int weighted_sum) {
+    return std::clamp(div100(weighted_sum), -TotalLimit, TotalLimit);
+}
+
+} // namespace forge::corrplus
diff --git a/include/forge/datagen.hpp b/include/forge/datagen.hpp
@@ -0,0 +1,140 @@
+#pragma once
+
+// S32 self-play datagen v2: fixed-node self-play inside the engine process, written directly in the
+// residual trainer's .pos (FPOS v1) / .hce (FHCE v1) format (record layout: fen2bin.cpp and
+// hce_dump.cpp of the lock archive, read by train_resid_v3b.py load_set/load_set_mmap).
+//
+// The code is part of forge_core. The engine executable exposes it only when it is compiled with
+// FORGE_DATAGEN (command line "forge datagen ..."); the UCI loop is identical with or without it.
+// Game rules, record filters and labels follow nnue/forge-nnue-tools/forge_selfplay.py (play_game,
+// terminal_result, decisive_side, eligible_score), so the same opening gives the same game.
+
+#include "forge/board.hpp"
+
+#include <array>
+#include <cstdint>
+#include <optional>
+#include <string>
+#include <utility>
+#include <vector>
+
+namespace forge {
+class Searcher;
+}
+
+namespace forge::datagen {
+
+// FPOS board: 64 nibbles, square s (a1 = 0 .. h8 = 63) in byte s >> 1, low nibble for even s;
+// 0 empty, 1..6 white P N B R Q K, 7..12 black p n b r q k (the forge::Piece values).
+using PackedBoard = std::array<std::uint8_t, 32>;
+[[nodiscard]] PackedBoard pack_board(const Board& board);
+
+// FPOS meta byte: bit 0 = black to move, bits 1-2 = White's result * 2 (0 loss, 1 draw, 2 win).
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr std::uint8_t pack_meta(Color side_to_move, int white_result_x2) {
+    return static_cast<std::uint8_t>((side_to_move == Black ? 1 : 0) | (white_result_x2 << 1));
+}
+
+struct Record {
+    PackedBoard board{};
+    std::int16_t score = 0; // search score, side to move's view, centipawns
+    std::int16_t hce = 0;   // Evaluator::evaluate_uncached, side to move's view, clamped to +-32767
+    std::uint8_t meta = 0;  // pack_meta(); the result bits are filled in when the game ends
+    std::string fen;        // kept only for the optional text output
+};
+
+enum class Termination { Checkmate, Stalemate, Insufficient, Repetition, FiftyMoves, MaxPly, AdjudicatedWin, AdjudicatedDraw };
+[[nodiscard]] const char* termination_name(Termination termination);
+
+struct Config {
+    std::uint64_t nodes = 5000;     // fixed-node search per move ("go nodes"), used when depth == 0
+    int depth = 0;                  // fixed-depth search per move ("go depth") when > 0
+    int random_plies = 8;           // uniformly random legal opening moves before the first search
+    int max_ply = 300;              // game ply (from the move counters) that ends the game as a draw
+    int win_cp = 2000;              // win adjudication: |White-view score| >= win_cp ...
+    int win_plies = 4;              // ... for this many consecutive searched plies, same side (0 = off)
+    int draw_cp = 0;                // draw adjudication: |score| <= draw_cp ...
+    int draw_plies = 0;             // ... for this many consecutive searched plies (0 = off, the default)
+    int draw_min_ply = 0;           // ... once the game ply is at least this
+    int max_score = 3000;           // positions with |score| > max_score are not recorded
+    std::uint64_t games = 0;        // stop after this many completed games (0 = no game limit)
+    std::uint64_t positions = 0;    // stop after the game that reaches this many records (0 = no limit)
+    std::uint64_t seed = 1;
+    std::size_t hash_mb = 16;
+    std::uint64_t positions_per_file = 1000000; // a shard is closed after the game that reaches this
+    std::string out;                // output prefix: <out>_NNN.pos/.hce[/.txt]/.info, <out>.games
+    bool text = false;              // also write "FEN | score | result" lines (forge_selfplay.py text)
+    std::string openings;           // optional EPD/FEN start positions, one per line (default startpos)
+    std::string convert;            // convert mode: "FEN | score | result" text file -> .pos/.hce
+};
+
+struct Game {
+    std::vector<Move> opening;      // the random plies
+    std::vector<Move> moves;        // the searched plies
+    std::vector<Record> records;    // eligible positions, meta result bits already set
+    int white_result_x2 = 1;
+    Termination termination = Termination::MaxPly;
+};
+
+// SplitMix64 (Steele, Lea, Flood 2014); fully specified, so a seed reproduces on every platform.
+class Rng {
+public:
+    explicit Rng(std::uint64_t seed) : state_(seed) {}
+    std::uint64_t next();
+    // Uniform enough for move choice (n <= 256): next() % n.
+    std::uint64_t below(std::uint64_t n) { return next() % n; }
+private:
+    std::uint64_t state_;
+};
+
+// Seed of game attempt `index`: games are independent of each other and of the process count.
+[[nodiscard]] std::uint64_t game_seed(std::uint64_t run_seed, std::uint64_t index);
+
+// Ply implied by the move counters (forge_selfplay.py uses python-chess Board.ply()).
+[[nodiscard]] int game_ply(const Board& board);
+
+// forge_selfplay.py terminal_result(): White's result * 2 and why, or nullopt while the game goes on.
+[[nodiscard]] std::optional<std::pair<int, Termination>> terminal(const Board& board, int max_ply);
+
+// Plays one game from `start`. nullopt when the random opening already ended the game (such an
+// attempt yields no samples, as in forge_selfplay.py). Throws std::runtime_error on an engine fault.
+[[nodiscard]] std::optional<Game> play_game(Searcher& searcher, const Config& config, const Board& start, Rng& rng);
+
+// Shard output. Each file is written as "<name>.part" (exclusive create, fails if it exists),
+// then renamed to "<name>" without replacing an existing file; the .pos is published last, so a
+// .pos without .part means a complete shard. Several processes may write into one directory as
+// long as their prefixes differ; a reused prefix fails before any data is written.
+class ShardWriter {
+public:
+    ShardWriter(std::string prefix, std::uint64_t positions_per_file, bool text, std::string info);
+    void add(Record record);
+    // Closes the current shard if it reached positions_per_file (call between games).
+    void maybe_close();
+    // Closes the current shard if it holds any record.
+    void close();
+    [[nodiscard]] std::uint64_t written() const { return written_; }
+    [[nodiscard]] std::uint64_t shards() const { return shard_index_; }
+    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t pending() const { return records_.size(); }
+private:
+    std::string prefix_;
+    std::uint64_t positions_per_file_;
+    bool text_;
+    std::string info_;
+    std::vector<Record> records_;
+    std::uint64_t shard_index_ = 0;
+    std::uint64_t written_ = 0;
+};
+
+[[nodiscard]] std::string shard_name(const std::string& prefix, std::uint64_t index);
+// Bytes of the .pos / .hce file for these records (little endian, as fen2bin / hce_dump).
+[[nodiscard]] std::string pos_bytes(const std::vector<Record>& records);
+[[nodiscard]] std::string hce_bytes(const std::vector<Record>& records);
+// Writes `bytes` to `path` via "<path>.part"; throws std::runtime_error if either name exists.
+void write_new_file(const std::string& path, const std::string& bytes);
+
+// One "FEN | score | result" line -> record (fen2bin + hce_dump semantics); nullopt if invalid.
+[[nodiscard]] std::optional<Record> parse_text_record(const std::string& line);
+
+// "forge datagen <options>" (argv excludes the program name and "datagen"); returns the exit code.
+int run(int argc, const char* const* argv);
+
+} // namespace forge::datagen
diff --git a/include/forge/hist_rework.hpp b/include/forge/hist_rework.hpp
@@ -0,0 +1,109 @@
+#pragma once
+// UCI HistRework (Astra A1), default 0 = the AL1 s198 lock search. Levels 1..3 rework how the quiet history
+// and the static-eval correction learn and blend:
+//   - the butterfly history gets a twin keyed by whether the move's from/to square is attacked by a LESSER
+//     enemy piece; quiet_history reads a blend of the lock table and the threat-keyed twin;
+//   - a correction table keyed by pawn structure x non-pawn material (one joint key) is added to the
+//     pawn + material corrections; it learns with a gravity-style (self-damping) update.
+// The lock's own tables keep their exact update and read rules. Design, overflow proof and tuning ranges:
+// opus_round1/sol_A1/forge_astra_A1_design_20261027.md.
+#include "forge/bitboard.hpp"
+#include "forge/board.hpp"
+
+#include <array>
+#include <cstddef>
+#include <cstdint>
+
+namespace forge {
+
+namespace search_options {
+// 0 = off (the lock). 1..3 = the tune steps below. Set only between searches (UCI setoption stops the search).
+extern int hist_rework;
+} // namespace search_options
+
+namespace hist_rework {
+
+constexpr int MaxLevel = 3;
+constexpr int HistoryLimit = 16384;        // the lock's HistoryMax (search.cpp asserts the equality)
+constexpr int CorrectionEntryLimit = 8192; // the lock's CorrectionLimit (search.cpp asserts the equality)
+
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr int clamp_level(int level) { return level < 1 ? 1 : (level > MaxLevel ? MaxLevel : level); }
+// Quarters of the butterfly term read from the threat-keyed twin: level 1, 2, 3 -> 2, 3, 4 (50 %, 75 %, 100 %).
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr int threat_quarters(int level) { return clamp_level(level) + 1; }
+// Read weight (x0.01) of the pawn x non-pawn-material correction: level 1, 2, 3 -> 50, 75, 100.
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr int joint_weight(int level) { return 25 * clamp_level(level) + 25; }
+
+// Squares attacked by a lesser enemy piece, indexed by the type of the piece that stands (or would stand)
+// there. Pawns, knights, bishops: enemy pawn attacks. Rooks: + enemy knights and bishops. Queens: + enemy
+// rooks. King and NoPieceType: empty (a king never moves onto an attacked square; evasions are their own
+// stage). The side to move is "us"; attacks use the current occupancy (no x-rays, pins ignored).
+struct LesserThreats {
+    std::array<Bitboard, 7> by_type{};
+};
+
+[[nodiscard]] inline LesserThreats lesser_threats(const Board& board) {
+    const Color them = opposite(board.side_to_move());
+    const Bitboard occupancy = board.occupied();
+    const Bitboard by_pawn = pawn_attacks_bb(them, board.pieces(them, Pawn));
+    Bitboard by_minor = by_pawn;
+    for (Bitboard bb = board.pieces(them, Knight); bb != 0;) by_minor |= attacks::knight(pop_lsb(bb));
+    for (Bitboard bb = board.pieces(them, Bishop); bb != 0;) by_minor |= attacks::bishop(pop_lsb(bb), occupancy);
+    Bitboard by_rook = by_minor;
+    for (Bitboard bb = board.pieces(them, Rook); bb != 0;) by_rook |= attacks::rook(pop_lsb(bb), occupancy);
+    LesserThreats threats;
+    threats.by_type[static_cast<std::size_t>(Pawn)] = by_pawn;
+    threats.by_type[static_cast<std::size_t>(Knight)] = by_pawn;
+    threats.by_type[static_cast<std::size_t>(Bishop)] = by_pawn;
+    threats.by_type[static_cast<std::size_t>(Rook)] = by_minor;
+    threats.by_type[static_cast<std::size_t>(Queen)] = by_rook;
+    return threats;
+}
+
+// 0 or 1: is `square` (0..63) in `set`.
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr std::size_t square_bit(Bitboard set, int square) {
+    return static_cast<std::size_t>((set >> square) & 1U);
+}
+
+// Butterfly term read by quiet_history: quarters/4 from the threat-keyed twin, the rest from the lock table.
+// |plain|, |threat| <= HistoryLimit and 0 <= quarters <= 4 give |numerator| <= 4 * HistoryLimit = 65536 and
+// |result| <= HistoryLimit (C++ division truncates toward zero).
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr int blend(int plain, int threat, int quarters) {
+    return (plain * (4 - quarters) + threat * quarters) / 4;
+}
+
+// Gravity-style update of a joint-correction entry: entry + step - entry * |step| / limit. For |entry| <= limit
+// and |step| <= limit the result stays within +-limit (design note, section 4); the clamp is a guard that the
+// unit test shows never changes a value on that domain. |entry * step| <= limit^2 = 2^26 fits int32.
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr std::int32_t gravity(std::int32_t entry, std::int32_t step, std::int32_t limit) {
+    const std::int32_t magnitude = step < 0 ? -step : step;
+    const std::int32_t next = entry + step - entry * magnitude / limit;
+    return next < -limit ? -limit : (next > limit ? limit : next);
+}
+
+// Non-pawn material signature: 4 bits per (colour, knight/bishop/rook/queen) count, saturated at 15.
+[[nodiscard]] inline std::uint32_t non_pawn_material(const Board& board) {
+    std::uint32_t signature = 0;
+    for (const Color color : {White, Black}) {
+        for (int type = Knight; type <= Queen; ++type) {
+            const int count = popcount(board.pieces(color, static_cast<PieceType>(type)));
+            signature = (signature << 4U) | static_cast<std::uint32_t>(count < 15 ? count : 15);
+        }
+    }
+    return signature;
+}
+
+// SplitMix64 finaliser (a public-domain mixing function): every signature bit reaches the low index bits.
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr std::uint64_t mix64(std::uint64_t z) {
+    z = (z ^ (z >> 30U)) * 0xBF58476D1CE4E5B9ULL;
+    z = (z ^ (z >> 27U)) * 0x94D049BB133111EBULL;
+    return z ^ (z >> 31U);
+}
+
+// Key of the pawn-structure x non-pawn-material correction: the Zobrist pawn key (pawn placement of both
+// colours) mixed with the non-pawn material signature. Unsigned arithmetic only (wraps by definition).
+[[nodiscard]] inline std::uint64_t joint_key(const Board& board) {
+    return board.pawn_key() ^ mix64(static_cast<std::uint64_t>(non_pawn_material(board)) + 1U);
+}
+
+} // namespace hist_rework
+} // namespace forge
diff --git a/include/forge/history.hpp b/include/forge/history.hpp
@@ -0,0 +1,64 @@
+#pragma once
+
+#include "forge/board.hpp"
+#include "forge/search_tune.hpp"
+
+#include <algorithm>
+#include <array>
+#include <cstddef>
+#include <cstdint>
+#include <cstdlib>
+#include <memory>
+
+namespace forge {
+
+struct SearchStack;
+
+// Move-ordering statistics.
+using ButterflyHistory = std::array<std::array<std::array<std::int16_t, 64>, 64>, 2>;
+using PieceToHistory = std::array<std::array<std::int16_t, 64>, 13>;
+using ContinuationHistory = std::array<std::array<PieceToHistory, 64>, 13>;
+using CaptureHistory = std::array<std::array<std::array<std::int16_t, 7>, 64>, 13>;
+using CounterMoveTable = std::array<std::array<Move, 64>, 13>;
+constexpr std::size_t CorrectionSize = 1U << 14U;
+constexpr int CorrectionGrain = 256;
+constexpr int CorrectionLimit = 32 * CorrectionGrain;
+using CorrectionHistory = std::array<std::array<std::int32_t, CorrectionSize>, 2>;
+// UCI HistRework: [side][from attacked by a lesser enemy piece][to attacked by a lesser enemy piece][from][to].
+using LesserThreatHistory = std::array<std::array<std::array<std::array<std::array<std::int16_t, 64>, 64>, 2>, 2>, 2>;
+
+// History entries saturate at +/-HistoryMax.
+constexpr int HistoryMax = 16384;
+
+// Bonus earned by a move that produced a cutoff at `depth` (tunable shape).
+[[nodiscard]] inline int history_bonus(int depth) {
+    return std::min(tune::HistoryBonusMax, tune::HistoryBonusQuadratic * depth * depth + tune::HistoryBonusLinear * depth - 32);
+}
+
+// History gravity: entry += bonus - entry * |bonus| / HistoryMax, with the
+// bonus clamped to +/-HistoryMax, so entries stay inside +/-HistoryMax.
+inline void apply_history(std::int16_t& entry, int bonus) {
+    const int clamped = std::clamp(bonus, -HistoryMax, HistoryMax);
+    entry = static_cast<std::int16_t>(entry + clamped - entry * std::abs(clamped) / HistoryMax);
+}
+
+// The move-ordering tables of one search thread: main (butterfly) history,
+// capture history, continuation history and counter moves. Their scoring and
+// update rules (quiet_history, capture_history_value, update_continuation,
+// update_quiet_stats, update_capture_stats) are Worker members defined in
+// src/search/history.inc, so the search calls them exactly as in AL1 s198.
+struct HistoryTables {
+    void clear() {
+        *main_history = ButterflyHistory{};
+        *capture_history = CaptureHistory{};
+        *continuation_history = ContinuationHistory{};
+        *counter_moves = CounterMoveTable{};
+    }
+
+    std::unique_ptr<ButterflyHistory> main_history = std::make_unique<ButterflyHistory>();
+    std::unique_ptr<CaptureHistory> capture_history = std::make_unique<CaptureHistory>();
+    std::unique_ptr<ContinuationHistory> continuation_history = std::make_unique<ContinuationHistory>();
+    std::unique_ptr<CounterMoveTable> counter_moves = std::make_unique<CounterMoveTable>();
+};
+
+} // namespace forge
diff --git a/include/forge/material_key_table.hpp b/include/forge/material_key_table.hpp
@@ -0,0 +1,60 @@
+#pragma once
+
+#include "forge/board.hpp"
+
+#include <array>
+#include <cstddef>
+#include <cstdint>
+
+// Job S11 (node-exact speed), UCI MaterialKeyTable (default off); also piece S11 of the speed stack S29
+// (compile switch FORGE_SPEEDSTACK, default off).
+//
+// The material-correction key of the search (src/search.cpp material_key) is a
+// Horner polynomial in the ten piece counts c0..c9 (white pawn..queen, then
+// black pawn..queen), evaluated in unsigned 64-bit arithmetic:
+//
+//     key = (((c0+1)*P + (c1+1))*P + ... + (c9+1)) * M  =  sum_s (c_s+1) * M * P^(9-s)   (mod 2^64)
+//
+// Multiplication distributes over addition modulo 2^64, so the key is a sum of
+// ten independent per-slot terms. Contributions[s][c] holds (c+1) * M * P^(9-s)
+// for every popcount c = 0..64, and table_key() adds ten table entries instead
+// of running ten dependent multiply-adds and the final multiply. The two keys
+// are equal for every board (tests/material_key_tests.cpp checks the table
+// against the polynomial on the whole count domain and on real positions).
+namespace forge::matkey {
+
+inline constexpr std::uint64_t HornerMultiplier = 1099511628211ULL;  // P, as in material_key
+inline constexpr std::uint64_t FinalMix = 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15ULL;     // M, as in material_key
+inline constexpr std::size_t Slots = 10;                             // 2 colours x pawn..queen
+inline constexpr std::size_t CountValues = 65;                       // popcount of a bitboard: 0..64
+
+using ContributionTable = std::array<std::array<std::uint64_t, CountValues>, Slots>;
+
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr ContributionTable make_contributions() {
+    ContributionTable values{};
+    std::uint64_t factor = FinalMix;  // slot 9 (black queens) is multiplied by M only
+    for (std::size_t slot = Slots; slot-- > 0;) {
+        for (std::size_t count = 0; count < CountValues; ++count) {
+            values[slot][count] = factor * (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(count) + 1U);
+        }
+        factor *= HornerMultiplier;
+    }
+    return values;
+}
+
+alignas(64) inline constexpr ContributionTable Contributions = make_contributions();
+
+// The search's material key, from the table.
+[[nodiscard, gnu::always_inline]] inline std::uint64_t table_key(const Board& board) {
+    std::uint64_t key = 0;
+    std::size_t slot = 0;
+    for (const Color color : {White, Black}) {
+        for (int type = Pawn; type <= Queen; ++type) {
+            const auto count = static_cast<std::size_t>(popcount(board.pieces(color, static_cast<PieceType>(type))));
+            key += Contributions[slot++][count];
+        }
+    }
+    return key;
+}
+
+} // namespace forge::matkey
diff --git a/include/forge/net_scale.hpp b/include/forge/net_scale.hpp
@@ -0,0 +1,27 @@
+#pragma once
+
+#include <cstdint>
+
+// S41 NetScale (UCI spin, percent; default 100 = OFF). With a network active, the static evaluation the search
+// uses (residual network: hand-crafted evaluation + network correction; stand-alone network: its output) is
+// multiplied by NetScale / 100 before the usual clamp, so a network whose evaluations are spread wider or
+// narrower than h256_e12's -- the scale the search margins were tuned on -- can be mapped back to that scale.
+// 100 returns the evaluation unchanged (the AL1 s198 search). The value is written only while no search runs
+// (the UCI loop stops the search first) and the hash is cleared, because the TT stores static evaluations.
+// The hand-crafted-only path (UseNNUE false) is never scaled.
+namespace forge::net_scale {
+
+inline constexpr int Default = 100;
+inline constexpr int Minimum = 50;
+inline constexpr int Maximum = 150;
+
+extern int percent;
+
+// eval * pct / 100 in 64-bit arithmetic, truncated toward zero (odd: apply(-e) == -apply(e)); pct == 100 is the
+// identity. Callers clamp the result to the non-mate range.
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr int apply(int eval, int pct) {
+    if (pct == Default) return eval;
+    return static_cast<int>(static_cast<std::int64_t>(eval) * pct / 100);
+}
+
+} // namespace forge::net_scale
diff --git a/include/forge/nnue.hpp b/include/forge/nnue.hpp
@@ -29,7 +29,12 @@ constexpr float SigmoidK = 2.25F;        // network output is in sigmoid units o
+#if !FORGE_NNUE_O2
+#else  // FORGE_NNUE_O2
+    // 64-byte aligned: every 512-bit (or 256-bit) access stays inside one cache line.
+    alignas(64) std::array<std::array<float, HiddenSize>, 2> values{};
+#endif // FORGE_NNUE_O2
diff --git a/include/forge/nnue_v2.hpp b/include/forge/nnue_v2.hpp
@@ -0,0 +1,705 @@
+#pragma once
+
+// v2 networks, loaded from a file at run time: king-bucketed input layer (C4)
+// and output buckets by piece count (C5). Used only when UCI NNUEKingBuckets
+// or NNUEOutputBuckets is > 0 and a matching file is loaded with UCI NNUEFile
+// (forge/search_options.hpp); with none of these options set the compiled-in
+// network (forge/nnue.hpp) is used and nothing here runs. A request that cannot
+// be honoured is an error, never a silent fall-back to the compiled-in network
+// (see options::nnue_v2_problem and src/uci.cpp).
+//
+// Feature of piece `pc` on `square` for perspective p (own king on `ksq`):
+//   r      = p == White ? square : square ^ 56       (rank flip, as forge/nnue.hpp)
+//   kr     = p == White ? ksq : ksq ^ 56
+//   flip   = (mirror flag && file_of(kr) >= 4) ? 7 : 0   (own king on files e-h)
+//   bucket = king_bucket_map[kr]                     (64 entries, indexed before mirroring)
+//   index  = bucket * 768 + ((colour(pc) == p ? 0 : 1) * 6 + type(pc) - 1) * 64 + (r ^ flip)
+// With one bucket and no mirroring this is nnue::feature_index(). The output
+// layer has the compiled-in architecture (SCReLU of the side-to-move and the
+// opponent accumulators, one linear output) and the arithmetic of
+// nnue::evaluate(); with output buckets (C5) the row of w2/b2 is
+//   output bucket = output_bucket_map[popcount(occupied squares)]   (33 entries, kings included)
+// The trainer's default map for N buckets puts n = pieces - 2 non-king pieces in
+// bucket min(N - 1, n * N / 32): for N = 8 that is n / 4.
+//
+// File format v2 (little-endian), written by tools/nnue/forge_nnue_v2.py:
+//   0   char[8] "FORGENN2"      8  u32 version = 2        12 u32 header_bytes = 160
+//   16  u32 feature_set         20 u32 inputs = 768       24 u32 hidden (= HiddenSize)
+//   28  u32 king_buckets (1-32) 32 u32 output_buckets (1-16)  36 u32 flags (bit0 residual, bit1 mirror)
+//   40  f32 weight_scale 4096   44 f32 output_scale 4096  48 f32 sigmoid_k 2.25
+//   52  u8 king_bucket_map[64]  116 u8 output_bucket_map[33]   149 u8 reserved[11] = 0
+//   160 i16 w1[king_buckets][768][hidden], [i16 wt[ThreatFeatures][hidden] when feature_set bit 0],
+//       [i16 wp[PawnPairFeatures][hidden] when feature_set bit 1],
+//       i16 b1[hidden], i16 w2[output_buckets][2*hidden], i16 b2[output_buckets],
+//       then u64 FNV-1a 64 of every preceding byte.
+// feature_set: 0 = piece-square inputs only (C4); bit 0 = threat inputs (O6, UCI NNUEThreats); bit 1 = pawn-pair
+// inputs (O7, UCI NNUEPawnPairs); definitions below. An engine that does not know a bit rejects the file.
+// Output buckets (C5) combine with every feature set: the output row is chosen by the piece count.
+//
+// Exactness: weights are de-quantised like the compiled-in net (int16 / 4096),
+// so every accumulator value is an integer multiple of 2^-12 below 2^12 in
+// magnitude and every float addition/subtraction on it is exact. Incremental
+// updates, the refresh cache and a full refresh therefore give bit-identical
+// accumulators whatever the order of the terms.
+//
+// Layout discipline: everything here is inline (an out-of-line copy is a COMDAT
+// section) and the search code that uses it lives in .text.forge_v2, so the
+// default build's .text is unchanged (see src/search/evaluation_v2.inc).
+
+#include "forge/nnue.hpp"
+
+#include <algorithm>
+#include <array>
+#include <cstddef>
+#include <cstdint>
+#include <memory>
+#include <new>
+#include <string>
+#include <vector>
+
+#if defined(__SSE__) || defined(_M_X64) || defined(_M_AMD64)
+#include <xmmintrin.h>
+#define FORGE_NNUE_V2_SSE 1
+#else
+#define FORGE_NNUE_V2_SSE 0
+#endif
+#if defined(__AVX2__)
+#include <immintrin.h>
+#endif
+
+namespace forge::nnue::v2 {
+
+constexpr int MaxKingBuckets = 32;
+constexpr int MaxOutputBuckets = 16;
+constexpr int MaxWorkers = 256;       // refresh caches are per worker id (Threads max)
+constexpr int HeaderBytes = 160;
+
+// Threat inputs (O6; header feature_set bit 0). One feature per pair (attacker A on s, victim V on v) where A
+// attacks the occupied square v in the position: pawn capture squares, knight and king steps, bishop / rook /
+// queen rays up to and including the first piece. Not features (redundant): a king "attacking" the other king
+// (impossible in a legal position) and any piece attacking its own side's king (a king is never captured, so
+// defending it carries no exchange information). Feature of a pair for perspective p with king key k:
+//   rel(piece) = (colour(piece) == p ? 0 : 6) + type(piece) - 1
+//   index      = (rel(A) * 12 + rel(V)) * 64 + ((p == White ? v : v ^ 56) ^ k.flip)
+// The block wt[ThreatFeatures][hidden] is shared by all king buckets. A victim attacked by two pieces of the
+// same kind adds the column twice (the input is the pair count, not a 0/1 flag).
+constexpr int ThreatFeatures = 12 * 12 * 64;
+constexpr int MaxThreatPairs = 512;   // at most 16 attackers per occupied square (8 rays + 8 knight squares) x 32 pieces
+constexpr std::uint32_t FeatureSetThreats = 1U;
+
+// Pawn-pair inputs (O7; header feature_set bit 1). One feature per unordered pair of pawns (any colours) on the
+// same or adjacent files at most two ranks apart, described in the perspective's frame (relative square
+// r = (p == White ? s : s ^ 56) ^ k.flip, colours own / their): the anchor is the pawn on the lower relative rank
+// (on the same rank: the one on the lower file), offset o of the other pawn:
+//   o: 0 (same rank, file +1)  1 (rank +1, file -1)  2 (rank +1, same file)  3 (rank +1, file +1)
+//      4 (rank +2, file -1)    5 (rank +2, same file) 6 (rank +2, file +1)
+//   index = ((colour(anchor) * 2 + colour(other)) * 7 + o) * 64 + r(anchor)      colour: 0 own, 1 their
+// This covers phalanxes, chains (supported pawns), levers, rams, doubled pawns and pawns two squares apart.
+// One block wp[PawnPairFeatures][hidden] shared by all king buckets, after the threat block (if any).
+constexpr int PawnPairOffsets = 7;
+constexpr int PawnPairFeatures = 4 * PawnPairOffsets * 64;
+constexpr int MaxPawnPairs = 16 * PawnPairOffsets;   // a pawn anchors at most 7 pairs
+constexpr std::uint32_t FeatureSetPawnPairs = 2U;
+// The threat pairs of one placement (O6; threat_keys).
+struct ThreatList {
+    int count = 0;
+    std::array<std::uint16_t, MaxThreatPairs> keys;   // pair keys ((A - 1) * 12 + (V - 1)) * 64 + v (absolute)
+};
+
+// The pawn pairs of one placement for one perspective (O7; pawn_pair_features).
+struct PawnPairList {
+    int count = 0;
+    std::array<std::uint16_t, MaxPawnPairs> features;   // feature indices of one perspective (with its flip)
+};
+
+struct KingKey {
+    int bucket = 0;
+    int flip = 0;     // 0, or 7 when the perspective's king stands on files e-h of a mirrored net
+    [[nodiscard]] bool operator==(const KingKey&) const = default;
+};
+
+// 64-byte aligned float array.
+class AlignedFloats {
+public:
+    AlignedFloats() = default;
+    AlignedFloats(const AlignedFloats&) = delete;
+    AlignedFloats& operator=(const AlignedFloats&) = delete;
+    ~AlignedFloats() { release(); }
+    void allocate(std::size_t count) {
+        release();
+        data_ = static_cast<float*>(::operator new(count * sizeof(float), std::align_val_t{64}));
+        size_ = count;
+        std::fill(data_, data_ + count, 0.0F);
+    }
+    [[nodiscard]] float* data() const { return data_; }
+    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t size() const { return size_; }
+
+private:
+    void release() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) ::operator delete(data_, std::align_val_t{64});
+        data_ = nullptr;
+        size_ = 0;
+    }
+    float* data_ = nullptr;
+    std::size_t size_ = 0;
+};
+
+// Piece bitboards indexed by Piece code (index 0 unused).
+using Pieces = std::array<Bitboard, 13>;
+
+// Per-worker accumulator cache: for each (perspective, bucket, flip) the
+// accumulator of the last piece placement refreshed under that key (piece-square
+// inputs only; threat inputs are added on top, see refresh_full_cached).
+struct RefreshCache {
+    struct Entry {
+        alignas(64) std::array<float, HiddenSize> values;
+        Pieces pieces;
+    };
+    std::vector<Entry> entries;   // index ((perspective * king_buckets + bucket) * 2 + (flip != 0))
+    // O6: pair-count differences of two threat lists (all zero between uses; threat_delta).
+    std::array<std::int8_t, ThreatFeatures> threat_counts{};
+    // O7: the same for two pawn-pair lists of one perspective.
+    std::array<std::int8_t, PawnPairFeatures> pawn_counts{};
+    // O7: the pawn-pair lists (White, Black perspective) of each search-stack entry whose accumulator the extra-input
+    // path computed (index ply + 4); written together with that accumulator, like ply_threats.
+    std::vector<std::array<PawnPairList, 2>> ply_pawns;
+    // O6: the threat list of the placement of each search-stack entry whose accumulator the threat path computed
+    // (index ply + 4); written together with that accumulator, so a valid accumulator always has its list here.
+    std::vector<ThreatList> ply_threats;
+};
+
+struct Net {
+    std::string path;
+    int king_buckets = 1;
+    int output_buckets = 1;
+    bool mirror = false;
+    bool residual = false;
+    bool threats = false;   // O6: feature_set bit 0
+    bool pawn_pairs = false;   // O7: feature_set bit 1
+    std::uint64_t checksum = 0;
+    std::array<std::uint8_t, 64> king_map{};
+    std::array<std::uint8_t, 33> output_map{};
+    AlignedFloats w1;   // [king_buckets][InputCount][HiddenSize]
+    AlignedFloats wt;   // [ThreatFeatures][HiddenSize] (threat nets only)
+    AlignedFloats wp;   // [PawnPairFeatures][HiddenSize] (pawn-pair nets only)
+    AlignedFloats b1;   // [HiddenSize]
+    AlignedFloats w2;   // [output_buckets][2 * HiddenSize]
+    std::vector<float> b2;   // [output_buckets]
+    // Per worker id; slot i is created and used only by the worker with id i.
+    mutable std::array<std::unique_ptr<RefreshCache>, MaxWorkers> caches;
+};
+
+struct Description {
+    std::string path;
+    int king_buckets = 1;
+    int output_buckets = 1;
+    bool mirror = false;
+    bool residual = false;
+    bool threats = false;
+    bool pawn_pairs = false;
+    std::uint64_t checksum = 0;
+    std::array<std::uint8_t, 33> output_bucket_map{};
+};
+
+// ---- loading and selection (src/nnue.cpp; must not run during a search) ----
+// Loads a v2 file. The previously loaded net is unloaded first, so after a
+// failed load no net is loaded (the engine never evaluates with a net other
+// than the one last requested). The file size is checked (seek to the end)
+// before anything is allocated: a file smaller than a header, larger than the
+// largest valid v2 file or of another size than its header implies is
+// rejected after reading at most the 160 header bytes.
+[[nodiscard]] bool load(const std::string& path, std::string* error);
+void unload();
+[[nodiscard]] const Net* loaded();
+// Size in bytes of a v2 file with these counts (header + weights + checksum).
+[[nodiscard]] std::uint64_t file_bytes(int king_buckets, int output_buckets, std::uint32_t feature_set = 0);
+// Bytes of its file the last load() read (0, the header only, or the whole file).
+[[nodiscard]] std::uint64_t last_load_bytes_read();
+// "<path> king buckets N output buckets M hidden H mirrored|unmirrored residual|stand-alone fnv1a X".
+[[nodiscard]] std::string summary(const Net& net);
+// Makes the loaded net the active one if its architecture matches the options:
+// king_buckets > 0 needs exactly that many king buckets (0: one unmirrored
+// bucket), output_buckets > 0 exactly that many output buckets (0: one), and at
+// least one of them must be > 0. Returns the active net (nullptr: the
+// compiled-in network); message gets a one-line state.
+const Net* select(int king_buckets, int output_buckets, std::string* message);
+[[nodiscard]] Description describe(const Net& net);
+// Allocates the refresh cache of one worker (on first use).
+[[nodiscard]] RefreshCache& make_cache(const Net& net, int worker_id);
+
+// The net the search evaluates with (nullptr: the compiled-in network).
+extern const Net* active_net;
+[[nodiscard]] inline const Net* active() { return active_net; }
+
+// ---- evaluation kernels ----
+[[nodiscard]] inline KingKey king_key(const Net& net, Color perspective, int king_square) {
+    const int relative = perspective == White ? king_square : king_square ^ 56;
+    return {net.king_map[static_cast<std::size_t>(relative)], net.mirror && (relative & 7) >= 4 ? 7 : 0};
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] inline int feature(Color perspective, KingKey key, Piece piece, int square) {
+    const int relative_color = color_of(piece) == perspective ? 0 : 1;
+    const int relative_square = (perspective == White ? square : square ^ 56) ^ key.flip;
+    return key.bucket * InputCount + (relative_color * 6 + piece_type(piece) - 1) * 64 + relative_square;
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] inline const float* column(const Net& net, Color perspective, KingKey key, Piece piece, int square) {
+    return net.w1.data() + static_cast<std::size_t>(feature(perspective, key, piece, square)) * HiddenSize;
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] inline Pieces pieces_of(const Board& board) {
+    Pieces pieces{};
+    for (int code = WP; code <= BK; ++code) pieces[static_cast<std::size_t>(code)] = board.pieces(static_cast<Piece>(code));
+    return pieces;
+}
+
+// Takes back the piece changes of one move (`dirty`) on a piece placement.
+inline void undo(Pieces& pieces, const DirtyPieces& dirty) {
+    for (int i = 0; i < dirty.count; ++i) {
+        const PieceChange& change = dirty.changes[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)];
+        Bitboard& bb = pieces[static_cast<std::size_t>(change.piece)];
+        if (change.add) bb &= ~square_bb(change.square);
+        else bb |= square_bb(change.square);
+    }
+}
+
+// O6: applies the piece changes of one move (`dirty`) to a piece placement (the inverse of undo).
+inline void redo(Pieces& pieces, const DirtyPieces& dirty) {
+    for (int i = 0; i < dirty.count; ++i) {
+        const PieceChange& change = dirty.changes[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)];
+        Bitboard& bb = pieces[static_cast<std::size_t>(change.piece)];
+        if (change.add) bb |= square_bb(change.square);
+        else bb &= ~square_bb(change.square);
+    }
+}
+
+// ---- O6 threat inputs (definition at ThreatFeatures; ThreatList above) ----
+
+[[nodiscard]] inline Bitboard attacks_from(int code, int square, Bitboard occupied) {
+    switch (code) {
+    case WP: return attacks::pawn(White, square);
+    case BP: return attacks::pawn(Black, square);
+    case WN: case BN: return attacks::knight(square);
+    case WB: case BB: return attacks::bishop(square, occupied);
+    case WR: case BR: return attacks::rook(square, occupied);
+    case WQ: case BQ: return attacks::queen(square, occupied);
+    default: return attacks::king(square);
+    }
+}
+
+// Every threat pair of a piece placement (attackers in piece-code order, then by square).
+inline void threat_keys(const Pieces& pieces, ThreatList& list) {
+    std::array<std::uint8_t, 64> mailbox{};
+    Bitboard occupied = 0;
+    for (int code = WP; code <= BK; ++code) {
+        Bitboard bb = pieces[static_cast<std::size_t>(code)];
+        occupied |= bb;
+        while (bb != 0) mailbox[static_cast<std::size_t>(pop_lsb(bb))] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(code);
+    }
+    int n = 0;
+    for (int a = WP; a <= BK; ++a) {
+        const bool a_king = a == WK || a == BK;
+        const bool a_white = a <= WK;
+        Bitboard bb = pieces[static_cast<std::size_t>(a)];
+        while (bb != 0) {
+            const int s = pop_lsb(bb);
+            Bitboard targets = attacks_from(a, s, occupied) & occupied;
+            while (targets != 0) {
+                const int v = pop_lsb(targets);
+                const int b = mailbox[static_cast<std::size_t>(v)];
+                if ((b == WK || b == BK) && (a_king || (b <= WK) == a_white)) continue;   // redundant pairs
+                list.keys[static_cast<std::size_t>(n++)] = static_cast<std::uint16_t>(((a - 1) * 12 + (b - 1)) * 64 + v);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    list.count = n;
+}
+
+// rel(A) * 12 + rel(V) for the Black perspective, from the absolute (A - 1) * 12 + (V - 1).
+inline constexpr auto BlackThreatKinds = [] {
+    std::array<std::uint8_t, 144> kinds{};
+    for (int a = 0; a < 12; ++a) {
+        for (int b = 0; b < 12; ++b) kinds[static_cast<std::size_t>(a * 12 + b)] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(((a + 6) % 12) * 12 + (b + 6) % 12);
+    }
+    return kinds;
+}();
+
+[[nodiscard]] inline int threat_feature(Color perspective, KingKey key, int pair_key) {
+    const int square = pair_key & 63;
+    if (perspective == White) return (pair_key & ~63) | (square ^ key.flip);
+    return BlackThreatKinds[static_cast<std::size_t>(pair_key >> 6)] * 64 + ((square ^ 56) ^ key.flip);
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] inline const float* threat_column(const Net& net, Color perspective, KingKey key, int pair_key) {
+    return net.wt.data() + static_cast<std::size_t>(threat_feature(perspective, key, pair_key)) * HiddenSize;
+}
+
+// Multiset difference after - before of two threat lists (counts: all zero on entry and on return).
+struct ThreatDelta {
+    int removed = 0;
+    int added = 0;
+    std::array<std::uint16_t, MaxThreatPairs> remove_keys;
+    std::array<std::uint16_t, MaxThreatPairs> add_keys;
+};
+inline void threat_delta(const ThreatList& before, const ThreatList& after, std::int8_t* counts, ThreatDelta& delta) {
+    for (int i = 0; i < before.count; ++i) --counts[before.keys[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)]];
+    for (int i = 0; i < after.count; ++i) ++counts[after.keys[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)]];
+    delta.removed = 0;
+    delta.added = 0;
+    for (int i = 0; i < before.count; ++i) {
+        const std::uint16_t k = before.keys[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)];
+        for (; counts[k] < 0; ++counts[k]) delta.remove_keys[static_cast<std::size_t>(delta.removed++)] = k;
+    }
+    for (int i = 0; i < after.count; ++i) {
+        const std::uint16_t k = after.keys[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)];
+        for (; counts[k] > 0; --counts[k]) delta.add_keys[static_cast<std::size_t>(delta.added++)] = k;
+    }
+}
+
+// dst = src - sub[0..subs) + add[0..adds) over the hidden units, in chunks of 64 floats (exact; any order of
+// the terms gives the same bits). src may be dst. Every pointer is 32-byte aligned (accumulators, weight columns).
+// With AVX2 the 64-float chunk stays in eight registers while all columns are applied (plain sub / add, no FMA).
+inline void apply_lists(const float* src, float* dst, const float* const* sub, int subs, const float* const* add, int adds) {
+    constexpr int Chunk = 64;
+    static_assert(HiddenSize % Chunk == 0, "HiddenSize must be a multiple of the chunk");
+#if defined(__AVX2__)
+    for (int h0 = 0; h0 < HiddenSize; h0 += Chunk) {
+        __m256 v0 = _mm256_load_ps(src + h0);
+        __m256 v1 = _mm256_load_ps(src + h0 + 8);
+        __m256 v2 = _mm256_load_ps(src + h0 + 16);
+        __m256 v3 = _mm256_load_ps(src + h0 + 24);
+        __m256 v4 = _mm256_load_ps(src + h0 + 32);
+        __m256 v5 = _mm256_load_ps(src + h0 + 40);
+        __m256 v6 = _mm256_load_ps(src + h0 + 48);
+        __m256 v7 = _mm256_load_ps(src + h0 + 56);
+        for (int i = 0; i < subs; ++i) {
+            const float* col = sub[i] + h0;
+            v0 = _mm256_sub_ps(v0, _mm256_load_ps(col));
+            v1 = _mm256_sub_ps(v1, _mm256_load_ps(col + 8));
+            v2 = _mm256_sub_ps(v2, _mm256_load_ps(col + 16));
+            v3 = _mm256_sub_ps(v3, _mm256_load_ps(col + 24));
+            v4 = _mm256_sub_ps(v4, _mm256_load_ps(col + 32));
+            v5 = _mm256_sub_ps(v5, _mm256_load_ps(col + 40));
+            v6 = _mm256_sub_ps(v6, _mm256_load_ps(col + 48));
+            v7 = _mm256_sub_ps(v7, _mm256_load_ps(col + 56));
+        }
+        for (int i = 0; i < adds; ++i) {
+            const float* col = add[i] + h0;
+            v0 = _mm256_add_ps(v0, _mm256_load_ps(col));
+            v1 = _mm256_add_ps(v1, _mm256_load_ps(col + 8));
+            v2 = _mm256_add_ps(v2, _mm256_load_ps(col + 16));
+            v3 = _mm256_add_ps(v3, _mm256_load_ps(col + 24));
+            v4 = _mm256_add_ps(v4, _mm256_load_ps(col + 32));
+            v5 = _mm256_add_ps(v5, _mm256_load_ps(col + 40));
+            v6 = _mm256_add_ps(v6, _mm256_load_ps(col + 48));
+            v7 = _mm256_add_ps(v7, _mm256_load_ps(col + 56));
+        }
+        _mm256_store_ps(dst + h0, v0);
+        _mm256_store_ps(dst + h0 + 8, v1);
+        _mm256_store_ps(dst + h0 + 16, v2);
+        _mm256_store_ps(dst + h0 + 24, v3);
+        _mm256_store_ps(dst + h0 + 32, v4);
+        _mm256_store_ps(dst + h0 + 40, v5);
+        _mm256_store_ps(dst + h0 + 48, v6);
+        _mm256_store_ps(dst + h0 + 56, v7);
+    }
+#else
+    for (int h0 = 0; h0 < HiddenSize; h0 += Chunk) {
+        float chunk[Chunk];
+        for (int k = 0; k < Chunk; ++k) chunk[k] = src[h0 + k];
+        for (int i = 0; i < subs; ++i) {
+            const float* col = sub[i] + h0;
+            for (int k = 0; k < Chunk; ++k) chunk[k] -= col[k];
+        }
+        for (int i = 0; i < adds; ++i) {
+            const float* col = add[i] + h0;
+            for (int k = 0; k < Chunk; ++k) chunk[k] += col[k];
+        }
+        for (int k = 0; k < Chunk; ++k) dst[h0 + k] = chunk[k];
+    }
+#endif
+}
+
+// values += every threat column of `list` for this perspective and king key.
+inline void add_threat_columns(const Net& net, Color perspective, KingKey key, const ThreatList& list, float* values) {
+    std::array<const float*, MaxThreatPairs> add;
+    for (int i = 0; i < list.count; ++i) add[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)] = threat_column(net, perspective, key, list.keys[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)]);
+    apply_lists(values, values, nullptr, 0, add.data(), list.count);
+}
+
+// ---- O7 pawn-pair inputs (definition at PawnPairFeatures) ----
+
+[[nodiscard]] inline Bitboard mirror_files(Bitboard bb) {   // square s -> s ^ 7
+    constexpr Bitboard k1 = 0x5555555555555555ULL;
+    constexpr Bitboard k2 = 0x3333333333333333ULL;
+    constexpr Bitboard k4 = 0x0F0F0F0F0F0F0F0FULL;
+    bb = ((bb >> 1U) & k1) | ((bb & k1) << 1U);
+    bb = ((bb >> 2U) & k2) | ((bb & k2) << 2U);
+    return ((bb >> 4U) & k4) | ((bb & k4) << 4U);
+}
+
+// A bitboard in the perspective's frame: square s -> (p == White ? s : s ^ 56) ^ flip.
+[[nodiscard]] inline Bitboard relative_bb(Bitboard bb, Color perspective, int flip) {
+    if (perspective == Black) bb = __builtin_bswap64(bb);
+    return flip != 0 ? mirror_files(bb) : bb;
+}
+
+// Every pawn pair of a placement for one perspective (its king key's flip).
+inline void pawn_pair_features(const Pieces& pieces, Color perspective, int flip, PawnPairList& list) {
+    struct Offset { int shift; Bitboard anchors; };   // other pawn = anchor + shift; anchors whose other square is on the board
+    constexpr std::array<Offset, PawnPairOffsets> offsets{{{1, ~FileHBB}, {7, ~FileABB}, {8, ~0ULL}, {9, ~FileHBB},
+                                                           {15, ~FileABB}, {16, ~0ULL}, {17, ~FileHBB}}};
+    const std::array<Bitboard, 2> side{relative_bb(pieces[static_cast<std::size_t>(perspective == White ? WP : BP)], perspective, flip),
+                                       relative_bb(pieces[static_cast<std::size_t>(perspective == White ? BP : WP)], perspective, flip)};
+    int n = 0;
+    for (int o = 0; o < PawnPairOffsets; ++o) {
+        const Offset& offset = offsets[static_cast<std::size_t>(o)];
+        for (int c1 = 0; c1 < 2; ++c1) {
+            for (int c2 = 0; c2 < 2; ++c2) {
+                Bitboard anchors = side[static_cast<std::size_t>(c1)] & offset.anchors &
+                                   (side[static_cast<std::size_t>(c2)] >> static_cast<unsigned>(offset.shift));
+                while (anchors != 0) {
+                    list.features[static_cast<std::size_t>(n++)] = static_cast<std::uint16_t>(((c1 * 2 + c2) * PawnPairOffsets + o) * 64 + pop_lsb(anchors));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    list.count = n;
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] inline const float* pawn_pair_column(const Net& net, int feature) {
+    return net.wp.data() + static_cast<std::size_t>(feature) * HiddenSize;
+}
+
+// Difference after - before of two feature lists of one perspective (counts: all zero on entry and on return).
+struct PawnPairDelta {
+    int removed = 0;
+    int added = 0;
+    std::array<std::uint16_t, MaxPawnPairs> remove_features;
+    std::array<std::uint16_t, MaxPawnPairs> add_features;
+};
+inline void pawn_pair_delta(const PawnPairList& before, const PawnPairList& after, std::int8_t* counts, PawnPairDelta& delta) {
+    for (int i = 0; i < before.count; ++i) --counts[before.features[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)]];
+    for (int i = 0; i < after.count; ++i) ++counts[after.features[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)]];
+    delta.removed = 0;
+    delta.added = 0;
+    for (int i = 0; i < before.count; ++i) {
+        const std::uint16_t f = before.features[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)];
+        for (; counts[f] < 0; ++counts[f]) delta.remove_features[static_cast<std::size_t>(delta.removed++)] = f;
+    }
+    for (int i = 0; i < after.count; ++i) {
+        const std::uint16_t f = after.features[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)];
+        for (; counts[f] > 0; --counts[f]) delta.add_features[static_cast<std::size_t>(delta.added++)] = f;
+    }
+}
+
+inline void add_pawn_pair_columns(const Net& net, const PawnPairList& list, float* values) {
+    std::array<const float*, MaxPawnPairs> add;
+    for (int i = 0; i < list.count; ++i) add[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)] = pawn_pair_column(net, list.features[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)]);
+    apply_lists(values, values, nullptr, 0, add.data(), list.count);
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] inline bool touches_pawns(const DirtyPieces& dirty) {
+    for (int i = 0; i < dirty.count; ++i) {
+        const Piece piece = dirty.changes[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)].piece;
+        if (piece == WP || piece == BP) return true;
+    }
+    return false;
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] inline RefreshCache& cache(const Net& net, int worker_id) {
+    const std::unique_ptr<RefreshCache>& slot = net.caches[static_cast<std::size_t>(std::clamp(worker_id, 0, MaxWorkers - 1))];
+    if (slot == nullptr) [[unlikely]] return make_cache(net, worker_id);
+    return *slot;
+}
+
+// dst = src - sub[0] - ... + add[0] + ... (exact; any order gives the same bits).
+template <int Subs, int Adds>
+inline void apply_columns(const float* src, float* dst, const float* const* sub, const float* const* add) {
+    for (int h = 0; h < HiddenSize; ++h) {
+        float value = src[h];
+        for (int i = 0; i < Subs; ++i) value -= sub[i][h];
+        for (int i = 0; i < Adds; ++i) value += add[i][h];
+        dst[h] = value;
+    }
+}
+
+// Accumulator of one perspective from scratch (reference implementation; with
+// the threat inputs of a threat net).
+inline void refresh(const Net& net, const Board& board, Color perspective, float* values) {
+    const KingKey key = king_key(net, perspective, board.king_square(perspective));
+    std::copy(net.b1.data(), net.b1.data() + HiddenSize, values);
+    Bitboard occupied = board.occupied();
+    while (occupied != 0) {
+        const int square = pop_lsb(occupied);
+        const float* col = column(net, perspective, key, board.piece_at(square), square);
+        for (int h = 0; h < HiddenSize; ++h) values[h] += col[h];
+    }
+    if (net.threats) {
+        ThreatList list;
+        threat_keys(pieces_of(board), list);
+        for (int i = 0; i < list.count; ++i) {
+            const float* col = threat_column(net, perspective, key, list.keys[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)]);
+            for (int h = 0; h < HiddenSize; ++h) values[h] += col[h];
+        }
+    }
+    if (net.pawn_pairs) {   // O7
+        PawnPairList list;
+        pawn_pair_features(pieces_of(board), perspective, key.flip, list);
+        for (int i = 0; i < list.count; ++i) {
+            const float* col = pawn_pair_column(net, list.features[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)]);
+            for (int h = 0; h < HiddenSize; ++h) values[h] += col[h];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Accumulator of one perspective for the placement `pieces` (own king on
+// `king_square`) through the cache: only the difference to the cached
+// placement under the same key is applied; the entry is updated.
+inline void refresh_cached(const Net& net, RefreshCache& cache, const Pieces& pieces, int king_square,
+                           Color perspective, float* values) {
+    const KingKey key = king_key(net, perspective, king_square);
+    RefreshCache::Entry& entry = cache.entries[(static_cast<std::size_t>(perspective) * static_cast<std::size_t>(net.king_buckets) +
+                                                static_cast<std::size_t>(key.bucket)) * 2U + (key.flip != 0 ? 1U : 0U)];
+    // At most one removal and one addition per square.
+    std::array<const float*, 64> sub;
+    std::array<const float*, 64> add;
+    int subs = 0;
+    int adds = 0;
+    for (int code = WP; code <= BK; ++code) {
+        const Piece piece = static_cast<Piece>(code);
+        const Bitboard now = pieces[static_cast<std::size_t>(code)];
+        Bitboard removed = entry.pieces[static_cast<std::size_t>(code)] & ~now;
+        Bitboard added = now & ~entry.pieces[static_cast<std::size_t>(code)];
+        while (removed != 0) sub[static_cast<std::size_t>(subs++)] = column(net, perspective, key, piece, pop_lsb(removed));
+        while (added != 0) add[static_cast<std::size_t>(adds++)] = column(net, perspective, key, piece, pop_lsb(added));
+        entry.pieces[static_cast<std::size_t>(code)] = now;
+    }
+    constexpr int Chunk = 64;
+    static_assert(HiddenSize % Chunk == 0, "HiddenSize must be a multiple of the refresh chunk");
+    for (int h0 = 0; h0 < HiddenSize; h0 += Chunk) {
+        float chunk[Chunk];
+        for (int k = 0; k < Chunk; ++k) chunk[k] = entry.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(h0 + k)];
+        for (int i = 0; i < subs; ++i) {
+            const float* col = sub[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)] + h0;
+            for (int k = 0; k < Chunk; ++k) chunk[k] -= col[k];
+        }
+        for (int i = 0; i < adds; ++i) {
+            const float* col = add[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)] + h0;
+            for (int k = 0; k < Chunk; ++k) chunk[k] += col[k];
+        }
+        for (int k = 0; k < Chunk; ++k) {
+            entry.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(h0 + k)] = chunk[k];
+            values[h0 + k] = chunk[k];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// O6: refresh_cached plus, for a threat net, the threat columns of `threats` (the
+// threat list of the same placement); O7: plus, for a pawn-pair net, the pawn-pair
+// columns of this perspective (the list is also stored in `pawn_list` if given).
+// The cache entries hold piece-square inputs only.
+inline void refresh_full_cached(const Net& net, RefreshCache& cache, const Pieces& pieces, const ThreatList* threats,
+                                int king_square, Color perspective, float* values, PawnPairList* pawn_list = nullptr) {
+    refresh_cached(net, cache, pieces, king_square, perspective, values);
+    const KingKey key = king_key(net, perspective, king_square);
+    if (threats != nullptr) add_threat_columns(net, perspective, key, *threats, values);
+    if (net.pawn_pairs) {
+        PawnPairList local;
+        PawnPairList& list = pawn_list != nullptr ? *pawn_list : local;
+        pawn_pair_features(pieces, perspective, key.flip, list);
+        add_pawn_pair_columns(net, list, values);
+    }
+}
+
+// Incremental update of one perspective whose king key did not change.
+inline void update(const Net& net, Color perspective, KingKey key, const float* parent, float* child,
+                   const DirtyPieces& dirty) {
+    std::array<const float*, 4> sub{};
+    std::array<const float*, 4> add{};
+    int subs = 0;
+    int adds = 0;
+    for (int i = 0; i < dirty.count; ++i) {
+        const PieceChange& change = dirty.changes[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)];
+        const float* col = column(net, perspective, key, change.piece, change.square);
+        if (change.add) add[static_cast<std::size_t>(adds++)] = col;
+        else sub[static_cast<std::size_t>(subs++)] = col;
+    }
+    if (subs == 1 && adds == 1) apply_columns<1, 1>(parent, child, sub.data(), add.data());
+    else if (subs == 2 && adds == 1) apply_columns<2, 1>(parent, child, sub.data(), add.data());
+    else if (subs == 2 && adds == 2) apply_columns<2, 2>(parent, child, sub.data(), add.data());
+    else {
+        apply_columns<0, 0>(parent, child, sub.data(), add.data());
+        for (int i = 0; i < subs; ++i) apply_columns<1, 0>(child, child, &sub[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)], add.data());
+        for (int i = 0; i < adds; ++i) apply_columns<0, 1>(child, child, sub.data(), &add[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)]);
+    }
+}
+
+// Output bucket of a position: by the number of pieces on the board (C5).
+[[nodiscard]] inline int output_bucket(const Net& net, const Board& board) {
+    return net.output_map[static_cast<std::size_t>(std::min(popcount(board.occupied()), 32))];
+}
+
+// Network output before the conversion to centipawns; nnue::evaluate()'s arithmetic.
+[[nodiscard]] inline float output(const Net& net, const Board& board, const Accumulator& accumulator) {
+    const Color us = board.side_to_move();
+    const auto& ours = accumulator.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(us)];
+    const auto& theirs = accumulator.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(opposite(us))];
+    const std::size_t bucket = static_cast<std::size_t>(output_bucket(net, board));
+    const float* w2 = net.w2.data() + bucket * 2U * HiddenSize;
+    constexpr int Lanes = 16;
+    static_assert(HiddenSize % Lanes == 0, "HiddenSize must be a multiple of the lane count");
+    alignas(64) float lanes[Lanes];
+#if defined(__AVX2__)
+    const __m256 zero = _mm256_setzero_ps();
+    const __m256 one = _mm256_set1_ps(1.0F);
+    __m256 sums[2] = {zero, zero};
+    for (int h = 0; h < HiddenSize; h += Lanes) {
+        for (int k = 0; k < 2; ++k) {
+            const std::size_t base = static_cast<std::size_t>(h + 8 * k);
+            const __m256 a = _mm256_min_ps(_mm256_max_ps(_mm256_load_ps(ours.data() + base), zero), one);
+            const __m256 b = _mm256_min_ps(_mm256_max_ps(_mm256_load_ps(theirs.data() + base), zero), one);
+            const __m256 term = _mm256_add_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(a, a), _mm256_load_ps(w2 + base)),
+                                            _mm256_mul_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(b, b), _mm256_load_ps(w2 + HiddenSize + base)));
+            sums[k] = _mm256_add_ps(sums[k], term);
+        }
+    }
+    for (int k = 0; k < 2; ++k) _mm256_store_ps(lanes + 8 * k, sums[k]);
+#elif FORGE_NNUE_V2_SSE
+    const __m128 zero = _mm_setzero_ps();
+    const __m128 one = _mm_set1_ps(1.0F);
+    __m128 sums[4] = {zero, zero, zero, zero};
+    for (int h = 0; h < HiddenSize; h += Lanes) {
+        for (int k = 0; k < 4; ++k) {
+            const std::size_t base = static_cast<std::size_t>(h + 4 * k);
+            const __m128 a = _mm_min_ps(_mm_max_ps(_mm_load_ps(ours.data() + base), zero), one);
+            const __m128 b = _mm_min_ps(_mm_max_ps(_mm_load_ps(theirs.data() + base), zero), one);
+            const __m128 term = _mm_add_ps(_mm_mul_ps(_mm_mul_ps(a, a), _mm_load_ps(w2 + base)),
+                                           _mm_mul_ps(_mm_mul_ps(b, b), _mm_load_ps(w2 + HiddenSize + base)));
+            sums[k] = _mm_add_ps(sums[k], term);
+        }
+    }
+    for (int k = 0; k < 4; ++k) _mm_store_ps(lanes + 4 * k, sums[k]);
+#else
+    for (float& lane : lanes) lane = 0.0F;
+    for (int h = 0; h < HiddenSize; h += Lanes) {
+        for (int j = 0; j < Lanes; ++j) {
+            const std::size_t index = static_cast<std::size_t>(h + j);
+            const float a = std::min(std::max(ours[index], 0.0F), 1.0F);
+            const float b = std::min(std::max(theirs[index], 0.0F), 1.0F);
+            lanes[j] += a * a * w2[index] + b * b * w2[static_cast<std::size_t>(HiddenSize) + index];
+        }
+    }
+#endif
+    float out = net.b2[bucket];
+    for (int j = 0; j < Lanes; ++j) out += lanes[j];
+    return out;
+}
+
+// Side-to-move evaluation in centipawns.
+[[nodiscard]] inline int evaluate(const Net& net, const Board& board, const Accumulator& accumulator) {
+    const float centipawns = output(net, board, accumulator) * 400.0F / SigmoidK;
+    return static_cast<int>(std::clamp(centipawns, -30000.0F, 30000.0F));
+}
+
+} // namespace forge::nnue::v2
