+#pragma once
+
+#include <atomic>
+#include <cstdint>
+#include <string>
+
+// Runtime search options set over UCI (src/uci.cpp). Every option defaults to
+// OFF; with all options OFF the search is the AL1 s198 search. Options are
+// written only while no search runs (the UCI loop stops the search first).
+namespace forge::options {
+
+// UCI SmpExactNodes (check, default false); acts only with Threads > 1.
+// Resets every worker's published node count before the helper threads start
+// (otherwise the main thread reads the previous search's counts until each
+// helper has started: `go nodes N` can stop at once and the first info lines
+// over-report nodes/nps), and reports the exact total after the helpers stop.
+extern bool smp_exact_nodes;
+
+// UCI SmpBestThread (check, default false); acts only with Threads > 1.
+// Reports the best move, ponder move, PV and score of the worker with the
+// deepest completed iteration (ties: higher score) instead of always thread 0.
+extern bool smp_best_thread;
+
+// UCI LocklessTT (check, default false). The transposition table is probed and
+// written without its striped locks: each entry is two 64-bit atomic words and
+// the first carries the key tag mixed with a hash of all data bits, so a read
+// that combines two writes fails the key check and counts as a miss
+// (src/search/tt_lockless.inc). Read once per search by Searcher::search;
+// changing it clears the table. Same table contents and rules as the locked
+// table, so a search with Threads=1 is unchanged.
+extern bool lockless_tt;
+// Syzygy tablebases (job C1). The master switch is UCI SyzygyPath (string,
+// default empty = no tables, forge/syzygy.hpp); the two options below only act
+// while tables are loaded.
+// UCI SyzygyProbeDepth (spin 1-10, default 1): positions with as many pieces
+// as the largest loaded tables are probed inside the search only at this
+// remaining depth or more (fewer pieces: at every depth).
+extern int syzygy_probe_depth;
+// UCI Syzygy50MoveRule (check, default true): cursed wins and blessed losses
+// (decided only beyond the 50-move rule) count as draws.
+extern bool syzygy_50_move_rule;
+
+// UCI CorrHistPlus (spin 0-7, default 0): extra correction-history keys, a
+// bitmask of forge::corrplus::{Continuation = 1, MinorPiece = 2, NonPawn = 4}
+// (forge/correction_plus.hpp). 0 keeps the AL1 s198 pawn + material
+// correction only; each bit is meant to be gated on its own. One byte so that
+// the default-path test in the hot corrected_eval is a single compare with
+// memory that needs no register.
+extern std::uint8_t corr_hist_plus;
+
+// UCI HardDeadline (spin 0-1000 ms, default 0 = off). When N > 0, every search
+// with a clock or movetime gets a watchdog thread that sleeps until the hard
+// time limit + N ms (ponder searches: counted from ponderhit). If the search has
+// not answered by then, the watchdog stops it and sends `bestmove` with the best
+// move of the last completed iteration (published by the main worker), so a
+// starved or delayed search thread cannot make the engine miss its deadline.
+extern int hard_deadline_ms;
+
+// HardDeadline runtime slot: best move (low 32 bits) and ponder move (high 32
+// bits) of the main worker's last completed iteration, packed so the pair is
+// consistent (forge::pack_move). Process-wide and constant-initialised (no
+// constructor code): one UCI search runs at a time; the UCI thread resets it to
+// 0 before each clocked search, the main worker writes it, the watchdog reads it.
+extern std::atomic<std::uint64_t> published_moves;
+
+// UCI NNUEKingBuckets (spin 0-32, default 0) together with UCI NNUEFile
+// (string): evaluate with a v2 network (king-bucketed input layer, see
+// forge/nnue.hpp nnue::v2) loaded from NNUEFile. N > 0 selects the loaded net
+// when it has exactly N king buckets.
+extern int nnue_king_buckets;
+
+// UCI NNUEOutputBuckets (spin 0-16, default 0) together with NNUEFile (C5):
+// N > 0 selects the loaded v2 net when it has exactly N output buckets (output
+// row chosen by the piece count). The two counts combine: a net with king and
+// output buckets needs both options; 0 means "this net has none" (one output
+// bucket; for king buckets: one unmirrored bucket).
+//
+// Fail-loud rule (no silent fall-back to the compiled-in network). The user
+// requests a v2 net with a non-empty NNUEFile, NNUEKingBuckets > 0 or
+// NNUEOutputBuckets > 0. A request that cannot be honoured (file missing or
+// unreadable, wrong size, bad header or checksum, a king or output bucket
+// count that does not match the loaded net, both counts 0 while a file is
+// loaded, a count > 0 without a loaded file) is an error: the UCI layer prints
+// `info string ERROR NNUE v2: <reason>` at once, and if the request is still
+// unsatisfied at the next `isready`, `go` or `bench` it prints the error again
+// and exits with status 2. Not errors: `UseNNUE false` (every network off on
+// purpose; the request is ignored while it lasts) and NNUEFile `<empty>` (clears
+// the file part of the request). With no NNUE option set nothing new is printed
+// and the engine is the AL1 s198 engine.
+extern int nnue_output_buckets;
+
+// UCI NNUEThreats (spin 0-1, default 0; O6; "true" / "false" are accepted as
+// well): 1 = evaluate with a v2 net that has threat inputs (header feature_set
+// bit 0; forge/nnue_v2.hpp ThreatFeatures), loaded with NNUEFile; with
+// NNUEKingBuckets 0 that net must have one king bucket, with NNUEKingBuckets
+// N > 0 exactly N. The header and the option must agree both ways: NNUEThreats 1
+// with a net without threat inputs, and NNUEThreats 0 with a threat net, are
+// fail-loud errors (rule above). NNUEThreats 1 is itself a v2 request (an error
+// without a usable net); UseNNUE false still wins.
+extern int nnue_threats;
+
+// UCI NNUEPawnPairs (spin 0-1, default 0; O7; "true" / "false" accepted as
+// well): 1 = evaluate with a v2 net that has pawn-pair inputs (header
+// feature_set bit 1; forge/nnue_v2.hpp PawnPairFeatures). Same rules as
+// NNUEThreats: with NNUEKingBuckets 0 a one-bucket net, the header bit and the
+// option must agree both ways (fail-loud otherwise), it is a v2 request of its
+// own, UseNNUE false wins. NNUEThreats and NNUEPawnPairs are independent: a net
+// with both blocks needs both options.
+extern int nnue_pawn_pairs;
+
+// UCI PureNnue (spin 0-1, default 0; O4; "true" / "false" are accepted as
+// well): 1 = the evaluation is the network alone, without the hand-crafted
+// evaluation (HCE). It needs a stand-alone v2 net (header flag residual = 0,
+// trainer --pure) loaded with NNUEFile; with NNUEKingBuckets 0 that net must
+// have one king bucket (the compiled-in input layer), with NNUEKingBuckets
+// N > 0 exactly N. The header flag and the option must agree both ways:
+// PureNnue 1 with a residual net, and PureNnue 0 with a stand-alone net, are
+// fail-loud errors (rule above), so the HCE is skipped exactly when PureNnue
+// is 1. PureNnue 1 is itself a v2 request (an error without a usable net);
+// UseNNUE false still wins.
+extern bool pure_nnue;
+
+// Derived switch read by Worker::static_evaluate: true while a v2 network is
+// active. Written only by apply_nnue_v2().
+extern bool nnue_v2;
+
+// Re-selects the evaluation network after NNUEFile, NNUEKingBuckets,
+// NNUEOutputBuckets or UseNNUE changed and returns whether a v2 network is now
+// active. The caller clears the hash afterwards (stored static evals may belong
+// to another network).
+// `message` (optional) receives a one-line description of the state.
+bool apply_nnue_v2(std::string* message);
+
+// The fail-loud rule above: why the current v2 request cannot be honoured, or
+// "" when it is honoured, when nothing is requested or when UseNNUE is false.
+// `file` is the NNUEFile value ("" = none) and `load_error` why it did not
+// load ("" = it loaded). Call after apply_nnue_v2().
+std::string nnue_v2_problem(const std::string& file, const std::string& load_error);
+
+// UCI CorrMargins (spin 0-1, default 0): correction-size pruning margins
+// (forge/corr_margins.hpp, weights tune::Corr*Pct). Read once per iteration at
+// the root, which then searches with the CorrMargins instance of the main
+// search (Worker::search_corr_margins, src/search/alphabeta.inc); with 0 no
+// node tests it and the search is the AL1 s198 search.
+extern std::uint8_t corr_margins;
+
+// UCI CorrExt (spin 0-1, default 0): correction-history extensions
+// (forge/corr_ext.hpp, weights tune::CorrExt*). Read once per iteration at the
+// root, which then searches with the CorrExt instance of the main search
+// (Worker::search_corr_ext, src/search/alphabeta.inc); with 0 no node tests it
+// and the search is the AL1 s198 search.
+extern std::uint8_t corr_ext;
+
+// UCI CuckooRep (check, default false): upcoming-repetition draw bound. At
+// every non-root node of the main and quiescence search whose alpha is below
+// the draw score, Board::upcoming_repetition() asks whether the side to move
+// can reach a search-level repetition draw with one reversible move; if so,
+// the draw score is a lower bound for the node (src/search/cuckoo.inc). Read
+// once per iteration at the root, which then searches with the CuckooRep
+// instances of the search functions; with the option off no node tests it.
+extern bool cuckoo_rep;
+
+// UCI MaterialKeyTable (check, default false), job S11 (node-exact speed).
+// The material-correction key (src/search.cpp material_key) is computed from
+// the contribution table of forge/material_key_table.hpp (ten table loads and
+// additions) instead of the ten dependent Horner multiplications. Both give the
+// same 64-bit key for every board, so the search (nodes, scores, moves) is
+// unchanged; only the speed can differ.
+extern bool material_key_table;
+
+// UCI IntendedThreefold (check, default false), S-IT. FIDE 9.2.1 lets the side to move
+// claim a draw before a legal move that would create the third occurrence; with
+// the option on, non-root search and quiescence treat that right as a floor at
+// the draw score, without TT score cutoffs or stores at those nodes.
+extern bool intended_threefold;
+
+} // namespace forge::options

+#pragma once
+
+#include "forge/board.hpp"
+
+#include <chrono>
+#include <cstdint>
+#include <vector>
+
+namespace forge {
+
+struct SearchLimits {
+#ifdef FORGE_S13_TT_CHILD_PREFETCH
+    bool s13_tt_child_prefetch = false;
+#endif
+    int depth = 64;
+    std::uint64_t nodes = 0;
+    bool nodes_specified = false;
+    int movetime_ms = -1;
+    int time_ms = -1;
+    int increment_ms = 0;
+    // 0 means the increment/sudden-death time control (no movestogo given).
+    int moves_to_go = 0;
+    int mate_moves = 0;
+    int move_overhead_ms = 10;
+    bool infinite = false;
+    bool ponder = false;
+    bool search_moves_specified = false;
+    std::vector<Move> search_moves;
+    // Optional steady-clock receipt origin. start_time takes precedence;
+    // request_start remains a compatibility spelling for existing callers.
+    // With both unset, finite timed searches start at Searcher entry; other
+    // searches retain a worker-local origin. Ponder budgets start at ponderhit.
+    std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point request_start{};
+    std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point start_time{};
+};
+
+struct SearchInfo {
+    Move best_move{};
+    Move ponder_move{};
+    int score = 0;
+    int depth = 0;
+    int seldepth = 0;
+    std::uint64_t nodes = 0;
+    std::uint64_t elapsed_ms = 0;
+    std::vector<Move> pv;
+    // A searchmoves intersection with no legal moves is not checkmate or stalemate.
+    bool restricted_root_empty = false;
+};
+
+constexpr int MaxPly = 128;
+constexpr int Infinite = 32001;
+constexpr int MateValue = 32000;
+constexpr int MateInMaxPly = MateValue - MaxPly;
+constexpr int ValueNone = 32002;
+
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_mate_score(int score) {
+    return score >= MateInMaxPly || score <= -MateInMaxPly;
+}
+
+enum Bound : std::uint8_t { BoundNone = 0, BoundUpper = 1, BoundLower = 2, BoundExact = 3 };
+
+} // namespace forge
diff --git a/include/forge/syzygy.hpp b/include/forge/syzygy.hpp
@@ -0,0 +1,133 @@
+#pragma once
+
+// Syzygy endgame tablebases (WDL .rtbw and DTZ .rtbz files), own reader.
+//
+// Written from the public description of the Syzygy file format and probing
+// rules (R. de Man's format notes, CPW "Syzygy Bases"; see
+// opus_round1/C1/IDEA_SOURCE.md); no third-party probing code is used.
+// Implementation: src/search/syzygy.inc (table files, indexing, decompression,
+// WDL/DTZ probes, root ranking) and src/search/tablebase.inc (search glue).
+//
+// Everything is inactive until load() finds tables (UCI SyzygyPath). Tables
+// are memory-mapped read-only; probes may run concurrently from any number of
+// search threads, but never concurrently with load().
+
+#include "forge/board.hpp"
+#include "forge/search_types.hpp"
+
+#include <atomic>
+#include <cstdint>
+#include <string>
+#include <vector>
+
+namespace forge::syzygy {
+
+// Game-theoretic value from the side to move's point of view. A cursed win
+// (blessed loss) is a win (loss) that the 50-move rule turns into a draw.
+enum Wdl : int { WdlLoss = -2, WdlBlessedLoss = -1, WdlDraw = 0, WdlCursedWin = 1, WdlWin = 2 };
+
+struct LoadSummary {
+    int wdl_tables = 0;   // .rtbw files accepted
+    int dtz_tables = 0;   // .rtbz files accepted (only for materials that also have a WDL table)
+    int rejected = 0;     // files with a table name that failed validation (empty, truncated, corrupt)
+    int max_pieces = 0;   // largest piece count (kings included) with a WDL table; 0 = none
+};
+
+// Replaces the loaded tables by those found in `paths`: directories separated
+// by ';' on Windows and ':' elsewhere. An empty string or "<empty>" unloads
+// everything. Missing folders, empty folders and invalid files are skipped.
+// Must not run while any search or probe runs.
+LoadSummary load(const std::string& paths);
+
+// Largest piece count with a loaded WDL table (0 when nothing is loaded).
+[[nodiscard]] int max_pieces();
+
+// WDL probe. Returns false if the position cannot be probed: castling rights,
+// more pieces than max_pieces(), or a needed table is missing or invalid. The
+// value assumes the 50-move counter is zero (exact right after a capture or a
+// pawn move). The board is restored before returning.
+[[nodiscard]] bool probe_wdl(Board& board, int& wdl);
+
+// DTZ probe, in plies, Syzygy convention: dtz > 0 win (dtz > 100: cursed win),
+// dtz < 0 loss (dtz < -100: blessed loss), 0 draw, -1 the side to move is
+// mated. Same failure rules as probe_wdl. A table that stores a value in moves
+// instead of plies rounds it: then `rounded` is set and the true distance is
+// |dtz| or |dtz| + 1 plies (DTZ50'' rounding).
+[[nodiscard]] bool probe_dtz(Board& board, int& dtz);
+[[nodiscard]] bool probe_dtz(Board& board, int& dtz, bool& rounded);
+
+// Outcome of the position with its current 50-move counter: like the WDL
+// value, but a win/loss whose zeroing move cannot come before the 50-move rule
+// is reported as cursed/blessed. Needs the DTZ table. Returns false also when
+// DTZ rounding leaves the outcome undecided (rounded distance + counter = 100).
+[[nodiscard]] bool probe_wdl_with_counter(Board& board, int& wdl);
+
+struct RootRank {
+    Move move{};
+    int rank = 0;          // larger is better; equal ranks are equivalent for the tablebase
+    int value = 0;         // DTZ of the move (counted from the root position) or its WDL
+    bool rounded = false;  // the DTZ value is rounded (see probe_dtz)
+};
+
+// Ranks every legal root move with DTZ (falls back to WDL when a DTZ probe
+// fails). rule50 = honour the 50-move rule. Returns false if the root cannot be
+// probed at all. used_dtz reports which tables ranked the moves.
+[[nodiscard]] bool rank_root_moves(Board& board, bool rule50, std::vector<RootRank>& ranks, bool& used_dtz);
+
+// Search-time state, written by the search driver before the worker threads
+// start (src/search/tablebase.inc) and read by the workers:
+//  - search_cardinality > 0 enables WDL probes inside the search for
+//    positions with at most that many pieces;
+//  - root_display != 0 means the root was ranked by the tablebase and the
+//    reported (non-mate) score is replaced by root_display_score.
+extern std::atomic<int> search_cardinality;
+extern std::atomic<int> root_display;
+extern std::atomic<int> root_display_score;
+extern std::atomic<std::uint64_t> probe_hits;
+
+// Tablebase scores, below the mate scores: a TB win found at ply p scores
+// TbWin - p; a cursed win (blessed loss) scores +/-TbCursed while the 50-move
+// rule is honoured.
+constexpr int TbWin = MateInMaxPly - 1;
+constexpr int TbCursed = 2;
+// Lowest TB win (a win found at the last ply); |score| >= TbWinInMaxPly is the
+// tablebase score range, just below the mate scores.
+constexpr int TbWinInMaxPly = TbWin - MaxPly;
+
+// Transposition-table encoding of tablebase scores (score_to_tt / score_from_tt
+// in src/search/scores.inc): while tables are loaded (tb_scores, set by load()
+// while no search runs) a score with |score| >= TbWinInMaxPly is stored as its
+// distance from the storing node, like a mate score, and read back relative to
+// the reading node; without tables that range holds ordinary search scores and
+// is stored unchanged, as in AL1 s198. load() is followed by a hash clear
+// (src/uci.cpp), so the table never holds both encodings.
+extern bool tb_scores;
+// Test hook: score_from_tt(score_to_tt(score, store_ply), read_ply) exactly as
+// the search computes it.
+[[nodiscard]] int tt_score_round_trip(int score, int store_ply, int read_ply);
+
+// Search driver hooks (src/search/tablebase.inc), only called while tables are
+// loaded. prepare_root runs before the workers start: it sets
+// search_cardinality, and if the root is a tablebase position it restricts
+// limits.search_moves to the best-ranked moves (intersected with a given
+// searchmoves list) and sets the reported score. display_score maps a search
+// score to the reported one.
+void prepare_root(const Board& root, SearchLimits& limits);
+[[nodiscard]] int display_score(int search_score);
+
+// Test hook: read table files into exact-size heap buffers instead of mapping
+// them (lets AddressSanitizer see every out-of-bounds read). Default false.
+void set_heap_loading(bool enabled);
+
+// Loaded files, for diagnostics and tests: `tail` is the number of bytes after
+// the last compressed block (the format ends with a 16-byte checksum).
+struct TableInfo {
+    std::string name;  // e.g. "KRPvKR.rtbz"
+    std::uint64_t file_size = 0;
+    std::uint64_t tail = 0;
+};
+[[nodiscard]] std::vector<TableInfo> loaded_tables();
+// True if both the WDL and the DTZ table of `name` (e.g. "KRPvKR") are loaded.
+[[nodiscard]] bool has_table(const std::string& name);
+

+// Move <-> 32-bit word, for publishing moves through one atomic.
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr std::uint32_t pack_move(Move move) {
+    return static_cast<std::uint32_t>(move.from) | static_cast<std::uint32_t>(move.to) << 8U |
+           static_cast<std::uint32_t>(move.promotion) << 16U | static_cast<std::uint32_t>(move.flags) << 24U;
+}
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr Move unpack_move(std::uint32_t bits) {
+    return Move{static_cast<std::uint8_t>(bits), static_cast<std::uint8_t>(bits >> 8U),
+                static_cast<std::uint8_t>(bits >> 16U), static_cast<std::uint8_t>(bits >> 24U)};
+}

+#pragma once
+
+#include "forge/search_types.hpp"
+
+#include <array>
+#include <atomic>
+#include <cstddef>
+#include <cstdint>
+#include <memory>
+#include <mutex>

+namespace forge {
+
+struct TTEntry {
+    std::uint32_t key32 = 0;
+    Move move{};
+    std::int16_t score = 0;
+    std::int16_t eval = 0;
+    std::uint8_t depth = 0;
+    // Bits 0..1 bound, bit 2 was-PV, bits 3..7 generation.
+    std::uint8_t gen_bound = 0;
+    std::uint8_t rule50_clock = 0; // Capped at100; belongs to admitted score payload.
+    std::uint8_t padding = 0;
+
+    [[nodiscard]] Bound bound() const { return static_cast<Bound>(gen_bound & 3U); }
+    [[nodiscard]] bool pv() const { return (gen_bound & 4U) != 0; }
+    [[nodiscard]] std::uint8_t generation() const { return static_cast<std::uint8_t>(gen_bound >> 3U); }
+    [[nodiscard]] bool occupied() const { return bound() != BoundNone; }
+    // Existing below90 policy, plus exact near50 context; not a repetition key.
+    [[nodiscard]] bool score_usable(std::uint16_t current_clock) const {
+        const int capped = current_clock < 100 ? current_clock : 100;
+        return (rule50_clock < 90 && capped < 90) || rule50_clock == capped;
+    }
+};
+
+struct alignas(64) TTCluster {
+    std::array<TTEntry, 4> entries{};
+};
+static_assert(sizeof(TTEntry) == 16);
+static_assert(sizeof(TTCluster) == 64);
+
+class TranspositionTable;
+
+// Immutable entry copy plus an opaque probe-time replacement destination.
+struct TTProbe {
+    TTEntry entry{};
+    bool hit = false;
+private:
+    friend class TranspositionTable;
+    const TranspositionTable* owner_ = nullptr;
+    std::size_t cluster_ = 0;
+    std::uint8_t slot_ = 0;
+    std::uint64_t epoch_ = 0;
+    std::uint64_t key_ = 0;
+};
+
+// Search reads and writes are coherent under striped locks (with LocklessTT:
+// without locks, torn entries detected by their key check). clear/resize/
+// new_search/destruction require all accessors quiescent (including diagnostic
+// readers and prefetch callers) and invalidate old probes. Handles must not
+// outlive the table; lifecycle operations are not safe concurrently with search.
+class TranspositionTable {
+public:
+    explicit TranspositionTable(std::size_t mb = 32) { resize(mb); }
+    void resize(std::size_t mb);
+    void clear();
+    void new_search() { generation_ = static_cast<std::uint8_t>((generation_ + 1U) & 31U); ++epoch_; }
+    // Pinned to 64-byte boundaries, as the search functions (forge/search.hpp).
+    [[nodiscard, gnu::aligned(64)]] TTProbe probe(std::uint64_t key) const;
+    [[gnu::aligned(64)]] void store(const TTProbe& probe, std::uint64_t key, int score, int eval, Move move, int depth, Bound bound, bool pv, std::uint16_t halfmove_clock);
+    [[nodiscard]] int hashfull() const;
+    [[nodiscard]] std::uint8_t generation() const { return generation_; }
+    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t cluster_count() const { return cluster_count_; }
+    // UCI LocklessTT (src/search/tt_lockless.inc): probe/store/hashfull without
+    // the stripe locks. Lifecycle operation: all accessors quiescent. The two
+    // modes store entries differently, so a change clears the table.
+    void set_lockless(bool lockless);
+    [[nodiscard]] bool lockless() const { return lockless_; }
+    // The lockless form of one entry: {move << 32 | key tag ^ check, bytes 8-15}.
+    [[nodiscard]] static std::array<std::uint64_t, 2> encode_lockless(const TTEntry& entry);
+    [[nodiscard]] static TTEntry decode_lockless(std::uint64_t first, std::uint64_t second);
+    void prefetch(std::uint64_t key) const {
+#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
+        __builtin_prefetch(&cluster_for(key));
+#else
+        (void)key;
+#endif
+    }
+
+private:
+    friend class Searcher;
+    static constexpr std::size_t StripeCount = 1024;
+    struct alignas(64) Stripe { std::mutex mutex; };
+    // Opt-in controller ownership: request exclusive access, drain each stripe,
+    // then publish TLS. Lifecycle mutations still require all users quiescent.
+    class ExclusiveLease {
+    public:
+        explicit ExclusiveLease(TranspositionTable& table);
+        ~ExclusiveLease();
+        ExclusiveLease(const ExclusiveLease&) = delete;
+        ExclusiveLease& operator=(const ExclusiveLease&) = delete;
+        ExclusiveLease(ExclusiveLease&&) = delete;
+        ExclusiveLease& operator=(ExclusiveLease&&) = delete;
+    private:
+        friend class TranspositionTable;
+        TranspositionTable& table_;
+        const ExclusiveLease* previous_;
+        std::unique_lock<std::mutex> gate_;
+    };
+    static thread_local const ExclusiveLease* active_lease_;
+    [[nodiscard]] bool has_exclusive_access() const;
+    [[nodiscard]] std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock_stripe(std::size_t index) const;
+    mutable std::array<Stripe, StripeCount> stripes_{};
+    mutable std::mutex exclusive_gate_;
+    std::atomic<bool> exclusive_requested_{false};
+    std::uint64_t epoch_ = 0;
+    std::unique_ptr<TTCluster[]> clusters_;
+    std::size_t cluster_count_ = 0;
+    std::uint8_t generation_ = 0;
+    bool lockless_ = false;
+
+    [[nodiscard]] TTProbe probe_lockless(std::uint64_t key) const;
+    void store_lockless(const TTProbe& probe, std::uint64_t key, int score, int eval, Move move, int depth, Bound bound,
+                        bool pv, std::uint16_t halfmove_clock);
+    [[nodiscard]] int hashfull_lockless() const;
+
+    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr std::uint32_t verification_key(std::uint64_t key) {
+#if defined(__SIZEOF_INT128__)
+        // Multiply-high indexing consumes high key bits. Keep the independent
+        // low bits as the tag, rather than reusing the bucket's index bits.
+        return static_cast<std::uint32_t>(key);
+#else
+        // Modulo indexing consumes low bits for power-of-two table sizes.
+        return static_cast<std::uint32_t>(key >> 32U);
+#endif
+    }
+
+    [[nodiscard]] TTCluster& cluster_for(std::uint64_t key) const {
+#if defined(__SIZEOF_INT128__)
+        __extension__ using u128 = unsigned __int128;
+        return clusters_[static_cast<std::size_t>((static_cast<u128>(key) * cluster_count_) >> 64U)];
+#else
+        return clusters_[static_cast<std::size_t>(key % cluster_count_)];
+#endif
+    }
+};
+
+} // namespace forge

diff --git a/src/board.cpp b/src/board.cpp
@@ -5,6 +5,7 @@
+#include <utility>
@@ -915,6 +916,81 @@ bool Board::is_repetition(std::size_t root_index) const {
+bool Board::can_claim_threefold_by_next_move() const {
+    // The position after a reversible move (opponent to move) would be history
+    // index `size`. Its window starts after the last irreversible move (halfmove
+    // clock + 1 plies back) and strictly after the most recent null move, whose
+    // position is virtual. Two earlier occurrences need at least 8 plies.
+    const std::ptrdiff_t size = static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(key_history_.size());
+    const std::ptrdiff_t span = std::min<std::ptrdiff_t>(static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(halfmove_) + 1,
+                                                         static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(plies_from_null_));
+    if (span < 8) return false;
+    const std::ptrdiff_t lo = std::max<std::ptrdiff_t>(size - span, 0);
+    const auto key_at = [this](std::ptrdiff_t i) { return key_history_[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)]; };
+
+    // The resulting key must occur at least twice on that parity: collect every such
+    // key (exact matches; the bucket masks only skip comparisons). The window holds
+    // at most 512 keys of one parity (plies_from_null_ <= 1024), so at most 256 keys
+    // occur twice.
+    std::array<std::uint64_t, 256> twice;
+    std::size_t twice_count = 0;
+    std::uint64_t buckets = 0;
+    std::uint64_t twice_buckets = 0;
+    for (std::ptrdiff_t i = size - 2; i >= lo; i -= 2) {
+        const std::uint64_t key = key_at(i);
+        const std::uint64_t bucket = 1ULL << (key & 63U);
+        if ((buckets & bucket) != 0U) {
+            for (std::ptrdiff_t j = i + 2; j <= size - 2; j += 2) {
+                if (key_at(j) != key) continue;
+                const auto known = twice.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(twice_count);
+                if (std::find(twice.begin(), known, key) == known && twice_count < twice.size()) {
+                    twice[twice_count++] = key;
+                    twice_buckets |= bucket;
+                }
+                break;
+            }
+        }
+        buckets |= bucket;
+    }
+    if (twice_count == 0) return false;
+
+    // Only quiet non-pawn moves can recreate an earlier position: captures, pawn
+    // moves and promotions are irreversible, and castling drops a right that every
+    // position in the window still had. These are the Quiet moves of
+    // generate_pseudo_legal() for knights, bishops, rooks, queens and the king. The
+    // resulting key mirrors make_move(); only a key in the set needs the legality test.
+    const std::uint64_t ep_key = ep_square_ >= 0 && has_legal_en_passant_capture()
+                                     ? Z.ep[static_cast<std::size_t>(file_of(ep_square_))] : 0;
+    const std::uint64_t base = key_ ^ ep_key ^ Z.side ^ Z.castling[castling_];
+    const auto twice_end = twice.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(twice_count);
+    const Bitboard occ = occupied();
+    const auto reaches_twice = [&](PieceType type, auto attacks_from) {
+        const Piece piece = make_piece(side_, type);
+        Bitboard from_set = pieces(side_, type);
+        while (from_set != 0) {
+            const int from = pop_lsb(from_set);
+            const std::uint64_t from_key = base ^ piece_key(from, piece);
+            const auto from_rights = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(castling_ & CastlingMasks[static_cast<std::size_t>(from)]);
+            Bitboard targets = attacks_from(from) & ~occ;
+            while (targets != 0) {
+                const int to = pop_lsb(targets);
+                const auto rights = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(from_rights & CastlingMasks[static_cast<std::size_t>(to)]);
+                const std::uint64_t next = from_key ^ Z.castling[rights] ^ piece_key(to, piece);
+                if ((twice_buckets & (1ULL << (next & 63U))) != 0U && std::find(twice.begin(), twice_end, next) != twice_end &&
+                    is_legal(Move{static_cast<std::uint8_t>(from), static_cast<std::uint8_t>(to), 0, Quiet})) {
+                    return true;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        return false;
+    };
+    return reaches_twice(Knight, [](int square) { return attacks::knight(square); }) ||
+           reaches_twice(Bishop, [occ](int square) { return attacks::bishop(square, occ); }) ||
+           reaches_twice(Rook, [occ](int square) { return attacks::rook(square, occ); }) ||
+           reaches_twice(Queen, [occ](int square) { return attacks::queen(square, occ); }) ||
+           reaches_twice(King, [](int square) { return attacks::king(square); });
+}
+
@@ -1055,4 +1131,179 @@ std::uint64_t Board::compute_pawn_key() const {
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Upcoming-repetition detection (UCI CuckooRep, default off).
+//
+// Idea (M. van Kervinck, 2013; see opus_round1/C6/IDEA_SOURCE.md): a position
+// P that occurred i plies ago (i odd, inside the reversible window) can be
+// reached again by one move of the side to move exactly when
+//   (1) the opponent's men stand where they stood in P: the XOR of the
+//       opponent's key deltas since P (side key removed) is zero, and
+//   (2) the key difference key ^ key(P) is the key of a single reversible move
+//       of the side to move, which the cuckoo table below recognises in at
+//       most two probes, and the move is possible now (mover on one end, the
+//       other end empty, no man in between).
+// Such a move is legal: it recreates P, in which the side to move was not in
+// check. This file's code is written from that description only.
+// The table and every helper are cold or constant: with CuckooRep off nothing
+// here is executed and the hot code and data layout of the board are unchanged
+// (the table is placed after all other read-only data on Windows).
+namespace {
+
+struct CuckooMove {
+    std::uint8_t piece = 0;  // Piece (never a pawn); 0 = empty slot
+    std::uint8_t a = 0;      // lower square
+    std::uint8_t b = 0;      // higher square
+    std::uint8_t unused = 0;
+};
+
+struct CuckooTable {
+    std::array<std::uint64_t, cuckoo::Slots> keys{};  // 0 = empty slot
+    std::array<CuckooMove, cuckoo::Slots> moves{};
+    std::size_t entries = 0;
+    bool complete = true;  // every insertion found a slot
+};
+
+// Two independent 13-bit slot indices from different halves of the key.
+constexpr std::size_t cuckoo_slot_low(std::uint64_t key) {
+    return static_cast<std::size_t>(key) & (cuckoo::Slots - 1);
+}
+constexpr std::size_t cuckoo_slot_high(std::uint64_t key) {
+    return static_cast<std::size_t>(key >> 32U) & (cuckoo::Slots - 1);
+}
+
+// Whether a piece of this type connects squares a and b on an empty board.
+constexpr bool reversible_step(int type, int a, int b) {
+    const int df = file_of(a) - file_of(b);
+    const int dr = rank_of(a) - rank_of(b);
+    const int files = df < 0 ? -df : df;
+    const int ranks = dr < 0 ? -dr : dr;
+    const bool diagonal = files == ranks && files != 0;
+    const bool straight = (files == 0) != (ranks == 0);
+    switch (type) {
+    case Knight: return (files == 1 && ranks == 2) || (files == 2 && ranks == 1);
+    case Bishop: return diagonal;
+    case Rook: return straight;
+    case Queen: return diagonal || straight;
+    case King: return files <= 1 && ranks <= 1 && (files | ranks) != 0;
+    default: return false;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr std::uint64_t reversible_move_key(int piece, int a, int b) {
+    return piece_key(a, static_cast<Piece>(piece)) ^ piece_key(b, static_cast<Piece>(piece)) ^ Z.side;
+}
+
+// Cuckoo hashing with two candidate slots per key: an insertion takes its
+// first slot and evicts the occupant, which moves to its other slot, and so on.
+constexpr CuckooTable build_cuckoo_table() {
+    CuckooTable table{};
+    for (int piece = WN; piece <= BK; ++piece) {
+        const int type = piece_type(static_cast<Piece>(piece));
+        if (type == Pawn) continue;
+        for (int a = 0; a < 64; ++a) {
+            for (int b = a + 1; b < 64; ++b) {
+                if (!reversible_step(type, a, b)) continue;
+                std::uint64_t key = reversible_move_key(piece, a, b);
+                CuckooMove move{static_cast<std::uint8_t>(piece), static_cast<std::uint8_t>(a),
+                                static_cast<std::uint8_t>(b), 0};
+                if (key == 0) table.complete = false;
+                std::size_t slot = cuckoo_slot_low(key);
+                for (int evictions = 0; key != 0; ++evictions) {
+                    if (evictions > 4096) {
+                        table.complete = false;
+                        break;
+                    }
+                    std::swap(table.keys[slot], key);
+                    std::swap(table.moves[slot], move);
+                    slot = slot == cuckoo_slot_low(key) ? cuckoo_slot_high(key) : cuckoo_slot_low(key);
+                }
+                ++table.entries;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    return table;
+}
+
+#if defined(_WIN32) && defined(__GNUC__)
+// Sorted after every other .rdata input section by the PE linker, so adding the
+// table does not move any existing read-only data of the engine.
+#define FORGE_CUCKOO_SECTION [[gnu::section(".rdata$zz_forge_cuckoo")]]
+#else
+#define FORGE_CUCKOO_SECTION
+#endif
+FORGE_CUCKOO_SECTION constexpr CuckooTable CuckooMoves = build_cuckoo_table();
+#undef FORGE_CUCKOO_SECTION
+
+static_assert(CuckooMoves.complete, "cuckoo table insertion failed");
+static_assert(CuckooMoves.entries == 3668, "2 colours x (N 168 + B 280 + R 448 + Q 728 + K 210) moves");
+
+[[gnu::always_inline]] inline const CuckooMove* cuckoo_find(std::uint64_t key) {
+    if (key == 0) return nullptr;
+    std::size_t slot = cuckoo_slot_low(key);
+    if (CuckooMoves.keys[slot] == key) return &CuckooMoves.moves[slot];
+    slot = cuckoo_slot_high(key);
+    if (CuckooMoves.keys[slot] == key) return &CuckooMoves.moves[slot];
+    return nullptr;
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
+namespace cuckoo {
+
+std::size_t entry_count() { return CuckooMoves.entries; }
+
+std::uint64_t move_key(Piece piece, int a, int b) { return reversible_move_key(piece, a, b); }
+
+std::optional<Entry> lookup(std::uint64_t key) {
+    const CuckooMove* const move = cuckoo_find(key);
+    if (move == nullptr) return std::nullopt;
+    return Entry{static_cast<Piece>(move->piece), move->a, move->b};
+}
+
+std::uint64_t slot_key(std::size_t slot) { return slot < Slots ? CuckooMoves.keys[slot] : 0; }
+
+} // namespace cuckoo
+
+bool Board::upcoming_repetition(std::size_t root_index) const {
+    // After one more reversible move, is_repetition() of the new position scans
+    // its earlier positions at even distances 4 .. span, where span is the
+    // smaller of its halfmove clock, its plies since a null move (both one more
+    // than here, with the same saturation as make_move) and its number of
+    // earlier positions. Seen from here those are the odd distances 3 .. span-1.
+    const std::size_t size = key_history_.size();
+    const std::size_t clock = std::min<std::size_t>(static_cast<std::size_t>(halfmove_) + 1, 65535);
+    const std::size_t since_null = std::min<std::size_t>(static_cast<std::size_t>(plies_from_null_) + 1, 1024);
+    const std::size_t span = std::min(std::min(clock, since_null), size);
+    if (span < 4) return false;
+    const std::uint64_t* const keys = key_history_.data();
+    const std::size_t now = size - 1;
+    // Running XOR of the opponent's move deltas (one ply each, side key removed)
+    // between the candidate earlier position and now.
+    std::uint64_t opponent = keys[now] ^ keys[now - 1] ^ Z.side;
+    for (std::size_t distance = 3; distance < span; distance += 2) {
+        opponent ^= keys[now - distance + 1] ^ keys[now - distance] ^ Z.side;
+        if (opponent != 0) continue;
+        const std::uint64_t earlier = keys[now - distance];
+        const CuckooMove* const move = cuckoo_find(key_ ^ earlier);
+        if (move == nullptr) continue;
+        const Piece piece = static_cast<Piece>(move->piece);
+        if (color_of(piece) != side_) continue;
+        int from = move->a;
+        int to = move->b;
+        if (squares_[static_cast<std::size_t>(from)] != piece) std::swap(from, to);
+        if (squares_[static_cast<std::size_t>(from)] != piece || squares_[static_cast<std::size_t>(to)] != Empty ||
+            (attacks::between(from, to) & occupied()) != 0) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        // As in is_repetition(): one occurrence at or after the root is a draw;
+        // before the root a second occurrence inside the window is needed.
+        if (now - distance >= root_index) return true;
+        for (std::size_t further = distance + 2; further < span; further += 2) {
+            if (keys[now - further] == earlier) return true;
+        }
+    }
+    return false;
+}
+

+PackedBoard pack_board(const Board& board) {
+    PackedBoard packed{};
+    for (int square = 0; square < 64; ++square) {
+        const auto code = static_cast<unsigned>(board.piece_at(square));
+        auto& byte = packed[static_cast<std::size_t>(square >> 1)];
+        byte = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(byte | ((square & 1) != 0 ? code << 4U : code));
+    }
+    return packed;
+}
+
+const char* termination_name(Termination termination) {
+    switch (termination) {
+        case Termination::Checkmate: return "checkmate";
+        case Termination::Stalemate: return "stalemate";
+        case Termination::Insufficient: return "insufficient";
+        case Termination::Repetition: return "repetition";
+        case Termination::FiftyMoves: return "fifty";
+        case Termination::MaxPly: return "max_ply";
+        case Termination::AdjudicatedWin: return "adj_win";
+        case Termination::AdjudicatedDraw: return "adj_draw";
+    }
+    return "unknown";
+}
+
+std::uint64_t Rng::next() {
+    std::uint64_t z = (state_ += 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15ULL);
+    z = (z ^ (z >> 30U)) * 0xBF58476D1CE4E5B9ULL;
+    z = (z ^ (z >> 27U)) * 0x94D049BB133111EBULL;
+    return z ^ (z >> 31U);
+}
+
+std::uint64_t game_seed(std::uint64_t run_seed, std::uint64_t index) {
+    Rng mix(run_seed ^ (index * 0xD1B54A32D192ED03ULL));
+    return mix.next();
+}
+
+int game_ply(const Board& board) {
+    return 2 * (board.fullmove_number() - 1) + (board.side_to_move() == Black ? 1 : 0);
+}
+
+std::optional<std::pair<int, Termination>> terminal(const Board& board, int max_ply) {
+    // Same order and outcome as forge_selfplay.py terminal_result(): checkmate first, then the draws.
+    const bool any_legal = board.has_legal_move();
+    if (!any_legal && board.in_check()) return std::pair{board.side_to_move() == White ? 0 : 2, Termination::Checkmate};
+    if (!any_legal) return std::pair{1, Termination::Stalemate};
+    if (board.is_insufficient_material()) return std::pair{1, Termination::Insufficient};
+    if (board.is_repetition()) return std::pair{1, Termination::Repetition};
+    if (board.halfmove_clock() >= 100) return std::pair{1, Termination::FiftyMoves};
+    if (game_ply(board) >= max_ply) return std::pair{1, Termination::MaxPly};
+    return std::nullopt;
+}
+
+std::optional<Game> play_game(Searcher& searcher, const Config& config, const Board& start, Rng& rng) {
+    Board board = start;
+    Game game;
+    for (int ply = 0; ply < config.random_plies; ++ply) {
+        if (terminal(board, config.max_ply)) return std::nullopt;
+        const MoveList moves = board.legal_moves();
+        const Move move = moves[static_cast<std::size_t>(rng.below(moves.size()))];
+        Undo undo;
+        if (!board.make_move(move, undo)) throw std::runtime_error("random opening move rejected");
+        game.opening.push_back(move);
+    }
+    if (terminal(board, config.max_ply)) return std::nullopt;
+
+    SearchLimits limits;
+    if (config.depth > 0) {
+        limits.depth = config.depth;
+    } else {
+        limits.nodes = config.nodes;
+        limits.nodes_specified = true;
+    }
+    // As "ucinewgame": empty table (generation 0), histories and evaluation caches.
+    searcher.clear();
+
+    auto finish = [&game](int white_result_x2, Termination why) {
+        game.white_result_x2 = white_result_x2;
+        game.termination = why;
+        for (Record& record : game.records) {
+            record.meta = static_cast<std::uint8_t>((record.meta & 1U) | (static_cast<unsigned>(white_result_x2) << 1U));
+        }
+    };
+    std::optional<Color> winner;
+    int win_streak = 0;
+    int draw_streak = 0;
+    while (true) {
+        if (const auto end = terminal(board, config.max_ply)) {
+            finish(end->first, end->second);
+            return game;
+        }
+        const SearchInfo info = searcher.search(board, limits, false, Searcher::TTAccess::Exclusive);
+        const Move move = info.best_move;
+        const MoveList legal = board.legal_moves();
+        if (move.is_none() || std::find(legal.begin(), legal.end(), move) == legal.end()) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("search returned no legal best move in " + board.fen());
+        }
+        const int score = info.score;
+        const Color side = board.side_to_move();
+        const bool mate = is_mate_score(score);
+        // forge_selfplay.py eligible_score(): quiet best move, not in check, no mate score, |cp| <= max.
+        if (!board.in_check() && !move.is_capture() && !move.is_promotion() && !mate &&
+            std::abs(score) <= config.max_score) {
+            Record record;
+            record.board = pack_board(board);
+            record.score = static_cast<std::int16_t>(score);
+            record.hce = static_cast<std::int16_t>(std::clamp(Evaluator::evaluate_uncached(board), -32767, 32767));
+            record.meta = pack_meta(side, 0);
+            if (config.text) record.fen = board.fen();
+            game.records.push_back(std::move(record));
+        }
+        // forge_selfplay.py decisive_side() + streak: the same side ahead by win_cp, White's view.
+        const int white_view = side == White ? score : -score;
+        std::optional<Color> ahead;
+        if (white_view >= config.win_cp) ahead = White;
+        else if (white_view <= -config.win_cp) ahead = Black;
+        win_streak = ahead.has_value() ? (ahead == winner ? win_streak + 1 : 1) : 0;
+        winner = ahead;
+        draw_streak = !mate && std::abs(score) <= config.draw_cp ? draw_streak + 1 : 0;
+
+        Undo undo;
+        if (!board.make_move(move, undo)) throw std::runtime_error("best move rejected by make_move");
+        game.moves.push_back(move);
+        if (const auto end = terminal(board, config.max_ply)) {
+            finish(end->first, end->second);
+            return game;
+        }
+        if (config.win_plies > 0 && win_streak >= config.win_plies) {
+            finish(winner == White ? 2 : 0, Termination::AdjudicatedWin);
+            return game;
+        }
+        if (config.draw_plies > 0 && draw_streak >= config.draw_plies && game_ply(board) >= config.draw_min_ply) {
+            finish(1, Termination::AdjudicatedDraw);
+            return game;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Output
+
+namespace {
+
+void append_le(std::string& out, std::uint64_t value, int bytes) {
+    for (int i = 0; i < bytes; ++i) out.push_back(static_cast<char>((value >> (8 * i)) & 0xFFU));
+}
+
+const char* result_text(int white_result_x2) {
+    return white_result_x2 == 2 ? "1.0" : (white_result_x2 == 1 ? "0.5" : "0.0");
+}
+
+const char* result_pgn(int white_result_x2) {
+    return white_result_x2 == 2 ? "1-0" : (white_result_x2 == 1 ? "1/2-1/2" : "0-1");
+}
+
+std::string text_bytes(const std::vector<Record>& records) {
+    std::string out;
+    for (const Record& record : records) {
+        out += record.fen;
+        out += " | ";
+        out += std::to_string(record.score);
+        out += " | ";
+        out += result_text(record.meta >> 1U);
+        out += '\n';
+    }
+    return out;
+}
+
+void sync_and_close(std::FILE* file, const std::string& name) {
+    bool ok = std::fflush(file) == 0;
+#if defined(_WIN32)
+    ok = ok && _commit(_fileno(file)) == 0;
+#else
+    ok = ok && fsync(fileno(file)) == 0;
+#endif
+    ok = std::fclose(file) == 0 && ok;
+    if (!ok) throw std::runtime_error("cannot flush " + name);
+}
+
+// Publishes <part> as <path> without ever replacing an existing <path>.
+void publish(const std::string& part, const std::string& path) {
+#if defined(_WIN32)
+    if (std::rename(part.c_str(), path.c_str()) != 0) {  // Windows rename fails if <path> exists
+        throw std::runtime_error("cannot publish " + path + " (exists?)");
+    }
+#else
+    if (::link(part.c_str(), path.c_str()) != 0) {  // link never replaces <path>
+        throw std::runtime_error("cannot publish " + path + " (exists?)");
+    }
+    (void)::unlink(part.c_str());
+#endif
+}
+
+std::FILE* create_new(const std::string& part) {
+    std::FILE* file = std::fopen(part.c_str(), "wbx");  // exclusive create (C11 "x")
+    if (file == nullptr) throw std::runtime_error("cannot create " + part + " (exists or not writable)");
+    return file;
+}
+
+// The run log (<out>.games or <out>.convert). Its exclusive .part is the prefix claim: a second
+// process with the same prefix fails here, before writing any data.
+class RunLog {
+public:
+    explicit RunLog(std::string path) : path_(std::move(path)) {
+        if (std::filesystem::exists(path_)) throw std::runtime_error("refusing to reuse prefix: " + path_ + " exists");
+        const std::filesystem::path parent = std::filesystem::path(path_).parent_path();
+        if (!parent.empty()) std::filesystem::create_directories(parent);
+        file_ = create_new(path_ + ".part");
+    }
+    ~RunLog() {
+        if (file_ != nullptr) std::fclose(file_);  // unpublished: the .part marks an unfinished run
+    }
+    RunLog(const RunLog&) = delete;
+    RunLog& operator=(const RunLog&) = delete;
+    void write(const std::string& text) {
+        if (std::fwrite(text.data(), 1, text.size(), file_) != text.size()) throw std::runtime_error("cannot write " + path_);
+    }
+    void publish_log() {
+        std::FILE* file = file_;
+        file_ = nullptr;
+        sync_and_close(file, path_);
+        publish(path_ + ".part", path_);
+    }
+private:
+    std::string path_;
+    std::FILE* file_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+} // namespace
+
+void write_new_file(const std::string& path, const std::string& bytes) {
+    if (std::filesystem::exists(path)) throw std::runtime_error("refusing to overwrite " + path);
+    const std::string part = path + ".part";
+    std::FILE* file = create_new(part);
+    if (std::fwrite(bytes.data(), 1, bytes.size(), file) != bytes.size()) {
+        std::fclose(file);
+        throw std::runtime_error("cannot write " + part);
+    }
+    sync_and_close(file, part);
+    publish(part, path);
+}
+
+std::string shard_name(const std::string& prefix, std::uint64_t index) {
+    std::ostringstream name;
+    name << prefix << '_' << std::setw(3) << std::setfill('0') << index;
+    return name.str();
+}
+
+std::string pos_bytes(const std::vector<Record>& records) {
+    const std::uint64_t n = records.size();
+    std::string out;
+    out.reserve(static_cast<std::size_t>(16 + 35 * n));
+    out.append("FPOS", 4);
+    append_le(out, 1, 4);
+    append_le(out, n, 8);
+    for (const Record& record : records) out.append(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(record.board.data()), record.board.size());
+    for (const Record& record : records) append_le(out, static_cast<std::uint16_t>(record.score), 2);
+    for (const Record& record : records) out.push_back(static_cast<char>(record.meta));
+    return out;
+}
+
+std::string hce_bytes(const std::vector<Record>& records) {
+    const std::uint64_t n = records.size();
+    std::string out;
+    out.reserve(static_cast<std::size_t>(16 + 2 * n));
+    out.append("FHCE", 4);
+    append_le(out, 1, 4);
+    append_le(out, n, 8);
+    for (const Record& record : records) append_le(out, static_cast<std::uint16_t>(record.hce), 2);
+    return out;
+}
+
+ShardWriter::ShardWriter(std::string prefix, std::uint64_t positions_per_file, bool text, std::string info)
+    : prefix_(std::move(prefix)), positions_per_file_(std::max<std::uint64_t>(1, positions_per_file)), text_(text),
+      info_(std::move(info)) {}
+
+void ShardWriter::add(Record record) { records_.push_back(std::move(record)); }
+
+void ShardWriter::maybe_close() {
+    if (records_.size() >= positions_per_file_) close();
+}
+
+void ShardWriter::close() {
+    if (records_.empty()) return;
+    const std::string base = shard_name(prefix_, shard_index_);
+    write_new_file(base + ".hce", hce_bytes(records_));
+    if (text_) write_new_file(base + ".txt", text_bytes(records_));
+    write_new_file(base + ".info", info_ + "records=" + std::to_string(records_.size()) + "\n");
+    write_new_file(base + ".pos", pos_bytes(records_));  // last: a .pos means a complete shard
+    written_ += records_.size();
+    ++shard_index_;
+    records_.clear();
+}
+
+std::optional<Record> parse_text_record(const std::string& raw) {
+    // fen2bin.cpp: "FEN | score | result", score int in [-32767, 32767], result 0 / 0.5 / 1 (White).
+    // hce_dump.cpp: FEN = text before the first '|', trailing blanks removed, Board::set_fen.
+    std::string line = raw;
+    if (!line.empty() && line.back() == '\r') line.pop_back();
+    const std::size_t first = line.find('|');
+    if (first == std::string::npos) return std::nullopt;
+    const std::size_t second = line.find('|', first + 1);
+    if (second == std::string::npos) return std::nullopt;
+    std::string fen = line.substr(0, first);
+    while (!fen.empty() && (fen.back() == ' ' || fen.back() == '\t')) fen.pop_back();
+    Board board;
+    if (!board.set_fen(fen)) return std::nullopt;
+    const std::string score_text = line.substr(first + 1, second - first - 1);
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const long score = std::strtol(score_text.c_str(), &end, 10);
+    if (end == score_text.c_str() || score < -32767 || score > 32767) return std::nullopt;
+    const std::string result_field = line.substr(second + 1);
+    const double result = std::strtod(result_field.c_str(), &end);
+    if (end == result_field.c_str()) return std::nullopt;
+    int white_result_x2 = 0;
+    if (result == 0.0) white_result_x2 = 0;
+    else if (result == 0.5) white_result_x2 = 1;
+    else if (result == 1.0) white_result_x2 = 2;
+    else return std::nullopt;
+    Record record;
+    record.board = pack_board(board);
+    record.score = static_cast<std::int16_t>(score);
+    record.hce = static_cast<std::int16_t>(std::clamp(Evaluator::evaluate_uncached(board), -32767, 32767));
+    record.meta = pack_meta(board.side_to_move(), white_result_x2);
+    record.fen = fen;
+    return record;
+}
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Command line
+
+namespace {
+
+struct UsageError : std::runtime_error {
+    using std::runtime_error::runtime_error;
+};
+
+constexpr const char* Usage =
+    "usage: forge datagen --out PREFIX (--games N | --positions N) [options]\n"
+    "       forge datagen --out PREFIX --convert TEXT_FILE [--positions-per-file N]\n"
+    "  --nodes N (5000) | --depth D        fixed search budget per move\n"
+    "  --seed S (1)  --hash MB (16)  --random-plies N (8)  --max-ply N (300)  --max-score CP (3000)\n"
+    "  --win-cp CP (2000) --win-plies N (4, 0 = off)   win adjudication (same side, consecutive plies)\n"
+    "  --draw-cp CP (0) --draw-plies N (0 = off) --draw-min-ply N (0)   draw adjudication\n"
+    "  --openings FILE   EPD/FEN start positions (default: the start position)\n"
+    "  --positions-per-file N (1000000)  --text (also write FEN | score | result lines)\n"
+    "Writes <PREFIX>_NNN.pos/.hce (FPOS/FHCE v1), <PREFIX>_NNN.info and <PREFIX>.games; never overwrites.";
+
+template <typename T>
+T parse_number(std::string_view name, std::string_view text, T minimum) {
+    T parsed{};
+    const auto [ptr, ec] = std::from_chars(text.data(), text.data() + text.size(), parsed);
+    if (ec != std::errc{} || ptr != text.data() + text.size() || parsed < minimum) {
+        throw UsageError("invalid value for " + std::string(name) + ": " + std::string(text));
+    }
+    return parsed;
+}
+
+std::vector<Board> load_starts(const std::string& path) {
+    std::vector<Board> starts;
+    if (path.empty()) {
+        Board board;
+        (void)board.set_fen(Board::StandardStartFen);
+        starts.push_back(board);
+        return starts;
+    }
+    std::ifstream in(path, std::ios::binary);
+    if (!in) throw std::runtime_error("cannot open openings file " + path);
+    std::string line;
+    std::size_t number = 0;
+    while (std::getline(in, line)) {
+        ++number;
+        std::istringstream fields(line);
+        std::vector<std::string> tokens;
+        std::string token;
+        while (tokens.size() < 6 && fields >> token) tokens.push_back(token);
+        if (tokens.empty() || tokens[0].front() == '#') continue;
+        if (tokens.size() < 4) throw std::runtime_error(path + ":" + std::to_string(number) + ": not a FEN/EPD line");
+        std::string fen = tokens[0] + ' ' + tokens[1] + ' ' + tokens[2] + ' ' + tokens[3];
+        const bool counters = tokens.size() == 6 &&
+            std::all_of(tokens[4].begin(), tokens[4].end(), [](char c) { return c >= '0' && c <= '9'; }) &&
+            std::all_of(tokens[5].begin(), tokens[5].end(), [](char c) { return c >= '0' && c <= '9'; });
+        fen += counters ? ' ' + tokens[4] + ' ' + tokens[5] : std::string(" 0 1");
+        Board board;
+        std::string error;
+        if (!board.set_fen(fen, &error)) throw std::runtime_error(path + ":" + std::to_string(number) + ": " + error);
+        starts.push_back(board);
+    }
+    if (starts.empty()) throw std::runtime_error("no positions in openings file " + path);
+    return starts;
+}
+
+std::string config_text(const Config& config) {
+    std::ostringstream text;
+    text << "format=FPOS v1 (.pos) + FHCE v1 (.hce, Evaluator::evaluate_uncached), forge datagen v2 (S32)\n";
+    if (!config.convert.empty()) {
+        text << "mode=convert\n";
+        return text.str();
+    }
+    text << "mode=selfplay\nseed=" << config.seed << "\nbudget=" << (config.depth > 0 ? "depth " : "nodes ")
+         << (config.depth > 0 ? static_cast<std::uint64_t>(config.depth) : config.nodes) << "\nhash_mb=" << config.hash_mb
+         << "\nrandom_plies=" << config.random_plies << "\nmax_ply=" << config.max_ply << "\nwin_cp=" << config.win_cp
+         << "\nwin_plies=" << config.win_plies << "\ndraw_cp=" << config.draw_cp << "\ndraw_plies=" << config.draw_plies
+         << "\ndraw_min_ply=" << config.draw_min_ply << "\nmax_score=" << config.max_score
+         << "\nopenings=" << (config.openings.empty() ? std::string("startpos") : std::filesystem::path(config.openings).filename().string())
+         << '\n';
+    return text.str();
+}
+
+double seconds_since(std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point start) {
+    return std::chrono::duration<double>(std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - start).count();
+}
+
+int generate(const Config& config) {
+    const std::vector<Board> starts = load_starts(config.openings);
+    const auto started = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
+    RunLog log(config.out + ".games");
+    const std::string info = config_text(config);
+    std::istringstream header(info);
+    for (std::string line; std::getline(header, line);) log.write("# " + line + '\n');
+    log.write("# game\tattempt\tstart\tresult\ttermination\trecords\topening\tmoves\n");
+    ShardWriter writer(config.out, config.positions_per_file, config.text, info);
+    Searcher searcher(config.hash_mb, 1);
+    std::uint64_t attempts = 0;
+    std::uint64_t games = 0;
+    std::uint64_t plies = 0;
+    std::array<std::uint64_t, 3> results{};
+    std::array<std::uint64_t, 8> terminations{};
+    while ((config.games == 0 || games < config.games) &&
+           (config.positions == 0 || writer.written() + writer.pending() < config.positions)) {
+        if (attempts - games > 100000) throw std::runtime_error("no game survives the random opening");
+        const std::uint64_t index = attempts++;
+        Rng rng(game_seed(config.seed, index));
+        const std::size_t start = starts.size() == 1 ? 0 : static_cast<std::size_t>(rng.below(starts.size()));
+        std::optional<Game> game = play_game(searcher, config, starts[start], rng);
+        if (!game.has_value()) continue;
+        ++games;
+        plies += game->moves.size();
+        ++results[static_cast<std::size_t>(game->white_result_x2)];
+        ++terminations[static_cast<std::size_t>(game->termination)];
+        std::string line = std::to_string(games - 1) + '\t' + std::to_string(index) + '\t' + std::to_string(start) + '\t' +
+                           result_pgn(game->white_result_x2) + '\t' + termination_name(game->termination) + '\t' +
+                           std::to_string(game->records.size()) + '\t';
+        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < game->opening.size(); ++i) line += (i ? " " : "") + Board::move_to_uci(game->opening[i]);
+        line += '\t';
+        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < game->moves.size(); ++i) line += (i ? " " : "") + Board::move_to_uci(game->moves[i]);
+        log.write(line + '\n');
+        for (Record& record : game->records) writer.add(std::move(record));
+        writer.maybe_close();
+    }
+    writer.close();
+    log.publish_log();
+    const double elapsed = seconds_since(started);
+    std::cout << "{\"mode\":\"selfplay\",\"games\":" << games << ",\"attempts\":" << attempts << ",\"positions\":"
+              << writer.written() << ",\"shards\":" << writer.shards() << ",\"searched_plies\":" << plies
+              << ",\"results\":{\"1-0\":" << results[2] << ",\"1/2-1/2\":" << results[1] << ",\"0-1\":" << results[0] << "},\"terminations\":{";
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < terminations.size(); ++i) {
+        std::cout << (i ? "," : "") << '"' << termination_name(static_cast<Termination>(i)) << "\":" << terminations[i];
+    }
+    std::cout << "},\"elapsed_s\":" << elapsed << ",\"positions_per_s\":"
+              << (elapsed > 0 ? static_cast<double>(writer.written()) / elapsed : 0.0) << "}" << std::endl;
+    return 0;
+}
+
+int convert(const Config& config) {
+    std::ifstream in(config.convert, std::ios::binary);
+    if (!in) throw std::runtime_error("cannot open " + config.convert);
+    const auto started = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
+    RunLog log(config.out + ".convert");
+    ShardWriter writer(config.out, config.positions_per_file, false, config_text(config));
+    std::string line;
+    std::uint64_t number = 0;
+    while (std::getline(in, line)) {
+        ++number;
+        std::optional<Record> record = parse_text_record(line);
+        if (!record.has_value()) throw std::runtime_error(config.convert + ":" + std::to_string(number) + ": invalid record");
+        record->fen.clear();
+        writer.add(std::move(*record));
+        writer.maybe_close();
+    }
+    writer.close();
+    log.write("source=" + std::filesystem::path(config.convert).filename().string() + "\nlines=" + std::to_string(number) +
+              "\nrecords=" + std::to_string(writer.written()) + "\nshards=" + std::to_string(writer.shards()) + "\n");
+    log.publish_log();
+    std::cout << "{\"mode\":\"convert\",\"lines\":" << number << ",\"positions\":" << writer.written()
+              << ",\"shards\":" << writer.shards() << ",\"elapsed_s\":" << seconds_since(started) << "}" << std::endl;
+    return 0;
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
+int run(int argc, const char* const* argv) {
+    Config config;
+    try {
+        for (int i = 0; i < argc; ++i) {
+            const std::string_view arg = argv[i];
+            auto next = [&]() -> std::string_view {
+                if (i + 1 >= argc) throw UsageError("missing value for " + std::string(arg));
+                return argv[++i];
+            };
+            if (arg == "--out") config.out = std::string(next());
+            else if (arg == "--convert") config.convert = std::string(next());
+            else if (arg == "--openings") config.openings = std::string(next());
+            else if (arg == "--nodes") config.nodes = parse_number<std::uint64_t>(arg, next(), 1);
+            else if (arg == "--depth") config.depth = parse_number<int>(arg, next(), 1);
+            else if (arg == "--games") config.games = parse_number<std::uint64_t>(arg, next(), 1);
+            else if (arg == "--positions") config.positions = parse_number<std::uint64_t>(arg, next(), 1);
+            else if (arg == "--positions-per-file") config.positions_per_file = parse_number<std::uint64_t>(arg, next(), 1);
+            else if (arg == "--seed") config.seed = parse_number<std::uint64_t>(arg, next(), 0);
+            else if (arg == "--hash") config.hash_mb = parse_number<std::size_t>(arg, next(), 1);
+            else if (arg == "--random-plies") config.random_plies = parse_number<int>(arg, next(), 0);
+            else if (arg == "--max-ply") config.max_ply = parse_number<int>(arg, next(), 1);
+            else if (arg == "--max-score") config.max_score = parse_number<int>(arg, next(), 0);
+            else if (arg == "--win-cp") config.win_cp = parse_number<int>(arg, next(), 1);
+            else if (arg == "--win-plies") config.win_plies = parse_number<int>(arg, next(), 0);
+            else if (arg == "--draw-cp") config.draw_cp = parse_number<int>(arg, next(), 0);
+            else if (arg == "--draw-plies") config.draw_plies = parse_number<int>(arg, next(), 0);
+            else if (arg == "--draw-min-ply") config.draw_min_ply = parse_number<int>(arg, next(), 0);
+            else if (arg == "--text") config.text = true;
+            else if (arg == "--help" || arg == "-h") {
+                std::cout << Usage << std::endl;
+                return 0;
+            } else {
+                throw UsageError("unknown option " + std::string(arg));
+            }
+        }
+        if (config.out.empty()) throw UsageError("--out is required");
+        if (config.convert.empty() && config.games == 0 && config.positions == 0) {
+            throw UsageError("--games or --positions is required");
+        }
+        if (config.max_ply <= config.random_plies) throw UsageError("--max-ply must exceed --random-plies");
+        if (config.hash_mb > 2048) throw UsageError("--hash must be at most 2048");
+    } catch (const UsageError& error) {
+        std::cerr << "datagen: " << error.what() << '\n' << Usage << std::endl;
+        return 2;
+    }
+    try {
+        return config.convert.empty() ? generate(config) : convert(config);
+    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+        std::cerr << "datagen: " << error.what() << std::endl;
+        return 1;
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace forge::datagen

+#if FORGE_NNUE_O2
+// One accumulator vector: the kernels below are written once against these
+// helpers. Lanes never interact, so the vector width only changes how many
+// lanes one instruction handles, never the operations a lane sees.
+#if FORGE_NNUE_KERNEL_AVX2
+using Vec = __m256;
+constexpr int VecWidth = 8;
+inline Vec vec_load(const float* p) { return _mm256_load_ps(p); }
+inline void vec_store(float* p, Vec v) { _mm256_store_ps(p, v); }
+inline Vec vec_add(Vec a, Vec b) { return _mm256_add_ps(a, b); }
+inline Vec vec_sub(Vec a, Vec b) { return _mm256_sub_ps(a, b); }
+#elif FORGE_NNUE_KERNEL_SSE2
+using Vec = __m128;
+constexpr int VecWidth = 4;
+inline Vec vec_load(const float* p) { return _mm_load_ps(p); }
+inline void vec_store(float* p, Vec v) { _mm_store_ps(p, v); }
+inline Vec vec_add(Vec a, Vec b) { return _mm_add_ps(a, b); }
+inline Vec vec_sub(Vec a, Vec b) { return _mm_sub_ps(a, b); }
+#else
+using Vec = float;
+constexpr int VecWidth = 1;
+inline Vec vec_load(const float* p) { return *p; }
+inline void vec_store(float* p, Vec v) { *p = v; }
+inline Vec vec_add(Vec a, Vec b) { return a + b; }
+inline Vec vec_sub(Vec a, Vec b) { return a - b; }
+#endif
+static_assert(HiddenSize % VecWidth == 0, "HiddenSize must be a multiple of the vector width");
+
+// Update kernels. Each lane takes the parent value and then applies the piece
+// changes in DirtyPieces order (subtract a removal, add an addition), which is
+// exactly what the generic copy-then-apply loop in update() computes. `out`
+// may equal `in` (in-place update): every vector is read before it is written.
+
+// Quiet moves and noncapturing promotions: (in - r) + a.
+inline void apply_sub_add(float* out, const float* in, const float* r, const float* a) {
+    for (int h = 0; h < HiddenSize; h += VecWidth)
+        vec_store(out + h, vec_add(vec_sub(vec_load(in + h), vec_load(r + h)), vec_load(a + h)));
+}
+
+// Captures, en passant and capturing promotions: ((in - r0) - r1) + a.
+inline void apply_sub_sub_add(float* out, const float* in, const float* r0, const float* r1, const float* a) {
+    for (int h = 0; h < HiddenSize; h += VecWidth)
+        vec_store(out + h, vec_add(vec_sub(vec_sub(vec_load(in + h), vec_load(r0 + h)), vec_load(r1 + h)), vec_load(a + h)));
+}
+
+// Castling (king, then rook): (((in - r0) + a0) - r1) + a1.
+inline void apply_sub_add_sub_add(float* out, const float* in, const float* r0, const float* a0, const float* r1,
+                                  const float* a1) {
+    for (int h = 0; h < HiddenSize; h += VecWidth)
+        vec_store(out + h, vec_add(vec_sub(vec_add(vec_sub(vec_load(in + h), vec_load(r0 + h)), vec_load(a0 + h)),
+                                           vec_load(r1 + h)),
+                                   vec_load(a1 + h)));
+}
+
+// Refresh kernel: out = bias + columns[0] + columns[1] + ..., lane by lane in
+// column order (the order in which AL1 s198 adds the pieces).
+inline void accumulate_columns(float* out, const float* bias, const float* const* columns, std::size_t count) {
+#if FORGE_NNUE_KERNEL_AVX2 || FORGE_NNUE_KERNEL_SSE2
+    // Register-blocked: a block of Regs vectors stays in registers while every
+    // column is added, instead of a load and a store per column and vector.
+    constexpr int Regs = HiddenSize % (8 * VecWidth) == 0 ? 8 : HiddenSize % (4 * VecWidth) == 0 ? 4 : 1;
+    for (int base = 0; base < HiddenSize; base += Regs * VecWidth) {
+        Vec sums[Regs];
+        for (int k = 0; k < Regs; ++k) sums[k] = vec_load(bias + base + k * VecWidth);
+        for (std::size_t c = 0; c < count; ++c) {
+            const float* source = columns[c] + base;
+            for (int k = 0; k < Regs; ++k) sums[k] = vec_add(sums[k], vec_load(source + k * VecWidth));
+        }
+        for (int k = 0; k < Regs; ++k) vec_store(out + base + k * VecWidth, sums[k]);
+    }
+#else
+    std::copy_n(bias, HiddenSize, out);
+    for (std::size_t c = 0; c < count; ++c) {
+        const float* source = columns[c];
+        for (int h = 0; h < HiddenSize; ++h) out[h] += source[h];
+    }
+#endif
+}

+                                  column(w, perspective, changes[2].piece, changes[2].square),
+                                  column(w, perspective, changes[3].piece, changes[3].square));
+#endif // FORGE_NNUE_O2
+#if FORGE_NNUE_O2
+    // Null moves (no change) and any other change list: copy, then apply each
+    // change in order.
+#endif // FORGE_NNUE_O2
+#if !FORGE_NNUE_O2
+#else  // FORGE_NNUE_O2
+            const PieceChange& change = changes[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)];
+            const float* feature = column(w, perspective, change.piece, change.square);
+            if (change.add) add_feature(acc, feature);
+            else sub_feature(acc, feature);
+#endif // FORGE_NNUE_O2

+    // scalar fallback) produces bit-identical results without -ffast-math.
+#endif // FORGE_NNUE_O2

+#if FORGE_NNUE_AVX2
