+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// v2 networks (forge/nnue_v2.hpp): loading and selection, all cold.
+// This translation unit is linked before search.cpp; the functions above keep
+// their code and addresses because nothing here is placed in .text (cold
+// functions go to .text.unlikely, which the PE linker puts after every
+// object's .text) and nothing here calls the local helpers above.
+namespace forge::nnue::v2 {
+
+// Constant-initialised: no dynamic initialisation or exit-time code.
+const Net* active_net = nullptr;
+
+namespace {
+
+Net* loaded_net = nullptr;   // owned; deleted by load() / unload()
+std::uint64_t last_bytes_read = 0;   // bytes of its file the last load() read
+
+constexpr std::uint32_t FlagResidual = 1U;
+constexpr std::uint32_t FlagMirror = 2U;
+constexpr char Magic[8] = {'F', 'O', 'R', 'G', 'E', 'N', 'N', '2'};
+
+[[gnu::cold]] std::uint32_t read_u32(const unsigned char* bytes, std::size_t offset) {
+    return static_cast<std::uint32_t>(bytes[offset]) | static_cast<std::uint32_t>(bytes[offset + 1]) << 8U |
+           static_cast<std::uint32_t>(bytes[offset + 2]) << 16U | static_cast<std::uint32_t>(bytes[offset + 3]) << 24U;
+}
+
+[[gnu::cold]] float read_f32(const unsigned char* bytes, std::size_t offset) {
+    const std::uint32_t bits = read_u32(bytes, offset);
+    float value = 0.0F;
+    std::memcpy(&value, &bits, sizeof(value));
+    return value;
+}
+
+[[gnu::cold]] std::int16_t read_i16(const unsigned char* bytes, std::size_t offset) {
+    return static_cast<std::int16_t>(static_cast<std::uint16_t>(bytes[offset] | (bytes[offset + 1] << 8U)));
+}
+
+[[gnu::cold]] std::uint64_t fnv1a64(const unsigned char* data, std::size_t size) {
+    std::uint64_t hash = 0xcbf29ce484222325ULL;
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
+        hash ^= data[i];
+        hash *= 0x100000001b3ULL;
+    }
+    return hash;
+}
+
+[[gnu::cold]] bool fail(std::string* error, const std::string& text) {
+    if (error != nullptr) *error = text;
+    return false;
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
+[[gnu::cold]] std::uint64_t file_bytes(int king_buckets, int output_buckets, std::uint32_t feature_set) {
+    const std::uint64_t w1 = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(king_buckets) * InputCount * HiddenSize;
+    const std::uint64_t wt = (feature_set & FeatureSetThreats) != 0U ? static_cast<std::uint64_t>(ThreatFeatures) * HiddenSize : 0U;
+    const std::uint64_t wp = (feature_set & FeatureSetPawnPairs) != 0U ? static_cast<std::uint64_t>(PawnPairFeatures) * HiddenSize : 0U;
+    const std::uint64_t w2 = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(output_buckets) * 2U * HiddenSize;
+    return static_cast<std::uint64_t>(HeaderBytes) + 2U * (w1 + wt + wp + HiddenSize + w2 + static_cast<std::uint64_t>(output_buckets)) + 8U;
+}
+
+[[gnu::cold]] std::uint64_t last_load_bytes_read() { return last_bytes_read; }
+
+[[gnu::cold]] bool load(const std::string& path, std::string* error) {
+    // Whatever happens below, the previously loaded net is gone: after a failed load no net is
+    // loaded, so the engine cannot evaluate with a net other than the one last requested.
+    unload();
+    last_bytes_read = 0;
+    std::ifstream file(path, std::ios::binary);
+    if (!file) return fail(error, "cannot open " + path);
+    // The file size comes first (seek to the end): nothing is allocated or read before the size is
+    // known to be the one the header implies.
+    file.seekg(0, std::ios::end);
+    const std::streamoff end = file.tellg();
+    if (!file || end < 0) return fail(error, "cannot determine the size of " + path);
+    const auto size = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(end);
+    const std::uint64_t largest = file_bytes(MaxKingBuckets, MaxOutputBuckets, FeatureSetThreats | FeatureSetPawnPairs);
+    if (size < static_cast<std::uint64_t>(HeaderBytes) + 8U) {
+        return fail(error, "file too short for a v2 net (" + std::to_string(size) + " bytes)");
+    }
+    if (size > largest) {
+        return fail(error, "file too large for a v2 net (" + std::to_string(size) + " bytes; the largest valid v2 file has " +
+                               std::to_string(largest) + ")");
+    }
+    std::array<unsigned char, HeaderBytes> header{};
+    file.seekg(0, std::ios::beg);
+    file.read(reinterpret_cast<char*>(header.data()), HeaderBytes);
+    if (!file) return fail(error, "cannot read the header of " + path);
+    last_bytes_read = HeaderBytes;
+    const unsigned char* bytes = header.data();
+    if (std::memcmp(bytes, Magic, sizeof(Magic)) != 0) return fail(error, "bad magic (not a FORGENN2 file)");
+    if (read_u32(bytes, 8) != 2U) return fail(error, "unsupported version " + std::to_string(read_u32(bytes, 8)));
+    if (read_u32(bytes, 12) != static_cast<std::uint32_t>(HeaderBytes)) return fail(error, "unexpected header size");
+    const std::uint32_t feature_set = read_u32(bytes, 16);
+    if ((feature_set & ~(FeatureSetThreats | FeatureSetPawnPairs)) != 0U) return fail(error, "unsupported feature set " + std::to_string(feature_set));
+    if (read_u32(bytes, 20) != static_cast<std::uint32_t>(InputCount)) return fail(error, "inputs per bucket must be 768");
+    if (read_u32(bytes, 24) != static_cast<std::uint32_t>(HiddenSize)) {
+        return fail(error, "hidden size " + std::to_string(read_u32(bytes, 24)) + " != engine hidden size " +
+                               std::to_string(HiddenSize) + " (FORGE_NNUE_HIDDEN)");
+    }
+    const std::uint32_t king_buckets = read_u32(bytes, 28);
+    const std::uint32_t output_buckets = read_u32(bytes, 32);
+    const std::uint32_t flags = read_u32(bytes, 36);
+    if (king_buckets < 1U || king_buckets > static_cast<std::uint32_t>(MaxKingBuckets)) return fail(error, "king buckets out of range 1-32");
+    if (output_buckets < 1U || output_buckets > static_cast<std::uint32_t>(MaxOutputBuckets)) {
+        return fail(error, "output buckets " + std::to_string(output_buckets) + " not supported (max " +
+                               std::to_string(MaxOutputBuckets) + ")");
+    }
+    if ((flags & ~(FlagResidual | FlagMirror)) != 0U) return fail(error, "unknown flag bits");
+    if (read_f32(bytes, 40) != WeightScale || read_f32(bytes, 44) != OutputScale || read_f32(bytes, 48) != SigmoidK) {
+        return fail(error, "quantisation scales / sigmoid K differ from the engine's (4096, 4096, 2.25)");
+    }
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 64; ++i) {
+        if (bytes[52 + i] >= king_buckets) return fail(error, "king bucket map entry out of range");
+    }
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 33; ++i) {
+        if (bytes[116 + i] >= output_buckets) return fail(error, "output bucket map entry out of range");
+    }
+    for (std::size_t i = 149; i < static_cast<std::size_t>(HeaderBytes); ++i) {
+        if (bytes[i] != 0U) return fail(error, "reserved header bytes are not zero");
+    }
+    const bool threats = (feature_set & FeatureSetThreats) != 0U;
+    const bool pawn_pairs = (feature_set & FeatureSetPawnPairs) != 0U;   // O7
+    const std::size_t w1_count = static_cast<std::size_t>(king_buckets) * InputCount * HiddenSize;
+    const std::size_t wt_count = threats ? static_cast<std::size_t>(ThreatFeatures) * HiddenSize : 0U;
+    const std::size_t wp_count = pawn_pairs ? static_cast<std::size_t>(PawnPairFeatures) * HiddenSize : 0U;
+    const std::size_t w2_count = static_cast<std::size_t>(output_buckets) * 2U * HiddenSize;
+    const std::uint64_t expected = file_bytes(static_cast<int>(king_buckets), static_cast<int>(output_buckets), feature_set);
+    if (size != expected) {
+        return fail(error, "file size " + std::to_string(size) + " bytes does not match the " + std::to_string(expected) +
+                               " bytes implied by its header (" + std::to_string(king_buckets) + " king buckets, " +
+                               std::to_string(output_buckets) + " output buckets, hidden " + std::to_string(HiddenSize) +
+                               (threats ? ", threat inputs" : "") + (pawn_pairs ? ", pawn-pair inputs" : "") + "); the weights were not read");
+    }
+    // Only now are the weight blocks allocated and read.
+    std::vector<unsigned char> all(static_cast<std::size_t>(expected));
+    std::copy(header.begin(), header.end(), all.begin());
+    file.read(reinterpret_cast<char*>(all.data() + HeaderBytes), static_cast<std::streamsize>(expected - HeaderBytes));
+    if (!file) return fail(error, "cannot read the weights of " + path);
+    last_bytes_read = expected;
+    bytes = all.data();
+    std::uint64_t stored = 0;
+    for (std::size_t i = 8; i-- > 0;) stored = (stored << 8U) | bytes[all.size() - 8U + i];
+    const std::uint64_t checksum = fnv1a64(bytes, all.size() - 8U);
+    if (stored != checksum) return fail(error, "checksum mismatch (file damaged)");
+
+    auto net = std::make_unique<Net>();
+    net->path = path;
+    net->king_buckets = static_cast<int>(king_buckets);
+    net->output_buckets = static_cast<int>(output_buckets);
+    net->mirror = (flags & FlagMirror) != 0U;
+    net->residual = (flags & FlagResidual) != 0U;
+    net->threats = threats;
+    net->pawn_pairs = pawn_pairs;
+    net->checksum = checksum;
+    std::copy(bytes + 52, bytes + 116, net->king_map.begin());
+    std::copy(bytes + 116, bytes + 149, net->output_map.begin());
+    net->w1.allocate(w1_count);
+    if (threats) net->wt.allocate(wt_count);
+    if (pawn_pairs) net->wp.allocate(wp_count);
+    net->b1.allocate(HiddenSize);
+    net->w2.allocate(w2_count);
+    net->b2.assign(output_buckets, 0.0F);
+    std::size_t offset = static_cast<std::size_t>(HeaderBytes);
+    // Same de-quantisation as the compiled-in network (weights(): int / scale).
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < w1_count; ++i, offset += 2) net->w1.data()[i] = static_cast<float>(read_i16(bytes, offset)) / WeightScale;
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < wt_count; ++i, offset += 2) net->wt.data()[i] = static_cast<float>(read_i16(bytes, offset)) / WeightScale;
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < wp_count; ++i, offset += 2) net->wp.data()[i] = static_cast<float>(read_i16(bytes, offset)) / WeightScale;
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < HiddenSize; ++i, offset += 2) net->b1.data()[i] = static_cast<float>(read_i16(bytes, offset)) / WeightScale;
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < w2_count; ++i, offset += 2) net->w2.data()[i] = static_cast<float>(read_i16(bytes, offset)) / OutputScale;
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < output_buckets; ++i, offset += 2) net->b2[i] = static_cast<float>(read_i16(bytes, offset)) / OutputScale;
+
+    loaded_net = net.release();
+    return true;
+}
+
+[[gnu::cold]] void unload() {
+    if (active_net == loaded_net) active_net = nullptr;
+    delete loaded_net;
+    loaded_net = nullptr;
+}
+
+[[gnu::cold]] const Net* loaded() { return loaded_net; }
+
+[[gnu::cold]] std::string summary(const Net& net) {
+    std::ostringstream out;
+    out << net.path << " king buckets " << net.king_buckets << " output buckets " << net.output_buckets << " hidden "
+        << HiddenSize << (net.mirror ? " mirrored" : " unmirrored") << (net.residual ? " residual" : " stand-alone")
+        << (net.threats ? " threat inputs" : "") << (net.pawn_pairs ? " pawn-pair inputs" : "") << " fnv1a " << std::hex << std::setw(16) << std::setfill('0') << net.checksum;
+    return out.str();
+}
+
+[[gnu::cold]] const Net* select(int king_buckets, int output_buckets, std::string* message) {
+    active_net = nullptr;
+    std::string text;
+    const std::string options = "NNUEKingBuckets " + std::to_string(std::max(king_buckets, 0)) +
+                                ", NNUEOutputBuckets " + std::to_string(std::max(output_buckets, 0));
+    if (king_buckets <= 0 && output_buckets <= 0) {
+        text = "off (NNUEKingBuckets 0, NNUEOutputBuckets 0): compiled-in network";
+    } else if (loaded_net == nullptr) {
+        text = options + " needs a v2 net (NNUEFile)";
+    } else if (king_buckets > 0 ? loaded_net->king_buckets != king_buckets
+                                : (loaded_net->king_buckets != 1 || loaded_net->mirror)) {
+        text = options + " does not match the loaded net (" + std::to_string(loaded_net->king_buckets) +
+               (loaded_net->mirror ? " mirrored" : "") + " king buckets)";
+    } else if (output_buckets > 0 ? loaded_net->output_buckets != output_buckets : loaded_net->output_buckets != 1) {
+        text = options + " does not match the loaded net (" + std::to_string(loaded_net->output_buckets) +
+               " output buckets)";
+    } else {
+        active_net = loaded_net;
+        text = "active: " + summary(*loaded_net);
+    }
+    if (message != nullptr) *message = text;
+    return active_net;
+}
+
+[[gnu::cold]] Description describe(const Net& net) {
+    Description d;
+    d.path = net.path;
+    d.king_buckets = net.king_buckets;
+    d.output_buckets = net.output_buckets;
+    d.mirror = net.mirror;
+    d.residual = net.residual;
+    d.threats = net.threats;
+    d.pawn_pairs = net.pawn_pairs;
+    d.checksum = net.checksum;
+    d.output_bucket_map = net.output_map;
+    return d;
+}
+
+[[gnu::cold]] RefreshCache& make_cache(const Net& net, int worker_id) {
+    std::unique_ptr<RefreshCache>& slot = net.caches[static_cast<std::size_t>(std::clamp(worker_id, 0, MaxWorkers - 1))];
+    if (slot == nullptr) {
+        auto cache = std::make_unique<RefreshCache>();
+        cache->entries.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(2 * net.king_buckets * 2));
+        for (RefreshCache::Entry& entry : cache->entries) {
+            std::copy(net.b1.data(), net.b1.data() + HiddenSize, entry.values.begin());
+            entry.pieces.fill(0);
+        }
+        slot = std::move(cache);
+    }
+    return *slot;
+}
+
+} // namespace forge::nnue::v2

-bool Worker::should_stop() {

-bool Worker::is_draw(const Board& board, int ply) const {

-                    std::copy_n((ss + 1)->pv.begin(), (ss + 1)->pv_length, ss->pv.begin() + 1);
-                    ss->pv_length = (ss + 1)->pv_length + 1;
-                }
-                if (score >= beta) {
-                    ++ss->cutoff_count;
-                    break;
-                }
-                alpha = score;
-            }
-        }
-        if (quiet && quiet_count < static_cast<int>(quiets_tried.size())) quiets_tried[static_cast<std::size_t>(quiet_count++)] = move;
-        else if (!quiet && capture_count < static_cast<int>(captures_tried.size())) captures_tried[static_cast<std::size_t>(capture_count++)] = move;
-    }
-
-    if (move_count == 0) {
-        if (!excluded.is_none()) return alpha;
-        if (root_node && root_restricted_) return 0;
-        return in_check ? mated_in(ply) : 0;
-    }
-
-    if (best_score >= beta) {
-        if (!best_move.is_capture() && !best_move.is_promotion()) {
-            update_quiet_stats(board, ss, best_move, depth + (best_score > beta + 100), quiets_tried.data(), quiet_count);
-        }
-        update_capture_stats(board, best_move, depth, captures_tried.data(), capture_count);
-    } else if (!best_move.is_none() && (!best_move.is_capture() && !best_move.is_promotion())) {
-        // Even without a cutoff, a new PV move earned its place.
-        apply_history((*main_history_)[static_cast<std::size_t>(board.side_to_move())][best_move.from][best_move.to],
-                      history_bonus(depth) / 4);
-    } else if (tune::FailLowContinuationBonus > 0 && best_move.is_none() && !(ss - 1)->current_move.is_none() &&
-               (ss - 1)->moved_piece != Empty && !(ss - 1)->current_move.is_capture() && ply > 0) {
-        // Every move failed low: the parent's quiet move refuted this line.
-        update_continuation(ss - 1, (ss - 1)->moved_piece, (ss - 1)->current_move.to,
-                            history_bonus(depth) * tune::FailLowContinuationBonus / 100);
-    }
-
-    const Bound bound = best_score >= beta ? BoundLower : (pv_node && !best_move.is_none() ? BoundExact : BoundUpper);
-    if (excluded.is_none() && !(root_node && root_restricted_)) {
-        shared_.tt.store(tt_probe, board.key(), score_to_tt(best_score, ply), ss->raw_eval, best_move, depth, bound, ss->tt_pv, board.halfmove_clock());
-    }
-    // Teach the static evaluation about this pawn structure when the search
-    // result is a usable bound in the direction of the difference.
-    if (!in_check && excluded.is_none() && !is_mate_score(best_score) &&
-        (best_move.is_none() || !best_move.is_capture()) &&
-        !(bound == BoundLower && best_score <= ss->static_eval) &&
-        !(bound == BoundUpper && best_score >= ss->static_eval)) {
-        update_correction(board, depth, best_score, ss->static_eval);
-    }
-    return best_score;
-}

+#undef FORGE_SEARCH_CORR_MARGINS
+#undef FORGE_SEARCH_CALL
+#undef FORGE_SEARCH_FN
+#define FORGE_SEARCH_FN search_cm_ce_cu_body
+#define FORGE_SEARCH_CALL search_cm_ce_cu
+#define FORGE_SEARCH_CORR_MARGINS 1
+#define FORGE_SEARCH_CORR_EXT 1
+#include "search/alphabeta.inc"
+#undef FORGE_SEARCH_CORR_EXT
+#undef FORGE_SEARCH_CORR_MARGINS
+#undef FORGE_SEARCH_CALL
+#undef FORGE_SEARCH_FN
+#undef FORGE_LMP_THRESHOLD
+#undef FORGE_QSEARCH_CALL
+#undef FORGE_QSEARCH_FN
+#include "search/cuckoo.inc"      // CuckooRep: upcoming-repetition draw bound
diff --git a/src/search/alphabeta.inc b/src/search/alphabeta.inc
@@ -0,0 +1,465 @@
+// Search module: main alpha-beta search (pruning, extensions, LMR).
+// Compiled as part of src/search.cpp, which includes this file once per search
+// instance (R3 stack: one instance per combination of UCI CorrMargins, CorrExt
+// and CuckooRep, eight in all; qsearch.inc twice):
+//   - FORGE_SEARCH_FN names the function defined here; FORGE_SEARCH_CALL and
+//     FORGE_QSEARCH_CALL name the functions it calls for child nodes and for
+//     quiescence (FORGE_SEARCH_FN itself and Worker::qsearch, except in the
+//     CuckooRep instances, whose calls go through the upcoming-repetition bound
+//     of src/search/cuckoo.inc); FORGE_LMP_THRESHOLD names the late-move pruning
+//     threshold it calls (each single-option instance calls its own copy, see
+//     src/search/corr_margins.inc, corr_ext.inc and cuckoo_lmp.inc);
+//   - FORGE_SEARCH_CORR_MARGINS = 1 adds the correction-size margins of UCI
+//     CorrMargins (forge/corr_margins.hpp) at reverse futility pruning, null-move
+//     pruning, quiet futility pruning and late-move reductions;
+//   - FORGE_SEARCH_CORR_EXT = 1 adds the correction-history extensions of UCI
+//     CorrExt (forge/corr_ext.hpp): the singular-extension decision and the
+//     per-path extension budget.
+// The root (iterative.inc) picks the instance once per iteration, so with the
+// three options off no node tests an option, and Worker::search's source is
+// the AL1 s198 function. A single option on runs the instance of its branch
+// (O5, O8, C6) unchanged.
+#if !defined(FORGE_SEARCH_FN) || !defined(FORGE_SEARCH_CALL) || !defined(FORGE_QSEARCH_CALL) || \
+    !defined(FORGE_LMP_THRESHOLD) || !defined(FORGE_SEARCH_CORR_MARGINS) || !defined(FORGE_SEARCH_CORR_EXT)
+#error "define FORGE_SEARCH_FN, FORGE_SEARCH_CALL, FORGE_QSEARCH_CALL, FORGE_LMP_THRESHOLD, FORGE_SEARCH_CORR_MARGINS and FORGE_SEARCH_CORR_EXT before including search/alphabeta.inc"
+#endif
+
+namespace forge {
+
+int Worker::FORGE_SEARCH_FN(Board& board, SearchStack* ss, int depth, int alpha, int beta, bool cut_node) {
+    bool pv_node = beta - alpha > 1;
+    const bool root_node = ss->ply == 0;
+    const int ply = ss->ply;
+
+    if (depth <= 0) return FORGE_QSEARCH_CALL(board, ss, alpha, beta);
+    // The frame is reused across siblings and same-ply verification searches.
+    // Keep this call's inherited path budget independent of those mutations.
+    const int inherited_double_extensions = (ss - 1)->double_extensions;
+    ss->double_extensions = inherited_double_extensions;
+    if (((local_nodes_ & 1023U) == 0 || node_limit_specified_) && should_stop()) aborted_ = true;
+    if (aborted_) return 0;
+    ++local_nodes_;
+    seldepth_ = std::max(seldepth_, ply);
+    ss->pv_length = 0;
+    ss->move_count = 0;
+
+    bool claim = false;
+    bool claim_raised_alpha = false;
+    if (!root_node) {
+        if (is_draw(board, ply)) return 0;
+        if (ply >= MaxPly) return board.in_check() ? 0 : static_evaluate(board, ss);
+        // Mate distance pruning.
+        alpha = std::max(alpha, mated_in(ply));
+        beta = std::min(beta, mate_in(ply + 1));
+        if (alpha >= beta) return alpha;
+        // UCI IntendedThreefold: the draw score is a floor (see qsearch); no TT
+        // score is used or stored at this node. A window (a, 1) with a < 0 becomes
+        // the null window (0, 1) and is searched as one.
+        claim = options::intended_threefold && board.can_claim_threefold_by_next_move();
+        if (claim) {
+            if (beta <= 0) return 0;
+            claim_raised_alpha = alpha < 0;
+            alpha = std::max(alpha, 0);
+            pv_node = beta - alpha > 1;
+        }
+    }
+
+    const bool in_check = board.in_check();
+    ss->in_check = in_check;
+    const Move excluded = ss->excluded_move;
+    (ss + 1)->excluded_move = Move::none();
+    (ss + 1)->cutoff_count = 0;
+    // Children share killers with their siblings; grandchildren start afresh.
+    (ss + 2)->killers = {};
+
+    // Transposition table probe.
+    const TTProbe tt_probe = shared_.tt.probe(board.key());
+    const bool tt_hit = tt_probe.hit;
+    const TTEntry& tte = tt_probe.entry;
+    const Move tt_move = tt_hit ? tte.move : Move::none();
+    const int tt_score = !claim && tt_hit && tte.score_usable(board.halfmove_clock())
+                             ? score_from_tt(tte.score, ply) : ValueNone;
+    const int tt_depth = tt_hit ? tte.depth : -1;
+    const Bound tt_bound = tt_hit ? tte.bound() : BoundNone;
+    const bool tt_capture = !tt_move.is_none() && tt_move.is_capture();
+    ss->tt_pv = pv_node || (tt_hit && tte.pv());
+
+    if (!pv_node && excluded.is_none() && tt_hit && tt_score != ValueNone && tt_depth >= depth &&
+        board.halfmove_clock() < 90 &&
+        (tt_bound == BoundExact || (tt_bound == BoundLower && tt_score >= beta) ||
+         (tt_bound == BoundUpper && tt_score <= alpha))) {
+        return tt_score;
+    }
+
+    // Tablebase probe (UCI SyzygyPath; cold path, tablebase.inc).
+    if (syzygy::search_cardinality.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != 0) [[unlikely]] {
+        const int tb_score = probe_tablebase(board, ss, depth, alpha, beta, tt_probe);
+        if (tb_score != ValueNone) return tb_score;
+    }
+
+    // Static evaluation.
+    int eval;
+    if (in_check) {
+        eval = ss->static_eval = ss->raw_eval = ValueNone;
+    } else if (!excluded.is_none()) {
+        eval = ss->static_eval;
+    } else {
+        if (tt_hit && tte.eval != ValueNone) ss->raw_eval = tte.eval;
+        else ss->raw_eval = static_evaluate(board, ss);
+        eval = ss->static_eval = corrected_eval(board, ss->raw_eval);
+        if (tt_hit && tt_score != ValueNone &&
+            ((tt_bound == BoundLower && tt_score > eval) || (tt_bound == BoundUpper && tt_score < eval) ||
+             tt_bound == BoundExact)) {
+            eval = tt_score;
+        }
+    }
+#if FORGE_SEARCH_CORR_MARGINS || FORGE_SEARCH_CORR_EXT
+    // CorrMargins / CorrExt: size of the correction-history adjustment at this node (0 in check;
+    // corr_margins::correction_size and corr_ext::correction_size are the same function).
+#if FORGE_SEARCH_CORR_MARGINS
+    const int correction = corr_margins::correction_size(in_check, ss->static_eval, ss->raw_eval);
+#else
+    const int correction = corr_ext::correction_size(in_check, ss->static_eval, ss->raw_eval);
+#endif
+#endif
+
+    bool improving = false;
+    if (!in_check) {
+        if ((ss - 2)->static_eval != ValueNone) improving = ss->static_eval > (ss - 2)->static_eval;
+        else if ((ss - 4)->static_eval != ValueNone) improving = ss->static_eval > (ss - 4)->static_eval;
+        else improving = true;
+    }
+
+    if (!pv_node && !in_check && excluded.is_none() && !is_mate_score(beta)) {
+        // Razoring: hopeless positions fall back to quiescence.
+        if (depth <= tune::RazorDepth && eval + tune::RazorBase + tune::RazorMultiplier * depth < alpha) {
+            const int razor = FORGE_QSEARCH_CALL(board, ss, alpha - 1, alpha);
+            if (aborted_) return 0;
+            if (razor < alpha) return razor;
+        }
+        // Reverse futility pruning.
+#if FORGE_SEARCH_CORR_MARGINS
+        if (depth <= tune::RfpDepth && eval - corr_margins::rfp_margin(depth, improving, correction) >= beta &&
+            eval < MateInMaxPly) {
+            return eval;
+        }
+#else
+        if (depth <= tune::RfpDepth && eval - (tune::RfpMargin * depth - tune::RfpImproving * improving) >= beta &&
+            eval < MateInMaxPly) {
+            return eval;
+        }
+#endif
+        // Null move pruning.
+#if FORGE_SEARCH_CORR_MARGINS
+        if (depth >= tune::NullMoveMinDepth && eval >= beta &&
+            ss->static_eval >= corr_margins::null_move_threshold(beta, depth, correction) &&
+#else
+        if (depth >= tune::NullMoveMinDepth && eval >= beta && ss->static_eval >= beta - 24 * depth + 180 &&
+#endif
+            ss->null_move_allowed && !(ss - 1)->current_move.is_none() &&
+            board.has_non_pawn_material(board.side_to_move()) &&
+            !(tt_hit && tt_bound == BoundUpper && tt_score < beta)) {
+            ++null_tries_;
+            const int reduction = tune::NullMoveBase + depth / tune::NullMoveDepthDivisor +
+                                  std::min((eval - beta) / tune::NullMoveEvalDivisor, 3);
+            ss->current_move = Move::none();
+            ss->moved_piece = Empty;
+            ss->continuation = &(*histories_.continuation_history)[Empty][0];
+            (ss + 1)->ply = ply + 1;
+            record_null_move(ss);
+            NullUndo undo;
+            board.make_null_move(undo);
+#ifdef FORGE_S13_TT_CHILD_PREFETCH
+            if (s13_tt_child_prefetch_) shared_.tt.prefetch(board.key());
+#endif
+#ifdef FORGE_SPEEDSTACK
+            shared_.tt.prefetch(board.key());  // speed stack (S29, piece S13): the child's TT bucket
+#endif
+            int null_score = -FORGE_SEARCH_CALL(board, ss + 1, depth - reduction, -beta, -beta + 1, !cut_node);
+            board.unmake_null_move(undo);
+            if (aborted_) return 0;
+            if (null_score >= beta) {
+                if (is_mate_score(null_score)) null_score = beta;
+                if (depth < 14) {
+                    ++null_cutoffs_;
+                    return null_score;
+                }
+                // Verification search at high depth guards against zugzwang.
+                ss->null_move_allowed = false;
+                const int verification = FORGE_SEARCH_CALL(board, ss, depth - reduction, beta - 1, beta, false);
+                ss->double_extensions = inherited_double_extensions;
+                ss->null_move_allowed = true;
+                if (aborted_) return 0;
+                if (verification >= beta) {
+                    ++null_cutoffs_;
+                    return null_score;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        // ProbCut: a good capture that beats beta by a margin at reduced depth.
+        const int probcut_beta = beta + tune::ProbcutMargin - 50 * improving;
+        if (depth >= tune::ProbcutDepth && !is_mate_score(beta) &&
+            !(tt_hit && tt_depth >= depth - 3 && tt_score != ValueNone && tt_score < probcut_beta)) {
+            MovePicker picker(board, *this, ss, tt_move, MovePicker::Mode::Quiescence);
+            Move move;
+            while (!(move = picker.next(false)).is_none()) {
+                if (move == excluded || !board.is_legal(move)) continue;
+                if (!board.see_ge(move, probcut_beta - ss->static_eval)) continue;
+                ss->current_move = move;
+                ss->moved_piece = board.moved_piece(move);
+                ss->continuation = &(*histories_.continuation_history)[static_cast<std::size_t>(ss->moved_piece)][move.to];
+                (ss + 1)->ply = ply + 1;
+                record_move(ss, board, move);
+                Undo undo;
+                (void)board.make_move(move, undo);
+#ifdef FORGE_S13_TT_CHILD_PREFETCH
+                if (s13_tt_child_prefetch_) shared_.tt.prefetch(board.key());
+#endif
+#ifdef FORGE_SPEEDSTACK
+                shared_.tt.prefetch(board.key());  // speed stack (S29, piece S13): the child's TT bucket
+#endif
+                int value = -FORGE_QSEARCH_CALL(board, ss + 1, -probcut_beta, -probcut_beta + 1);
+                if (value >= probcut_beta && !aborted_) {
+                    value = -FORGE_SEARCH_CALL(board, ss + 1, depth - 4, -probcut_beta, -probcut_beta + 1, !cut_node);
+                }
+                board.unmake_move(move, undo);
+                if (aborted_) return 0;
+                if (value >= probcut_beta) {
+                    if (!claim) {
+                        shared_.tt.store(tt_probe, board.key(), score_to_tt(value, ply), ss->raw_eval, move, depth - 3, BoundLower, ss->tt_pv, board.halfmove_clock());
+                    }
+                    return value;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Internal iterative reduction: no hash move suggests a less important node.
+    if (!in_check && depth >= tune::IirDepth && tt_move.is_none() && (pv_node || cut_node)) --depth;
+
+    // HistRework: lesser-attacker maps of this node, read by quiet_history and the history updates below.
+    if (search_options::hist_rework != 0) [[unlikely]] threat_maps_[static_cast<std::size_t>(ply)] = hist_rework::lesser_threats(board);
+    MovePicker picker(board, *this, ss, tt_move, MovePicker::Mode::Main);
+    std::array<Move, 128> quiets_tried{};
+    std::array<Move, 64> captures_tried{};
+    int quiet_count = 0;
+    int capture_count = 0;
+    int best_score = -Infinite;
+    Move best_move = Move::none();
+    int move_count = 0;
+    bool skip_quiets = false;
+    const bool has_material = board.has_non_pawn_material(board.side_to_move());
+
+    Move move;
+    while (!(move = picker.next(skip_quiets)).is_none()) {
+        if (move == excluded) continue;
+        if (root_node && root_restricted_ &&
+            std::none_of(root_moves_.begin(), root_moves_.end(), [&](const Move& m) { return m == move; })) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        if (!board.is_legal(move)) continue;
+        ++move_count;
+        ss->move_count = move_count;
+        const bool capture = move.is_capture();
+        const bool quiet = !capture && !move.is_promotion();
+        const bool gives_check = board.gives_check(move);
+        const Piece moved = board.moved_piece(move);
+        const int history = quiet ? quiet_history(board, ss, move) : capture_history_value(board, move);
+
+        // Shallow-depth pruning of unpromising moves.
+        if (!root_node && best_score > -MateInMaxPly && has_material) {
+            if (quiet && !skip_quiets && move_count >= FORGE_LMP_THRESHOLD(depth, improving)) skip_quiets = true;
+            const int lmr_depth = std::max(0, depth - reduction(quiet, depth, move_count));
+            if (quiet && !gives_check) {
+#if FORGE_SEARCH_CORR_MARGINS
+                if (!in_check && lmr_depth <= tune::FutilityDepth &&
+                    ss->static_eval + corr_margins::futility_margin(lmr_depth, correction) <= alpha) {
+#else
+                if (!in_check && lmr_depth <= tune::FutilityDepth &&
+                    ss->static_eval + tune::FutilityBase + tune::FutilityMultiplier * lmr_depth <= alpha) {
+#endif
+                    skip_quiets = true;
+                    continue;
+                }
+                if (lmr_depth <= tune::HistoryPruneDepth && history < -tune::HistoryPruneMultiplier * depth) continue;
+                if (!board.see_ge(move, -tune::SeeQuietMultiplier * lmr_depth * lmr_depth)) continue;
+            } else {
+                // Futility pruning for captures: even winning the piece cannot
+                // bring the position back to alpha.
+                if (tune::CaptureFutilityBase > 0 && capture && !in_check && !gives_check &&
+                    lmr_depth <= tune::CaptureFutilityDepth &&
+                    ss->static_eval + tune::CaptureFutilityBase + tune::CaptureFutilityMultiplier * lmr_depth +
+                            capture_value(board, move) + history / 8 <= alpha) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                const int see_threshold = -tune::SeeNoisyMultiplier * depth;
+                if (!(picker.last_move_see_nonnegative() && see_threshold <= 0) &&
+                    !board.see_ge(move, see_threshold)) continue;
+            }
+        }
+
+        // Extensions.
+        int extension = 0;
+        if (!root_node && depth >= tune::SingularDepth && move == tt_move && excluded.is_none() && tt_bound != BoundUpper &&
+            tt_score != ValueNone && !is_mate_score(tt_score) && tt_depth >= depth - 3) {
+            const int singular_beta = tt_score - tune::SingularMargin * depth;
+            const int singular_depth = (depth - 1) / 2;
+            const bool saved_tt_pv = ss->tt_pv;
+            ss->excluded_move = move;
+            const int singular_score = FORGE_SEARCH_CALL(board, ss, singular_depth, singular_beta - 1, singular_beta, cut_node);
+            ss->tt_pv = saved_tt_pv;
+            ss->double_extensions = inherited_double_extensions;
+            ss->excluded_move = Move::none();
+            if (aborted_) return 0;
+            if (singular_score < singular_beta) {
+#if FORGE_SEARCH_CORR_EXT
+                // CorrExt: double margin shrinking with |correction|, triple extension, per-path budget.
+                extension = corr_ext::singular_extension(pv_node, singular_score, singular_beta, correction,
+                                                         inherited_double_extensions);
+#else
+                extension = 1;
+                if (!pv_node && singular_score < singular_beta - 24 && inherited_double_extensions <= 6) {
+                    extension = 2;
+                }
+#endif
+            } else if (singular_beta >= beta) {
+                return singular_beta;
+            } else if (tt_score >= beta) {
+                extension = -2;
+            } else if (cut_node) {
+                extension = -1;
+            }
+        } else if (gives_check && depth >= tune::CheckExtensionDepth && !root_node &&
+                   (picker.last_move_see_nonnegative() || board.see_ge(move, 0))) {
+            extension = 1;
+        }
+#if FORGE_SEARCH_CORR_EXT
+        // CorrExt: the path pays extension - 1 plies for a double or triple extension.
+        ss->double_extensions = corr_ext::path_extra_after(inherited_double_extensions, extension);
+#else
+        ss->double_extensions = inherited_double_extensions + (extension == 2);
+#endif
+        const int new_depth = depth - 1 + extension;
+
+        ss->current_move = move;
+        ss->moved_piece = moved;
+        ss->continuation = &(*histories_.continuation_history)[static_cast<std::size_t>(moved)][move.to];
+        (ss + 1)->ply = ply + 1;
+        record_move(ss, board, move);
+        Undo undo;
+        (void)board.make_move(move, undo);
+        shared_.tt.prefetch(board.key());
+        const std::uint64_t nodes_before = local_nodes_;
+        std::size_t root_effort_index = 0;
+        if (root_node) {
+            const auto root_effort_end = root_effort_moves_.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(root_effort_count_);
+            root_effort_index = static_cast<std::size_t>(std::find(root_effort_moves_.begin(), root_effort_end, move) - root_effort_moves_.begin());
+            assert(root_effort_index < root_effort_count_);
+        }
+
+        int score = -Infinite;
+        bool full_depth_needed = !pv_node || move_count > 1;
+        if (depth >= 2 && move_count > 1 + (root_node ? 1 : 0) && (quiet || !ss->tt_pv || move_count > 4)) {
+            int r = reduction(quiet, depth, move_count);
+            if (ss->tt_pv) r -= 1;
+            if (!improving) r += 1;
+            if (cut_node) r += 2;
+            if (tt_capture && quiet) r += 1;
+            if (gives_check) r -= 1;
+            if (in_check) r -= 1;
+            if (tune::LmrCutoffThreshold > 0 && (ss + 1)->cutoff_count > tune::LmrCutoffThreshold) r += 1;
+            if (quiet) r -= std::clamp(history / tune::LmrHistoryDivisor, -2, 2);
+#if FORGE_SEARCH_CORR_MARGINS
+            r = corr_margins::lmr_reduction(r, correction);
+#endif
+            const int reduced_depth = std::clamp(new_depth - r, 1, new_depth);
+            score = -FORGE_SEARCH_CALL(board, ss + 1, reduced_depth, -alpha - 1, -alpha, true);
+            full_depth_needed = score > alpha && reduced_depth < new_depth;
+            if (full_depth_needed) {
+                score = -FORGE_SEARCH_CALL(board, ss + 1, new_depth, -alpha - 1, -alpha, !cut_node);
+                full_depth_needed = false;
+            }
+            if (!aborted_ && quiet && score > alpha) {
+                // A late quiet move that beat expectations deserves a history boost.
+                update_continuation(ss, moved, move.to, history_bonus(depth) / 2);
+            }
+        }
+        if (full_depth_needed && !aborted_) {
+            score = -FORGE_SEARCH_CALL(board, ss + 1, new_depth, -alpha - 1, -alpha, !cut_node);
+        }
+        if (pv_node && !aborted_ && (move_count == 1 || score > alpha)) {
+            score = -FORGE_SEARCH_CALL(board, ss + 1, new_depth, -beta, -alpha, false);
+        }
+        board.unmake_move(move, undo);
+        if (aborted_) return 0;
+        if (root_node && root_effort_index < root_effort_count_)
+            root_move_nodes_[root_effort_index] += local_nodes_ - nodes_before;
+
+        if (score > best_score) {
+            best_score = score;
+            if (score > alpha) {
+                best_move = move;
+                if (pv_node) {
+                    ss->pv[0] = move;
+                    std::copy_n((ss + 1)->pv.begin(), (ss + 1)->pv_length, ss->pv.begin() + 1);
+                    ss->pv_length = (ss + 1)->pv_length + 1;
+                }
+                if (score >= beta) {
+                    ++ss->cutoff_count;
+                    break;
+                }
+                alpha = score;
+            }
+        }
+        if (quiet && quiet_count < static_cast<int>(quiets_tried.size())) quiets_tried[static_cast<std::size_t>(quiet_count++)] = move;
+        else if (!quiet && capture_count < static_cast<int>(captures_tried.size())) captures_tried[static_cast<std::size_t>(capture_count++)] = move;
+    }
+
+    if (move_count == 0) {
+        if (!excluded.is_none()) return alpha;
+        if (root_node && root_restricted_) return 0;
+        return in_check ? mated_in(ply) : 0;
+    }
+    if (claim && best_score < 0) best_score = 0;
+
+    if (best_score >= beta) {
+        if (!best_move.is_capture() && !best_move.is_promotion()) {
+            update_quiet_stats(board, ss, best_move, depth + (best_score > beta + 100), quiets_tried.data(), quiet_count);
+        }
+        update_capture_stats(board, best_move, depth, captures_tried.data(), capture_count);
+    } else if (!best_move.is_none() && (!best_move.is_capture() && !best_move.is_promotion())) {
+        // Even without a cutoff, a new PV move earned its place.
+        apply_history((*histories_.main_history)[static_cast<std::size_t>(board.side_to_move())][best_move.from][best_move.to],
+                      history_bonus(depth) / 4);
+        if (search_options::hist_rework != 0) [[unlikely]]
+            apply_history(threat_history_entry(board, ss, best_move), history_bonus(depth) / 4);
+    } else if (tune::FailLowContinuationBonus > 0 && best_move.is_none() && !claim_raised_alpha &&
+               !(ss - 1)->current_move.is_none() && (ss - 1)->moved_piece != Empty &&
+               !(ss - 1)->current_move.is_capture() && ply > 0) {
+        // Every move failed low: the parent's quiet move refuted this line. Not when
+        // the claim floor raised alpha: the value 0 then lies inside the original window.
+        update_continuation(ss - 1, (ss - 1)->moved_piece, (ss - 1)->current_move.to,
+                            history_bonus(depth) * tune::FailLowContinuationBonus / 100);
+    }
+
+    const Bound bound = best_score >= beta ? BoundLower : (pv_node && !best_move.is_none() ? BoundExact : BoundUpper);
+    if (excluded.is_none() && !(root_node && root_restricted_) && !claim) {
+        shared_.tt.store(tt_probe, board.key(), score_to_tt(best_score, ply), ss->raw_eval, best_move, depth, bound, ss->tt_pv, board.halfmove_clock());
+    } else if (claim && excluded.is_none() && (!tt_hit || tte.score == ValueNone)) {
+        // Claim node: move and static evaluation only, raw score ValueNone (see qsearch).
+        shared_.tt.store(tt_probe, board.key(), ValueNone, ss->raw_eval, best_move, 0, BoundUpper, ss->tt_pv,
+                         static_cast<std::uint16_t>(board.halfmove_clock()));
+    }
+    // Teach the static evaluation about this pawn structure when the search
+    // result is a usable bound in the direction of the difference (a claim floor
+    // says nothing about the structure).
+    if (!in_check && excluded.is_none() && !claim && !is_mate_score(best_score) &&
+        (best_move.is_none() || !best_move.is_capture()) &&
+        !(bound == BoundLower && best_score <= ss->static_eval) &&
+        !(bound == BoundUpper && best_score >= ss->static_eval)) {
+        update_correction(board, depth, best_score, ss->static_eval);
+    }
+    return best_score;
+}
+
+} // namespace forge
diff --git a/src/search/checks.inc b/src/search/checks.inc
@@ -0,0 +1,54 @@
+// Search module: per-node checks — the clock (ponder-aware), node totals over
+// all workers, the hard stop test (stop flag, node limit, hard time limit) and
+// draw detection.
+// Compiled as part of src/search.cpp (included exactly once).
+
+namespace forge {
+
+std::int64_t Worker::elapsed_ms() const {
+    auto origin = start_;
+    if (pondering_) {
+        const std::int64_t hit_ns = shared_.ponder_hit_ns.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
+        if (hit_ns == 0) return 0;
+        origin = std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point(
+            std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::steady_clock::duration>(std::chrono::nanoseconds(hit_ns)));
+    }
+    return std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - origin).count();
+}
+
+std::uint64_t Worker::total_nodes() const {
+    std::uint64_t total = local_nodes_;
+    if (all_workers_ != nullptr) {
+        for (const Worker* worker : *all_workers_) {
+            if (worker != this) total += worker->nodes();
+        }
+    }
+    return total;
+}
+
+bool Worker::should_stop() {
+    // Publish this thread's node count for the others at the same cadence.
+    nodes_.store(local_nodes_, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+    if (shared_.stop.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) return true;
+    if (!is_main()) return false;
+    if (node_limit_specified_ && total_nodes() >= node_limit_) return true;
+    if (hard_limit_ms_ > 0) {
+        if (pondering_ && shared_.ponder_hit_ns.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == 0) return false;
+        if (elapsed_ms() >= hard_limit_ms_) return true;
+    }
+    return false;
+}
+
+bool Worker::is_draw(const Board& board, int ply) const {
+    (void)ply;
+    if (board.halfmove_clock() >= 100) {
+        // Checkmate on the hundredth half-move still wins; everything else is a draw.
+        return !board.in_check() || board.has_legal_move();
+    }
+    // Detect stalemate before TT cutoffs, stand pat or forward pruning can
+    // replace its terminal draw score with a material evaluation.
+    return board.is_repetition(root_history_index_) || board.is_insufficient_material() ||
+           (!board.in_check() && !board.has_legal_move());
+}
+
+} // namespace forge

+// The late-move pruning threshold of tune.inc, as a copy for the CorrExt

+// pass, and that alone changes the code layout of the default search.
