diff --git a/src/search/correction_plus.inc b/src/search/correction_plus.inc
@@ -0,0 +1,145 @@
+// Search module: CorrHistPlus, the extra correction-history keys
+// (forge/correction_plus.hpp, UCI option CorrHistPlus, default 0 = off).
+// Compiled as part of src/search.cpp (included exactly once, after
+// evaluation.inc).
+//
+// How the default build stays the AL1 s198 build: Worker::corrected_eval and
+// Worker::update_correction (evaluation.inc) only gained one branch at their
+// top, `if (options::corr_hist_plus != 0) [[unlikely]] return <twin>(...)`,
+// which jumps with the arguments untouched to the cold twins below. Each twin
+// repeats the AL1 s198 statements of its hot counterpart and adds the extra
+// keys. The twins are [[gnu::cold]], so GCC places them in .text.unlikely,
+// away from the hot .text. corrected_eval_plus is also a leaf (all helpers
+// are always_inline): GCC's interprocedural register allocation lets the
+// callers of corrected_eval (search, qsearch) keep values in registers that
+// corrected_eval leaves alone, so a twin that called other functions would
+// clobber them and change the callers' code.
+
+namespace forge {
+
+// Frame of the node whose evaluation is being corrected. corrected_eval and
+// update_correction run on the search board of that node, every make_move /
+// make_null_move between the root and the node pushed one key onto the board
+// history, the root frame is stack_[4] and the child of frame ss is ss + 1
+// (iterative.inc, alphabeta.inc, qsearch.inc), so the board history gives the
+// frame without passing it through the hot callers. The ply recorded in the
+// frame confirms it; anything else (e.g. a probe outside a search) gets none.
+inline const SearchStack* Worker::correction_frame(const Board& board) const {
+    const std::size_t history = board.history_size();
+    if (history <= root_history_index_) return nullptr;
+    const std::size_t ply = history - 1 - root_history_index_;
+    if (ply > static_cast<std::size_t>(MaxPly)) return nullptr;
+    const SearchStack* ss = &stack_[4 + ply];
+    return ss->ply == static_cast<int>(ply) ? ss : nullptr;
+}
+
+// Continuation-table slot of the node (forge::corrplus::continuation_index), or
+// ContinuationSize when the previous two moves are not both known: next to the
+// root (the sentinel frames hold no move) or after a null move.
+inline std::size_t Worker::continuation_correction_index(const Board& board) const {
+    const SearchStack* ss = correction_frame(board);
+    if (ss == nullptr) return corrplus::ContinuationSize;
+    const SearchStack* theirs = ss - 1;  // the move that led to this node
+    const SearchStack* ours = ss - 2;    // our move before it
+    const Color side = board.side_to_move();
+    if (theirs->current_move.is_none() || ours->current_move.is_none() ||
+        theirs->moved_piece == Empty || ours->moved_piece == Empty ||
+        color_of(ours->moved_piece) != side || color_of(theirs->moved_piece) == side) {
+        return corrplus::ContinuationSize;
+    }
+    // The colours are fixed by the side to move (checked above): the piece type
+    // is the piece minus its colour offset (no modulo in this size-optimised code).
+    const int ours_offset = side == White ? 0 : 6;
+    return corrplus::continuation_index(side, static_cast<int>(ours->moved_piece) - ours_offset, ours->current_move.to,
+                                        static_cast<int>(theirs->moved_piece) - (6 - ours_offset), theirs->current_move.to);
+}
+
+// Correction of the enabled extra keys in CorrectionGrain units (weighted,
+// clamped by corrplus::blend); added to the pawn + material correction.
+inline int Worker::correction_plus(const Board& board) const {
+    const corrplus::Tables* tables = correction_plus_.get();
+    if (tables == nullptr) return 0;  // nothing learned yet: the first update allocates the tables
+    const int keys = options::corr_hist_plus;
+    const std::size_t side = static_cast<std::size_t>(board.side_to_move());
+    int sum = 0;
+    if ((keys & corrplus::Continuation) != 0) {
+        const std::size_t index = continuation_correction_index(board);
+        if (index < corrplus::ContinuationSize) sum += tables->continuation[index] * tune::CorrContWeight;
+    }
+    if ((keys & corrplus::MinorPiece) != 0) {
+        sum += tables->minor[side][corrplus::key_index(corrplus::minor_piece_key(board))] * tune::CorrMinorWeight;
+    }
+    if ((keys & corrplus::NonPawn) != 0) {
+        const int white = tables->non_pawn[White][side][corrplus::key_index(corrplus::non_pawn_key(board, White))];
+        const int black = tables->non_pawn[Black][side][corrplus::key_index(corrplus::non_pawn_key(board, Black))];
+        sum += (white + black) * tune::CorrNonPawnWeight;
+    }
+    return corrplus::blend(sum);
+}
+
+// corrected_eval with CorrHistPlus on: the AL1 s198 statements of
+// corrected_eval (pawn + material correction; the divisions written as
+// corrplus::div100 / div_pow2, the same values) plus the extra keys.
+int Worker::corrected_eval_plus(const Board& board, int raw_eval) const {
+    const std::size_t side = static_cast<std::size_t>(board.side_to_move());
+    std::int32_t correction = (*correction_history_)[side][board.pawn_key() & (CorrectionSize - 1U)];
+    if (tune::MaterialCorrectionWeight > 0) {
+        correction += corrplus::div100((*material_correction_)[side][material_key(board) & (CorrectionSize - 1U)] * tune::MaterialCorrectionWeight);
+    }
+    // R3 stack: HistRework's joint term as in corrected_eval, so HistRework and CorrHistPlus combine.
+    if (const int level = search_options::hist_rework; level != 0) [[unlikely]] {
+        // HistRework: pawn structure x non-pawn material. |entry| <= 8192, weight <= 100: |term| <= 8192.
+        correction += (*joint_correction_)[side][hist_rework::joint_key(board) & (CorrectionSize - 1U)] *
+                      hist_rework::joint_weight(level) / 100;
+    }
+    correction += correction_plus(board);
+    return std::clamp(raw_eval + corrplus::div_pow2<8>(correction), -MateInMaxPly + 1, MateInMaxPly - 1);
+}
+
+// update_correction with CorrHistPlus on: the AL1 s198 statements of
+// update_correction (pawn + material tables; / 256 written as div_pow2<8>,
+// the same value) plus the extra keys. The search
+// calls update_correction under one rule for all tables: not in check, no
+// excluded move, no mate score, best move quiet (or none), and the bound on the
+// side of the difference (alphabeta.inc). The extra entries take the same
+// residual and depth weight (corrplus::step) with a decay (corrplus::apply).
+void Worker::update_correction_plus(const Board& board, int depth, int best_score, int static_eval) {
+    const std::size_t side = static_cast<std::size_t>(board.side_to_move());
+    const int diff = std::clamp((best_score - static_eval) * CorrectionGrain, -CorrectionLimit / 4, CorrectionLimit / 4);
+    const int weight = std::min(depth + 1, 16);
+    auto apply = [&](std::int32_t& entry) {
+        entry = std::clamp(entry + corrplus::div_pow2<8>(diff * weight), -CorrectionLimit, CorrectionLimit);
+    };
+    apply((*correction_history_)[side][board.pawn_key() & (CorrectionSize - 1U)]);
+    if (tune::MaterialCorrectionWeight > 0) apply((*material_correction_)[side][material_key(board) & (CorrectionSize - 1U)]);
+    // R3 stack: HistRework's joint update as in update_correction, so HistRework and CorrHistPlus combine.
+    if (search_options::hist_rework != 0) [[unlikely]] {
+        // HistRework: the joint table learns the same residual step (|step| <= 2048 * 16 / 256 = 128) with a
+        // gravity-style damping instead of the lock's add-and-clamp; the lock tables above are unchanged.
+        std::int32_t& entry = (*joint_correction_)[side][hist_rework::joint_key(board) & (CorrectionSize - 1U)];
+        entry = hist_rework::gravity(entry, diff * weight / 256, CorrectionLimit);
+    }
+
+    if (!correction_plus_) {
+        // Value-initialised (all entries 0). On allocation failure the extra keys stay unused.
+        correction_plus_.reset(new (std::nothrow) corrplus::Tables());
+        if (!correction_plus_) return;
+    }
+    corrplus::Tables& tables = *correction_plus_;
+    const int keys = options::corr_hist_plus;
+    const int step = corrplus::step(best_score, static_eval, depth);
+    auto learn = [step](corrplus::Entry& entry) { entry = static_cast<corrplus::Entry>(corrplus::apply(entry, step)); };
+    if ((keys & corrplus::Continuation) != 0) {
+        const std::size_t index = continuation_correction_index(board);
+        if (index < corrplus::ContinuationSize) learn(tables.continuation[index]);
+    }
+    if ((keys & corrplus::MinorPiece) != 0) {
+        learn(tables.minor[side][corrplus::key_index(corrplus::minor_piece_key(board))]);
+    }
+    if ((keys & corrplus::NonPawn) != 0) {
+        learn(tables.non_pawn[White][side][corrplus::key_index(corrplus::non_pawn_key(board, White))]);
+        learn(tables.non_pawn[Black][side][corrplus::key_index(corrplus::non_pawn_key(board, Black))]);
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace forge
diff --git a/src/search/cuckoo.inc b/src/search/cuckoo.inc
@@ -0,0 +1,56 @@
+// Search module: the upcoming-repetition draw bound (UCI CuckooRep, default off).
+// Compiled as part of src/search.cpp (included exactly once), after the
+// CuckooRep instances of the quiescence and main search (qsearch_cuckoo_body,
+// search_cuckoo_body and, R3 stack, search_{cm,ce,cm_ce}_cu_body), whose
+// quiescence and child calls come here.
+//
+// At a node whose side to move has a legal reversible move into a position the
+// search scores as a repetition draw (Board::upcoming_repetition: an earlier
+// occurrence inside the rule-50 and null-move window, at or after the root, or
+// two before it), the draw score is a lower bound. With alpha below it the node
+// fails high at once when beta is at or below the draw score; otherwise it is
+// searched with alpha raised to the draw score and its result is kept at or
+// above the draw score (a fail-low there, e.g. because pruning skipped the
+// drawing move, still proves no less than the draw). Nodes with an excluded
+// move (singular verification) are left alone: the drawing move could be the
+// excluded one. The root never gets the bound. Depth-0 nodes are handed to
+// quiescence unchanged and get the bound there (one detector call per node).
+
+namespace forge {
+
+namespace {
+// Board::upcoming_repetition() is false below a halfmove clock of 3 (no earlier
+// position at distance 3 inside the window); testing that inline first skips the
+// call at the many nodes just after a capture or pawn move.
+[[nodiscard]] inline bool may_repeat(const Board& board) { return board.halfmove_clock() >= 3; }
+} // namespace
+
+// One wrapper per CuckooRep instance of the main search (R3 stack: CuckooRep
+// alone, as on opus-C6, and with CorrMargins / CorrExt); BODY is the instance.
+#define FORGE_CUCKOO_SEARCH_WRAPPER(WRAPPER, BODY)                                                              \
+    int Worker::WRAPPER(Board& board, SearchStack* ss, int depth, int alpha, int beta, bool cut_node) {          \
+        constexpr int DrawScore = 0;                                                                             \
+        if (depth > 0 && alpha < DrawScore && ss->ply != 0 && ss->excluded_move.is_none() && may_repeat(board) && \
+            board.upcoming_repetition(root_history_index_)) {                                                    \
+            if (beta <= DrawScore) return DrawScore;                                                             \
+            return std::max(BODY(board, ss, depth, DrawScore, beta, cut_node), DrawScore);                      \
+        }                                                                                                        \
+        return BODY(board, ss, depth, alpha, beta, cut_node);                                                   \
+    }
+
+FORGE_CUCKOO_SEARCH_WRAPPER(search_cuckoo, search_cuckoo_body)
+FORGE_CUCKOO_SEARCH_WRAPPER(search_cm_cu, search_cm_cu_body)
+FORGE_CUCKOO_SEARCH_WRAPPER(search_ce_cu, search_ce_cu_body)
+FORGE_CUCKOO_SEARCH_WRAPPER(search_cm_ce_cu, search_cm_ce_cu_body)
+#undef FORGE_CUCKOO_SEARCH_WRAPPER
+
+int Worker::qsearch_cuckoo(Board& board, SearchStack* ss, int alpha, int beta) {
+    constexpr int DrawScore = 0;
+    if (alpha < DrawScore && may_repeat(board) && board.upcoming_repetition(root_history_index_)) {
+        if (beta <= DrawScore) return DrawScore;
+        return std::max(qsearch_cuckoo_body(board, ss, DrawScore, beta), DrawScore);
+    }
+    return qsearch_cuckoo_body(board, ss, alpha, beta);
+}
+
+} // namespace forge
diff --git a/src/search/cuckoo_lmp.inc b/src/search/cuckoo_lmp.inc
@@ -0,0 +1,20 @@
+// Search module: the late-move pruning threshold of tune.inc, as a copy for the
+// CuckooRep instance of the main search (UCI CuckooRep). Compiled as part of
+// src/search.cpp (included exactly once), right before the second inclusion of
+// alphabeta.inc. The AL1 s198 lmp_threshold() keeps its single call site in the
+// default Worker::search: with a second caller GCC inlines it in a later pass,
+// which shifts the SSA numbering of the default search, swaps the operands of
+// one comparison and changes its block layout (found in job O5,
+// opus_round1/O5/logs/codegen_second_instance_investigation.log on opus-O5).
+
+namespace forge {
+
+namespace {
+
+[[nodiscard]] int lmp_threshold_cuckoo(int depth, bool improving) {
+    return improving ? tune::LmpBase + depth * depth : (tune::LmpBase + depth * depth) / 2;
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
+} // namespace forge
diff --git a/src/search/evaluation.inc b/src/search/evaluation.inc
@@ -0,0 +1,127 @@
+// Search module: static evaluation glue (lazy NNUE accumulators, residual
+// HCE + network) and pawn/material correction history.
+// Compiled as part of src/search.cpp (included exactly once).
+
+// Padding bytes in a cold branch (see Worker::static_evaluate). Only for the
+// MinGW g++ build whose layout was measured; elsewhere a no-op.
+#if defined(__MINGW32__) && defined(__GNUC__) && !defined(__clang__) && defined(__x86_64__)
+#define FORGE_LAYOUT_PAD(bytes) asm volatile(".nops " #bytes)
+#else
+#define FORGE_LAYOUT_PAD(bytes) ((void)0)
+#endif
+
+namespace forge {
+
+namespace {
+// Hash of the piece counts of both sides, for material-indexed statistics.
+// Always inlined: it also has a call site in the cold CorrHistPlus path, and an
+// out-of-line copy would be emitted at the start of the hot .text.
+[[gnu::always_inline]] [[nodiscard]] inline std::uint64_t material_key(const Board& board) {
+#ifdef FORGE_SPEEDSTACK
+    // Speed stack (S29, piece S11): the same 64-bit key as the polynomial below, from a contribution table.
+    return matkey::table_key(board);
+#else
+    // S11 MaterialKeyTable: the same key from a contribution table (forge/material_key_table.hpp).
+    if (options::material_key_table) [[unlikely]] return matkey::table_key(board);
+    std::uint64_t key = 0;
+    for (const Color color : {White, Black}) {
+        for (int type = Pawn; type <= Queen; ++type) {
+            key = key * 1099511628211ULL + static_cast<std::uint64_t>(popcount(board.pieces(color, static_cast<PieceType>(type))) + 1);
+        }
+    }
+    return key * 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15ULL;
+#endif
+}
+} // namespace
+
+int Worker::static_evaluate(const Board& board, SearchStack* ss) {
+    if (options::nnue_v2) [[unlikely]] {
+        // Layout pad: with it the dispatch grows this function by exactly 64
+        // bytes in the PC build (MinGW g++ 15.2), so the search functions
+        // emitted after it keep their AL1 s198 addresses mod 64.
+        FORGE_LAYOUT_PAD(32);
+        return static_evaluate_v2(board, ss);
+    }
+    if (!nnue::enabled || !nnue::available()) return evaluator_.evaluate(board);
+    const bool residual = nnue::residual();
+    if (!ss->accumulator.valid) {
+        // Walk back to the nearest valid accumulator, then replay the moves.
+        int back = 0;
+        while (back < ss->ply && !(ss - back - 1)->accumulator.valid) ++back;
+        if (back >= ss->ply && !(ss - back - 1)->accumulator.valid) {
+            nnue::refresh(board, ss->accumulator);
+            if (residual) ss->runtime_psqt = Evaluator::refresh_runtime_psqt(board);
+        } else {
+            for (int i = back; i >= 0; --i) {
+                nnue::update((ss - i - 1)->accumulator, (ss - i)->accumulator, (ss - i)->dirty);
+                if (residual) Evaluator::update_runtime_psqt((ss - i - 1)->runtime_psqt, (ss - i)->runtime_psqt, (ss - i)->dirty);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    const int network = nnue::evaluate(board, ss->accumulator);
+    // S41 NetScale (default 100 = OFF, forge/net_scale.hpp): net_scale::apply is the identity at 100.
+    // (R3 stack: the compiled-in network only, as on opus-sol-S41; v2 nets are not scaled.)
+    const int scale = net_scale::percent;
+    if (!residual) {
+        if (scale == net_scale::Default) [[likely]] return network;
+        return std::clamp(net_scale::apply(network, scale), -MateInMaxPly + 1, MateInMaxPly - 1);
+    }
+    if (!ss->runtime_psqt.valid) ss->runtime_psqt = Evaluator::refresh_runtime_psqt(board);
+    // A residual network is a learned correction on top of the hand-crafted evaluation.
+    return std::clamp(net_scale::apply(evaluator_.evaluate_runtime(board, ss->runtime_psqt) + network, scale),
+                      -MateInMaxPly + 1, MateInMaxPly - 1);
+}
+
+void Worker::record_move(SearchStack* ss, const Board& board, const Move& move) {
+    // Called before make_move: the child's accumulator becomes stale and the
+    // piece changes are remembered for the lazy update.
+    if (!nnue::enabled || !nnue::available()) return;
+    (ss + 1)->dirty = nnue::dirty_pieces(board, move);
+    (ss + 1)->accumulator.valid = false;
+    (ss + 1)->runtime_psqt.valid = false;
+}
+
+void Worker::record_null_move(SearchStack* ss) {
+    (ss + 1)->dirty.count = 0;
+    (ss + 1)->accumulator.valid = false;
+    (ss + 1)->runtime_psqt.valid = false;
+}
+
+int Worker::corrected_eval(const Board& board, int raw_eval) const {
+    // CorrHistPlus (default 0 = off): one predictable branch; the cold path
+    // computes this correction plus the extra keys (correction_plus.inc).
+    if (options::corr_hist_plus != 0) [[unlikely]] return corrected_eval_plus(board, raw_eval);
+    const std::size_t side = static_cast<std::size_t>(board.side_to_move());
+    std::int32_t correction = (*correction_history_)[side][board.pawn_key() & (CorrectionSize - 1U)];
+    if (tune::MaterialCorrectionWeight > 0) {
+        correction += (*material_correction_)[side][material_key(board) & (CorrectionSize - 1U)] * tune::MaterialCorrectionWeight / 100;
+    }
+    if (const int level = search_options::hist_rework; level != 0) [[unlikely]] {
+        // HistRework: pawn structure x non-pawn material. |entry| <= 8192, weight <= 100: |term| <= 8192.
+        correction += (*joint_correction_)[side][hist_rework::joint_key(board) & (CorrectionSize - 1U)] *
+                      hist_rework::joint_weight(level) / 100;
+    }
+    return std::clamp(raw_eval + correction / CorrectionGrain, -MateInMaxPly + 1, MateInMaxPly - 1);
+}
+
+void Worker::update_correction(const Board& board, int depth, int best_score, int static_eval) {
+    // CorrHistPlus (default 0 = off): one predictable branch; the cold path
+    // performs this update plus the extra keys (correction_plus.inc).
+    if (options::corr_hist_plus != 0) [[unlikely]] return update_correction_plus(board, depth, best_score, static_eval);
+    const std::size_t side = static_cast<std::size_t>(board.side_to_move());
+    const int diff = std::clamp((best_score - static_eval) * CorrectionGrain, -CorrectionLimit / 4, CorrectionLimit / 4);
+    const int weight = std::min(depth + 1, 16);
+    auto apply = [&](std::int32_t& entry) {
+        entry = std::clamp(entry + diff * weight / 256, -CorrectionLimit, CorrectionLimit);
+    };
+    apply((*correction_history_)[side][board.pawn_key() & (CorrectionSize - 1U)]);
+    if (tune::MaterialCorrectionWeight > 0) apply((*material_correction_)[side][material_key(board) & (CorrectionSize - 1U)]);
+    if (search_options::hist_rework != 0) [[unlikely]] {
+        // HistRework: the joint table learns the same residual step (|step| <= 2048 * 16 / 256 = 128) with a
+        // gravity-style damping instead of the lock's add-and-clamp; the lock tables above are unchanged.
+        std::int32_t& entry = (*joint_correction_)[side][hist_rework::joint_key(board) & (CorrectionSize - 1U)];
+        entry = hist_rework::gravity(entry, diff * weight / 256, CorrectionLimit);
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace forge
diff --git a/src/search/evaluation_extra.inc b/src/search/evaluation_extra.inc
@@ -0,0 +1,127 @@
+// Search module: static evaluation with a v2 network that has extra input blocks: threat inputs (O6; UCI
+// NNUEThreats) and / or pawn-pair inputs (O7; UCI NNUEPawnPairs), both OFF by default; the feature definitions are
+// in forge/nnue_v2.hpp at ThreatFeatures and PawnPairFeatures. Compiled as part of src/search.cpp (included exactly
+// once, before evaluation_v2.inc, whose static_evaluate_v2 calls it: GCC emits a caller after its callees). The
+// function lives in section .text.forge_v2 like the rest of the v2 code, so the default build's .text is unchanged.
+//
+// Reached only through static_evaluate_v2 when the active net has an extra block. Accumulators follow C4's lazy
+// scheme: an invalid ply walks back to the nearest valid ancestor and replays the recorded piece changes; every
+// replayed ply becomes valid. Per replayed ply, the child's piece placement is the parent's with that ply's piece
+// changes applied (starting from the board with every replayed ply taken back). Threat inputs: the child's threat
+// list is generated and the multiset difference to the parent's list is applied (each computed ply's list is kept
+// in the worker's cache, RefreshCache::ply_threats, so the valid ancestor's list is read). Pawn-pair inputs: each
+// perspective's pawn-pair list is kept per ply in the worker's cache (RefreshCache::ply_pawns) next to the accumulator;
+// it is regenerated only on a ply that moves,
+// captures or promotes a pawn (other plies cannot change pawn pairs), and the difference is applied. All changes of
+// a ply go through one chunked pass over each perspective's accumulator. On a king-key change the perspective is
+// refreshed: piece-square inputs through the refresh cache, then every threat / pawn-pair column of the child.
+// All weights are int16 / 4096, so every accumulator value is an exact multiple of 2^-12 and incremental, cached
+// and from-scratch accumulators are bit-identical (tests/nnue_threat_tests.cpp, tests/nnue_pawn_pair_tests.cpp).
+
+namespace forge {
+
+__attribute__((section(".text.forge_v2"), noinline)) int Worker::static_evaluate_extra(const Board& board, SearchStack* ss) {
+    const nnue::v2::Net& net = *nnue::v2::active();
+    const bool residual = net.residual;
+    const bool threats = net.threats;
+    const bool pawns = net.pawn_pairs;
+    if (!ss->accumulator.valid) {
+        nnue::v2::RefreshCache& cache = nnue::v2::cache(net, id_);
+        if (threats && cache.ply_threats.empty()) [[unlikely]] cache.ply_threats.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(MaxPly + 8));
+        auto list_of = [&cache](const SearchStack* entry) -> nnue::v2::ThreatList& {
+            return cache.ply_threats[static_cast<std::size_t>(entry->ply + 4)];
+        };
+        if (pawns && cache.ply_pawns.empty()) [[unlikely]] cache.ply_pawns.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(MaxPly + 8));
+        auto pawns_of = [&cache](const SearchStack* entry) -> std::array<nnue::v2::PawnPairList, 2>& {
+            return cache.ply_pawns[static_cast<std::size_t>(entry->ply + 4)];
+        };
+        int back = 0;
+        while (back < ss->ply && !(ss - back - 1)->accumulator.valid) ++back;
+        nnue::v2::Pieces pieces = nnue::v2::pieces_of(board);
+        if (back >= ss->ply && !(ss - back - 1)->accumulator.valid) {
+            if (threats) nnue::v2::threat_keys(pieces, list_of(ss));
+            for (const Color color : {White, Black}) {
+                const std::size_t c = static_cast<std::size_t>(color);
+                nnue::v2::refresh_full_cached(net, cache, pieces, threats ? &list_of(ss) : nullptr, board.king_square(color), color,
+                                              ss->accumulator.values[c].data(), pawns ? &pawns_of(ss)[c] : nullptr);
+            }
+            ss->accumulator.valid = true;
+            if (residual) ss->runtime_psqt = Evaluator::refresh_runtime_psqt(board);
+        } else {
+            for (int k = 0; k <= back; ++k) nnue::v2::undo(pieces, (ss - k)->dirty);   // the valid ancestor's placement
+            auto king_of = [&pieces](Color color) { return lsb(pieces[static_cast<std::size_t>(make_piece(color, King))]); };
+            std::array<nnue::v2::KingKey, 2> key{nnue::v2::king_key(net, White, king_of(White)),
+                                                 nnue::v2::king_key(net, Black, king_of(Black))};
+            nnue::v2::ThreatDelta delta;
+            nnue::v2::PawnPairDelta pawn_delta;
+            constexpr std::size_t MaxColumns = nnue::v2::MaxThreatPairs + nnue::v2::MaxPawnPairs + 4;
+            std::array<const float*, MaxColumns> sub;
+            std::array<const float*, MaxColumns> add;
+            for (int i = back; i >= 0; --i) {
+                const SearchStack* parent = ss - i - 1;
+                SearchStack* child = ss - i;
+                const nnue::DirtyPieces& dirty = child->dirty;
+                nnue::v2::redo(pieces, dirty);   // the child's placement
+                nnue::v2::ThreatList* after = nullptr;
+                if (threats) {
+                    after = &list_of(child);   // the parent's list is valid: computed with the parent's accumulator
+                    nnue::v2::threat_keys(pieces, *after);
+                    nnue::v2::threat_delta(list_of(parent), *after, cache.threat_counts.data(), delta);
+                }
+                const bool pawn_move = pawns && nnue::v2::touches_pawns(dirty);
+                // The parent's pawn-pair lists are valid (computed with the parent's accumulator); a ply that touches
+                // no pawn keeps them (copied to the child's slot, a king-key refresh below may still replace one).
+                if (pawns && !pawn_move) pawns_of(child) = pawns_of(parent);
+                for (const Color color : {White, Black}) {
+                    const std::size_t c = static_cast<std::size_t>(color);
+                    const int king_square = king_of(color);
+                    const nnue::v2::KingKey moved = nnue::v2::king_key(net, color, king_square);
+                    if (!(moved == key[c])) [[unlikely]] {
+                        key[c] = moved;
+                        nnue::v2::refresh_full_cached(net, cache, pieces, after, king_square, color,
+                                                      child->accumulator.values[c].data(), pawns ? &pawns_of(child)[c] : nullptr);
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    int subs = 0;
+                    int adds = 0;
+                    for (int k = 0; k < dirty.count; ++k) {
+                        const nnue::PieceChange& change = dirty.changes[static_cast<std::size_t>(k)];
+                        const float* col = nnue::v2::column(net, color, key[c], change.piece, change.square);
+                        if (change.add) add[static_cast<std::size_t>(adds++)] = col;
+                        else sub[static_cast<std::size_t>(subs++)] = col;
+                    }
+                    if (threats) {
+                        for (int k = 0; k < delta.removed; ++k) {
+                            sub[static_cast<std::size_t>(subs++)] = nnue::v2::threat_column(net, color, key[c], delta.remove_keys[static_cast<std::size_t>(k)]);
+                        }
+                        for (int k = 0; k < delta.added; ++k) {
+                            add[static_cast<std::size_t>(adds++)] = nnue::v2::threat_column(net, color, key[c], delta.add_keys[static_cast<std::size_t>(k)]);
+                        }
+                    }
+                    if (pawn_move) {
+                        nnue::v2::PawnPairList& pawn_child = pawns_of(child)[c];
+                        nnue::v2::pawn_pair_features(pieces, color, key[c].flip, pawn_child);
+                        nnue::v2::pawn_pair_delta(pawns_of(parent)[c], pawn_child, cache.pawn_counts.data(), pawn_delta);
+                        for (int k = 0; k < pawn_delta.removed; ++k) {
+                            sub[static_cast<std::size_t>(subs++)] = nnue::v2::pawn_pair_column(net, pawn_delta.remove_features[static_cast<std::size_t>(k)]);
+                        }
+                        for (int k = 0; k < pawn_delta.added; ++k) {
+                            add[static_cast<std::size_t>(adds++)] = nnue::v2::pawn_pair_column(net, pawn_delta.add_features[static_cast<std::size_t>(k)]);
+                        }
+                    }
+                    nnue::v2::apply_lists(parent->accumulator.values[c].data(), child->accumulator.values[c].data(), sub.data(), subs,
+                                          add.data(), adds);
+                }
+                child->accumulator.valid = true;
+                if (residual) Evaluator::update_runtime_psqt(parent->runtime_psqt, child->runtime_psqt, dirty);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    const int network = nnue::v2::evaluate(net, board, ss->accumulator);
+    if (!residual) return network;
+    if (!ss->runtime_psqt.valid) ss->runtime_psqt = Evaluator::refresh_runtime_psqt(board);
+    // A residual network is a learned correction on top of the hand-crafted evaluation.
+    return std::clamp(evaluator_.evaluate_runtime(board, ss->runtime_psqt) + network, -MateInMaxPly + 1, MateInMaxPly - 1);
+}
+
+} // namespace forge
diff --git a/src/search/evaluation_threats.inc b/src/search/evaluation_threats.inc
@@ -0,0 +1,3 @@
+// O7: superseded by src/search/evaluation_extra.inc (the update path for threat and pawn-pair inputs,
+// Worker::static_evaluate_extra). This file is not included anywhere on this branch; it is kept only because
+// files are not deleted on the Opus job branches. A merge of the O-branches can drop it.
diff --git a/src/search/evaluation_v2.inc b/src/search/evaluation_v2.inc
@@ -0,0 +1,218 @@
+// Search module: static evaluation with a v2 network (C4: king-bucketed input
+// layer, C5: output buckets; UCI NNUEFile + NNUEKingBuckets / NNUEOutputBuckets,
+// OFF by default; forge/nnue_v2.hpp).
+// Compiled as part of src/search.cpp (included exactly once, before
+// evaluation.inc so that GCC keeps emitting static_evaluate at its AL1 s198
+// position: a caller is emitted after its callees).
+//
+// Reached only through the options::nnue_v2 branch at the top of
+// Worker::static_evaluate. This code lives in section .text.forge_v2 (or is
+// cold), so the default build's search functions keep their addresses.
+//
+// Accumulators reuse SearchStack::accumulator (a v2 net has the engine's
+// HiddenSize) with the lazy scheme of the compiled-in network: an invalid ply
+// walks back to the nearest valid ancestor and replays the recorded piece
+// changes, and every replayed ply becomes valid. New for king buckets: on a ply
+// where a king changes its own perspective's (bucket, flip) key, that
+// perspective is refreshed through the worker's refresh cache. The piece
+// placement of that ply is the board with the later plies' piece changes taken
+// back, so no board copy is kept per ply.
+
+#define FORGE_SEARCH_V2 __attribute__((section(".text.forge_v2"), noinline))
+
+namespace forge {
+
+FORGE_SEARCH_V2 int Worker::static_evaluate_v2(const Board& board, SearchStack* ss) {
+    // nnue_v2 is only set while a v2 net is active; the UCI layer re-selects
+    // after every change, so this fallback is defensive.
+    const nnue::v2::Net* active = nnue::v2::active();
+    if (active == nullptr || !nnue::enabled) return evaluator_.evaluate(board);
+    const nnue::v2::Net& net = *active;
+    // O6 / O7: a net with threat or pawn-pair inputs has its own update path (evaluation_extra.inc);
+    // apply_nnue_v2 activates one only with the matching NNUEThreats / NNUEPawnPairs.
+    if (net.threats || net.pawn_pairs) [[unlikely]] return static_evaluate_extra(board, ss);
+    // O4: residual == !options::pure_nnue (apply_nnue_v2 selects only a net whose header flag agrees with
+    // PureNnue), so with PureNnue the hand-crafted evaluation and its PSQT accumulator are never computed.
+    const bool residual = net.residual;
+    if (!ss->accumulator.valid) {
+        int back = 0;
+        while (back < ss->ply && !(ss - back - 1)->accumulator.valid) ++back;
+        if (back >= ss->ply && !(ss - back - 1)->accumulator.valid) {
+            nnue::v2::RefreshCache& cache = nnue::v2::cache(net, id_);
+            const nnue::v2::Pieces pieces = nnue::v2::pieces_of(board);
+            nnue::v2::refresh_cached(net, cache, pieces, board.king_square(White), White, ss->accumulator.values[White].data());
+            nnue::v2::refresh_cached(net, cache, pieces, board.king_square(Black), Black, ss->accumulator.values[Black].data());
+            ss->accumulator.valid = true;
+            if (residual) ss->runtime_psqt = Evaluator::refresh_runtime_psqt(board);
+        } else {
+            // King squares of the ancestor position: take back the replayed king
+            // moves (changes[0] is always the moving piece, removed from `from`).
+            std::array<int, 2> king{board.king_square(White), board.king_square(Black)};
+            for (int i = 0; i <= back; ++i) {
+                const nnue::DirtyPieces& dirty = (ss - i)->dirty;
+                if (dirty.count > 0 && piece_type(dirty.changes[0].piece) == King) {
+                    king[static_cast<std::size_t>(color_of(dirty.changes[0].piece))] = dirty.changes[0].square;
+                }
+            }
+            std::array<nnue::v2::KingKey, 2> key{nnue::v2::king_key(net, White, king[0]),
+                                                 nnue::v2::king_key(net, Black, king[1])};
+            for (int i = back; i >= 0; --i) {
+                const SearchStack* parent = ss - i - 1;
+                SearchStack* child = ss - i;
+                const nnue::DirtyPieces& dirty = child->dirty;
+                for (const Color color : {White, Black}) {
+                    const std::size_t c = static_cast<std::size_t>(color);
+                    if (dirty.count > 0 && dirty.changes[0].piece == make_piece(color, King)) {
+                        // The king's destination: the added change of the same piece.
+                        int to = dirty.changes[0].square;
+                        for (int k = 1; k < dirty.count; ++k) {
+                            const nnue::PieceChange& change = dirty.changes[static_cast<std::size_t>(k)];
+                            if (change.add && change.piece == dirty.changes[0].piece) to = change.square;
+                        }
+                        const nnue::v2::KingKey moved = nnue::v2::king_key(net, color, to);
+                        if (!(moved == key[c])) [[unlikely]] {
+                            key[c] = moved;
+                            nnue::v2::Pieces pieces = nnue::v2::pieces_of(board);
+                            for (int k = 0; k < i; ++k) nnue::v2::undo(pieces, (ss - k)->dirty);
+                            nnue::v2::refresh_cached(net, nnue::v2::cache(net, id_), pieces, to, color,
+                                                     child->accumulator.values[c].data());
+                            continue;
+                        }
+                    }
+                    nnue::v2::update(net, color, key[c], parent->accumulator.values[c].data(),
+                                     child->accumulator.values[c].data(), dirty);
+                }
+                child->accumulator.valid = true;
+                if (residual) Evaluator::update_runtime_psqt(parent->runtime_psqt, child->runtime_psqt, dirty);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    const int network = nnue::v2::evaluate(net, board, ss->accumulator);
+    if (!residual) return network;
+    if (!ss->runtime_psqt.valid) ss->runtime_psqt = Evaluator::refresh_runtime_psqt(board);
+    // A residual network is a learned correction on top of the hand-crafted evaluation.
+    return std::clamp(evaluator_.evaluate_runtime(board, ss->runtime_psqt) + network, -MateInMaxPly + 1, MateInMaxPly - 1);
+}
+
+namespace options {
+
+[[gnu::cold]] bool apply_nnue_v2(std::string* message) {
+    // UseNNUE false disables every network (hand-crafted evaluation only).
+    // O4 / O6 / O7: PureNnue 1, NNUEThreats 1 or NNUEPawnPairs 1 with NNUEKingBuckets 0 asks for a net without king
+    // buckets (R3 stack: with NNUEOutputBuckets 0 as well that is a request for one king bucket; with
+    // NNUEOutputBuckets N > 0, C5's rule applies: king count 0 = one unmirrored king bucket).
+    const bool threats = nnue_threats != 0;
+    const bool pawns = nnue_pawn_pairs != 0;
+    const bool alone = threats || pawns || pure_nnue;
+    const int wanted = nnue_king_buckets > 0 ? nnue_king_buckets : (alone && nnue_output_buckets <= 0 ? 1 : 0);
+    const nnue::v2::Net* net = nnue::v2::select(nnue::enabled ? wanted : 0, nnue::enabled ? nnue_output_buckets : 0, message);
+    if (net != nullptr && (net->threats != threats || net->pawn_pairs != pawns)) {
+        // O6 / O7: the header's extra-input bits must agree with NNUEThreats / NNUEPawnPairs (nnue_v2_problem
+        // names the mismatch).
+        (void)nnue::v2::select(0, 0, nullptr);
+        net = nullptr;
+        if (message != nullptr) *message = "the loaded net's threat / pawn-pair inputs differ from NNUEThreats / NNUEPawnPairs (not selected)";
+    } else if (net != nullptr && net->residual == pure_nnue) {
+        // O4: the header's residual flag must agree with PureNnue, so that the hand-crafted
+        // evaluation is skipped exactly when PureNnue is 1 (nnue_v2_problem names the mismatch).
+        (void)nnue::v2::select(0, 0, nullptr);
+        net = nullptr;
+        if (message != nullptr) {
+            *message = pure_nnue ? "PureNnue is 1 but the loaded net is residual (not selected)"
+                                 : "the loaded net is stand-alone and PureNnue is 0 (not selected)";
+        }
+    }
+    if (!nnue::enabled && message != nullptr) *message = "UseNNUE is false: hand-crafted evaluation only";
+    nnue_v2 = net != nullptr;
+    return nnue_v2;
+}
+
+[[gnu::cold]] std::string nnue_v2_problem(const std::string& file, const std::string& load_error) {
+    const bool threats = nnue_threats != 0;
+    const bool pawns = nnue_pawn_pairs != 0;
+    const bool extras = threats || pawns;
+    const bool alone = extras || pure_nnue;   // options that request a v2 net on their own
+    if (!nnue::enabled) return {};   // UseNNUE false: every network off on purpose
+    const int kb = nnue_king_buckets;
+    const int ob = nnue_output_buckets;
+    if (file.empty() && kb <= 0 && ob <= 0 && !alone) return {};   // nothing requested
+    if (!file.empty() && !load_error.empty()) return "cannot load NNUEFile " + file + ": " + load_error;
+    const nnue::v2::Net* net = nnue::v2::loaded();
+    if (net == nullptr) {
+        if (!file.empty()) return "NNUEFile " + file + " is not loaded";
+        if (kb <= 0 && ob <= 0) {   // O4 / O6 / O7: PureNnue, NNUEThreats or NNUEPawnPairs alone
+            if (threats) {
+                return "NNUEThreats is 1 but no v2 net is loaded (NNUEFile is empty); NNUEThreats needs a net with threat "
+                       "inputs (trainer --threats)";
+            }
+            if (pawns) {
+                return "NNUEPawnPairs is 1 but no v2 net is loaded (NNUEFile is empty); NNUEPawnPairs needs a net with "
+                       "pawn-pair inputs (trainer --pawn-pairs)";
+            }
+            return "PureNnue is 1 but no v2 net is loaded (NNUEFile is empty); PureNnue needs a stand-alone net "
+                   "(trainer --pure)";
+        }
+        const std::string set = kb > 0 && ob > 0 ? "NNUEKingBuckets " + std::to_string(kb) + " and NNUEOutputBuckets " +
+                                                       std::to_string(ob) + " are"
+                                : kb > 0 ? "NNUEKingBuckets " + std::to_string(kb) + " is"
+                                         : "NNUEOutputBuckets " + std::to_string(ob) + " is";
+        return set + " set but no v2 net is loaded (NNUEFile is empty)";
+    }
+    if (nnue_v2 && nnue::v2::active() == net) return {};
+    const std::string buckets = std::to_string(net->king_buckets) + (net->mirror ? " mirrored" : "") + " king buckets";
+    // O6 / O7: the extra-input bits of the file and NNUEThreats / NNUEPawnPairs must agree.
+    const std::string threat_net = "NNUEFile " + net->path + " has threat inputs (trainer --threats); set NNUEThreats 1 to use it";
+    const std::string pawn_net = "NNUEFile " + net->path + " has pawn-pair inputs (trainer --pawn-pairs); set NNUEPawnPairs 1 to use it";
+    // O4: the residual flag of the file and PureNnue must agree.
+    const std::string standalone = "NNUEFile " + net->path + " is a stand-alone net (network only, no hand-crafted "
+                                   "evaluation; trainer --pure); set PureNnue 1 to use it";
+    // C4 / C5: the bucket counts that select this net (select(): 0 stands for one unmirrored king bucket / one
+    // output bucket); the other options the net needs are named as well.
+    int want_kb = net->king_buckets == 1 && !net->mirror ? 0 : net->king_buckets;
+    const int want_ob = net->output_buckets == 1 ? 0 : net->output_buckets;
+    if (want_kb == 0 && want_ob == 0) want_kb = 1;
+    const std::string shape = buckets + ", " + std::to_string(net->output_buckets) + " output buckets";
+    const std::string hint = "; to use it set NNUEKingBuckets " + std::to_string(want_kb) + ", NNUEOutputBuckets " +
+                             std::to_string(want_ob) + (net->threats ? ", NNUEThreats 1" : "") +
+                             (net->pawn_pairs ? ", NNUEPawnPairs 1" : "") + (!net->residual ? ", PureNnue 1" : "");
+    // The request as apply_nnue_v2() passes it to select().
+    const int wanted_kb = kb > 0 ? kb : (alone && ob <= 0 ? 1 : 0);
+    const bool king_ok = wanted_kb > 0 ? net->king_buckets == wanted_kb : (net->king_buckets == 1 && !net->mirror);
+    const bool output_ok = ob > 0 ? net->output_buckets == ob : net->output_buckets == 1;
+    if (kb <= 0 && ob <= 0 && !alone) {   // a file without any bucket or net-kind option
+        const bool single = net->king_buckets == 1 && net->output_buckets == 1;
+        if (single && net->threats) return threat_net;
+        if (single && net->pawn_pairs) return pawn_net;
+        if (single && !net->residual) return standalone;
+        return "NNUEFile " + net->path + " is loaded (" + shape + ") but NNUEKingBuckets is 0 and NNUEOutputBuckets is 0" + hint;
+    }
+    if (!king_ok && kb <= 0 && ob <= 0 && alone) {
+        return std::string(threats ? "NNUEThreats" : pawns ? "NNUEPawnPairs" : "PureNnue") +
+               " is 1 and NNUEKingBuckets is 0, which selects a net without king buckets, but NNUEFile " + net->path +
+               " has " + buckets + "; set NNUEKingBuckets " + std::to_string(net->king_buckets) + " as well";
+    }
+    if (!king_ok || !output_ok) {
+        return "NNUEFile " + net->path + " (" + shape + ") does not match NNUEKingBuckets " + std::to_string(kb) +
+               ", NNUEOutputBuckets " + std::to_string(ob) + hint;
+    }
+    if (threats && !net->threats) {
+        return "NNUEThreats is 1 but NNUEFile " + net->path + " has no threat inputs; NNUEThreats needs a net trained with "
+               "--threats";
+    }
+    if (pawns && !net->pawn_pairs) {
+        return "NNUEPawnPairs is 1 but NNUEFile " + net->path + " has no pawn-pair inputs; NNUEPawnPairs needs a net trained "
+               "with --pawn-pairs";
+    }
+    if (!threats && net->threats) return threat_net;
+    if (!pawns && net->pawn_pairs) return pawn_net;
+    if (pure_nnue && net->residual) {
+        return "PureNnue is 1 but NNUEFile " + net->path + " is a residual net (a correction added to the hand-crafted "
+               "evaluation; trainer --residual); PureNnue needs a stand-alone net (trainer --pure)";
+    }
+    if (!pure_nnue && !net->residual) return standalone;
+    return "NNUEFile " + net->path + " was not selected";   // not reached: every unselected case is named above
+}
+
+} // namespace options
+
+} // namespace forge
diff --git a/src/search/history.inc b/src/search/history.inc
@@ -0,0 +1,97 @@
+// Search module: scoring and update rules of the move-ordering history tables
+// (Worker::histories_; tables, bonus and gravity in forge/history.hpp).
+// Compiled as part of src/search.cpp (included exactly once).
+
+namespace forge {
+
+static_assert(hist_rework::HistoryLimit == HistoryMax, "HistRework bounds assume the lock's history limit");
+static_assert(hist_rework::CorrectionEntryLimit == CorrectionLimit, "HistRework bounds assume the lock's correction limit");
+
+// HistRework: the threat-keyed butterfly slot of a quiet move at the node of `ss` (its maps are threat_maps_[ply]).
+std::int16_t& Worker::threat_history_entry(const Board& board, const SearchStack* ss, Move move) const {
+    const hist_rework::LesserThreats& threats = threat_maps_[static_cast<std::size_t>(ss->ply)];
+    const Bitboard set = threats.by_type[static_cast<std::size_t>(piece_type(board.moved_piece(move)))];
+    return (*threat_history_)[static_cast<std::size_t>(board.side_to_move())][hist_rework::square_bit(set, move.from)]
+                             [hist_rework::square_bit(set, move.to)][move.from][move.to];
+}
+
+void Worker::update_threat_history(const Board& board, const SearchStack* ss, Move best, int bonus, const Move* quiets,
+                                   int quiet_count) {
+    apply_history(threat_history_entry(board, ss, best), bonus);
+    for (int i = 0; i < quiet_count; ++i) {
+        if (quiets[i] == best) continue;
+        apply_history(threat_history_entry(board, ss, quiets[i]), -bonus);
+    }
+}
+
+int Worker::quiet_history(const Board& board, const SearchStack* ss, Move move) const {
+    const Piece piece = board.moved_piece(move);
+    int score = (*histories_.main_history)[static_cast<std::size_t>(board.side_to_move())][move.from][move.to];
+    if (const int level = search_options::hist_rework; level != 0 && ss != nullptr) [[unlikely]] {
+        // |score| <= 16384 after the blend, as for the lock table alone (hist_rework::blend).
+        score = hist_rework::blend(score, threat_history_entry(board, ss, move), hist_rework::threat_quarters(level));
+    }
+    if (ss != nullptr) {
+        if ((ss - 1)->continuation != nullptr) score += (*(ss - 1)->continuation)[static_cast<std::size_t>(piece)][move.to];
+        if ((ss - 2)->continuation != nullptr) score += (*(ss - 2)->continuation)[static_cast<std::size_t>(piece)][move.to];
+    }
+    return score;
+}
+
+int Worker::capture_history_value(const Board& board, Move move) const {
+    const Piece piece = board.moved_piece(move);
+    const int captured = piece_type(board.captured_piece(move));
+    return (*histories_.capture_history)[static_cast<std::size_t>(piece)][move.to][static_cast<std::size_t>(captured)];
+}
+
+void Worker::update_continuation(SearchStack* ss, Piece piece, int to, int bonus) {
+    for (const int back : {1, 2, 4}) {
+        const SearchStack* previous = ss - back;
+        if (previous->continuation == nullptr || previous->current_move.is_none()) continue;
+        apply_history((*previous->continuation)[static_cast<std::size_t>(piece)][static_cast<std::size_t>(to)],
+                      back == 4 ? bonus / 2 : bonus);
+    }
+}
+
+void Worker::update_quiet_stats(const Board& board, SearchStack* ss, Move move, int depth, const Move* quiets,
+                                  int quiet_count) {
+    const int bonus = history_bonus(depth);
+    const Color us = board.side_to_move();
+    if (ss->killers[0] != move) {
+        ss->killers[1] = ss->killers[0];
+        ss->killers[0] = move;
+    }
+    const SearchStack* previous = ss - 1;
+    if (!previous->current_move.is_none() && previous->moved_piece != Empty) {
+        (*histories_.counter_moves)[static_cast<std::size_t>(previous->moved_piece)][previous->current_move.to] = move;
+    }
+    const Piece piece = board.moved_piece(move);
+    apply_history((*histories_.main_history)[static_cast<std::size_t>(us)][move.from][move.to], bonus);
+    update_continuation(ss, piece, move.to, bonus);
+    for (int i = 0; i < quiet_count; ++i) {
+        const Move quiet = quiets[i];
+        if (quiet == move) continue;
+        apply_history((*histories_.main_history)[static_cast<std::size_t>(us)][quiet.from][quiet.to], -bonus);
+        update_continuation(ss, board.moved_piece(quiet), quiet.to, -bonus);
+    }
+    // HistRework: the threat-keyed twin learns the same bonus and maluses (lock gravity, apply_history).
+    if (search_options::hist_rework != 0) [[unlikely]] update_threat_history(board, ss, move, bonus, quiets, quiet_count);
+}
+
+void Worker::update_capture_stats(const Board& board, Move best, int depth, const Move* captures, int capture_count) {
+    const int bonus = history_bonus(depth);
+    if (best.is_capture()) {
+        const Piece piece = board.moved_piece(best);
+        const int captured = piece_type(board.captured_piece(best));
+        apply_history((*histories_.capture_history)[static_cast<std::size_t>(piece)][best.to][static_cast<std::size_t>(captured)], bonus);
+    }
+    for (int i = 0; i < capture_count; ++i) {
+        const Move capture = captures[i];
+        if (capture == best) continue;
+        const Piece piece = board.moved_piece(capture);
+        const int captured = piece_type(board.captured_piece(capture));
+        apply_history((*histories_.capture_history)[static_cast<std::size_t>(piece)][capture.to][static_cast<std::size_t>(captured)], -bonus);
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace forge
diff --git a/src/search/iterative.inc b/src/search/iterative.inc
@@ -0,0 +1,242 @@
+// Search module: root setup and the iterative-deepening driver with
+// aspiration windows (one Worker; thread 0 also does time management/output).
+// Compiled as part of src/search.cpp (included exactly once).
+
+namespace forge {
+
+// Cold: called only with HardDeadline on, once per iteration (keeps the hot
+// search functions' placement unchanged).
+[[gnu::cold]] void Worker::publish_best_move(const SearchInfo& result) {
+    if (!is_main()) return;
+    const std::uint64_t pair = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(pack_move(result.ponder_move)) << 32U | pack_move(result.best_move);
+    options::published_moves.store(pair, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+}
+
+SearchInfo Worker::search(Board board, const SearchLimits& limits, bool emit_info, const std::vector<Worker*>& all) {
+#ifdef FORGE_S13_TT_CHILD_PREFETCH
+    s13_tt_child_prefetch_ = limits.s13_tt_child_prefetch;
+#endif
+    const auto origin = limits.start_time != std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point{}
+        ? limits.start_time : limits.request_start;
+    start_ = origin == std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point{}
+        ? std::chrono::steady_clock::now() : origin;
+    local_nodes_ = 0;
+    nodes_.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+    all_workers_ = &all;
+    seldepth_ = 0;
+    aborted_ = false;
+    pondering_ = limits.ponder;
+    null_tries_ = 0;
+    null_cutoffs_ = 0;
+    root_moves_ = limits.search_moves;
+    root_restricted_ = limits.search_moves_specified;
+    root_history_index_ = board.history_size() - 1;
+    node_limit_ = limits.nodes;
+    node_limit_specified_ = is_main() && (limits.nodes_specified || limits.nodes != 0);
+
+    // Time allocation.
+    timeman::allocate(limits, soft_limit_ms_, hard_limit_ms_, time_managed_);
+    SearchInfo result;
+    const MoveList legal = board.legal_moves();
+    MoveList root_candidates;
+    for (const Move move : legal) {
+        if (!root_restricted_ ||
+            std::any_of(root_moves_.begin(), root_moves_.end(), [&](const Move& m) { return m == move; })) {
+            root_candidates.push_back(move);
+        }
+    }
+    result.restricted_root_empty = root_restricted_ && !legal.empty() && root_candidates.empty();
+    if (result.restricted_root_empty && emit_info) {
+        const std::lock_guard<std::mutex> output_lock(uci_output_mutex());
+        std::cout << "info string searchmoves restriction has no legal root moves" << std::endl;
+    }
+
+    const int mate_moves = std::clamp(limits.mate_moves, 0, 32);
+    const int mate_depth = mate_moves > 0 ? mate_moves * 2 - 1 : 64;
+    const int max_depth = std::clamp(std::min(limits.depth, mate_depth), 1, 64);
+
+    auto emit = [&](int depth, int score, const std::vector<Move>& pv) {
+        if (!emit_info) return;
+        const std::int64_t elapsed = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
+            std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - start_).count();
+        const std::uint64_t nodes = total_nodes();
+        const std::uint64_t nps = elapsed > 0 ? nodes * 1000U / static_cast<std::uint64_t>(elapsed) : 0;
+        const std::lock_guard<std::mutex> output_lock(uci_output_mutex());
+        std::cout << "info depth " << depth << " seldepth " << std::max(seldepth_, depth) << " score " << format_score(score)
+                  << " nodes " << nodes << " nps " << nps << " hashfull " << shared_.tt.hashfull() << " time " << elapsed << " pv";
+        for (const Move move : pv) std::cout << ' ' << Board::move_to_uci(move);
+        std::cout << std::endl;
+    };
+
+    // Terminal or adjudicated roots: report without searching.
+    const bool root_drawn = !legal.empty() && (board.is_repetition() || board.halfmove_clock() >= 100 ||
+                                               board.is_insufficient_material());
+    if (root_candidates.empty() || root_drawn) {
+        int score = 0;
+        if (legal.empty() && board.in_check()) score = -MateValue;
+        for (int depth = 1; depth <= max_depth; ++depth) {
+            result.depth = depth;
+            result.score = score;
+            emit(depth, score, {});
+            if (should_stop()) break;
+        }
+        if (!root_candidates.empty()) result.best_move = root_candidates.front();
+        result.nodes = total_nodes();
+        nodes_.store(local_nodes_, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+        result.elapsed_ms = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
+            std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - start_).count());
+        return result;
+    }
+
+    // An explicit zero node budget is an immediate bounded answer.
+    if (limits.nodes_specified && limits.nodes == 0) {
+        result.best_move = root_candidates.front();
+        result.depth = 0;
+        return result;
+    }
+
+    // Dispatch and root setup may already have consumed the entire budget.
+    // Check helpers too before they switch to the main worker's stop signal.
+    if (hard_limit_ms_ > 0 && elapsed_ms() >= hard_limit_ms_) {
+        result.best_move = root_candidates.front();
+        result.elapsed_ms = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
+            std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - start_).count());
+        return result;
+    }
+    if (!is_main()) {
+        // Helpers run until the main thread stops them.
+        soft_limit_ms_ = hard_limit_ms_ = 0;
+        time_managed_ = false;
+    }
+
+    for (SearchStack& ss : stack_) {
+        ss.pv_length = 0;
+        ss.current_move = Move::none();
+        ss.excluded_move = Move::none();
+        ss.moved_piece = Empty;
+        ss.static_eval = ValueNone;
+        ss.continuation = &(*histories_.continuation_history)[Empty][0];
+        ss.double_extensions = 0;
+        ss.null_move_allowed = true;
+        ss.killers = {};
+    }
+    SearchStack* ss = &stack_[4];
+    for (int i = 0; i < MaxPly + 4; ++i) stack_[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)].ply = i - 4;
+    for (SearchStack& entry : stack_) {
+        entry.accumulator.valid = false;
+        entry.runtime_psqt.valid = false;
+    }
+    if (nnue::enabled && nnue::available()) {
+        nnue::refresh(board, ss->accumulator);
+        if (nnue::residual()) ss->runtime_psqt = Evaluator::refresh_runtime_psqt(board);
+    }
+    // A v2 network computes the root accumulator itself (static_evaluate_v2).
+    if (options::nnue_v2) [[unlikely]] ss->accumulator.valid = false;
+
+    Move previous_best = Move::none();
+    int stable_iterations = 0;
+    int score = 0;
+    int previous_score = ValueNone;
+    root_effort_count_ = root_candidates.size();
+    assert(root_effort_count_ <= root_effort_moves_.size());
+    std::copy(root_candidates.begin(), root_candidates.end(), root_effort_moves_.begin());
+    root_move_nodes_.fill(0);
+    for (int depth = 1; depth <= max_depth; ++depth) {
+        root_depth_ = depth;
+        int delta = tune::AspirationDelta;
+        int alpha = -Infinite;
+        int beta = Infinite;
+        if (depth >= 4) {
+            alpha = std::max(score - delta, -Infinite);
+            beta = std::min(score + delta, Infinite);
+        }
+        int iteration_score = score;
+        while (true) {
+            ss->ply = 0;
+            // UCI CorrMargins / CorrExt / CuckooRep pick the search instance (src/search.cpp, head of
+            // alphabeta.inc); with all three off it is the AL1 s198 search.
+            switch ((options::corr_margins != 0 ? 1 : 0) | (options::corr_ext != 0 ? 2 : 0) | (options::cuckoo_rep ? 4 : 0)) {
+                case 0: iteration_score = search(board, ss, depth, alpha, beta, false); break;
+                case 1: iteration_score = search_corr_margins(board, ss, depth, alpha, beta, false); break;
+                case 2: iteration_score = search_corr_ext(board, ss, depth, alpha, beta, false); break;
+                case 3: iteration_score = search_cm_ce(board, ss, depth, alpha, beta, false); break;
+                case 4: iteration_score = search_cuckoo(board, ss, depth, alpha, beta, false); break;
+                case 5: iteration_score = search_cm_cu(board, ss, depth, alpha, beta, false); break;
+                case 6: iteration_score = search_ce_cu(board, ss, depth, alpha, beta, false); break;
+                default: iteration_score = search_cm_ce_cu(board, ss, depth, alpha, beta, false); break;
+            }
+            if (aborted_) break;
+            if (iteration_score <= alpha) {
+                beta = (alpha + beta) / 2;
+                alpha = std::max(iteration_score - delta, -Infinite);
+            } else if (iteration_score >= beta) {
+                beta = std::min(iteration_score + delta, Infinite);
+            } else {
+                break;
+            }
+            delta += delta / 2 + 4;
+        }
+        if (aborted_) {
+            // A partially searched iteration that already raised alpha at the
+            // root has found a move at least as good as the previous best.
+            if (ss->pv_length > 0 && !(result.pv.size() > 0 && result.pv[0] == ss->pv[0])) {
+                result.best_move = ss->pv[0];
+                result.pv.assign(ss->pv.begin(), ss->pv.begin() + ss->pv_length);
+                result.ponder_move = result.pv.size() > 1 ? result.pv[1] : Move::none();
+                if (options::hard_deadline_ms > 0) publish_best_move(result);
+                // Publish the revised line so GUIs see a PV consistent with the move.
+                // A tablebase root (UCI SyzygyPath) reports the tablebase result.
+                emit(depth, syzygy::root_display.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != 0 ? syzygy::display_score(result.score)
+                                                                                     : result.score, result.pv);
+            }
+            break;
+        }
+        score = iteration_score;
+        result.score = score;
+        result.depth = depth;
+        result.seldepth = std::max(seldepth_, depth);
+        result.pv.assign(ss->pv.begin(), ss->pv.begin() + ss->pv_length);
+        if (!result.pv.empty()) result.best_move = result.pv.front();
+        result.ponder_move = result.pv.size() > 1 ? result.pv[1] : Move::none();
+        result.nodes = total_nodes();
+        if (options::hard_deadline_ms > 0) publish_best_move(result);
+        emit(depth, syzygy::root_display.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != 0 ? syzygy::display_score(score) : score,
+             result.pv);
+
+        if (result.best_move == previous_best) ++stable_iterations;
+        else stable_iterations = 0;
+        previous_best = result.best_move;
+        const int iteration_previous_score = previous_score;
+        previous_score = score;
+
+        if (mate_moves > 0 && is_mate_score(score) && MateValue - std::abs(score) <= mate_depth) break;
+        if (should_stop()) break;
+        if (time_managed_ && !(pondering_ && shared_.ponder_hit_ns.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == 0)) {
+            double factor = timeman::stability_factor(stable_iterations, depth);
+            if (local_nodes_ > 0 && depth >= 6) {
+                const auto effort_end = root_effort_moves_.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(root_effort_count_);
+                const auto best_effort = std::find(root_effort_moves_.begin(), effort_end, result.best_move);
+                if (best_effort != effort_end) {
+                    const auto index = static_cast<std::size_t>(best_effort - root_effort_moves_.begin());
+                    const double effort = static_cast<double>(root_move_nodes_[index]) / static_cast<double>(local_nodes_);
+                    factor *= timeman::effort_scale(effort);
+                }
+            }
+            if (timeman::eval_fell(iteration_previous_score, score)) {
+                factor *= timeman::falling_eval_scale(iteration_previous_score, score);
+            }
+            factor = timeman::factor_floor(factor);
+            if (elapsed_ms() >= static_cast<std::int64_t>(static_cast<double>(soft_limit_ms_) * factor)) break;
+        }
+    }
+
+    nodes_.store(local_nodes_, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+    result.nodes = total_nodes();
+    result.seldepth = std::max(result.seldepth, seldepth_);
+    result.elapsed_ms = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
+        std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - start_).count());
+    if (result.best_move.is_none()) result.best_move = root_candidates.front();
+    return result;
+}
+
+} // namespace forge
diff --git a/src/search/movepicker.hpp b/src/search/movepicker.hpp
@@ -0,0 +1,279 @@
+#pragma once
+
+// Search module: staged move picker.
+//   Main:       TT move, good captures (SEE >= 0, MVV + capture history),
+//               killer 1, killer 2, counter move, quiets (history + direct
+//               check bonus), bad captures.
+//   Quiescence: TT move (captures/promotions only), captures.
+//   Evasions:   TT move, then every move (captures first).
+// The picker scores moves with the worker's history rules (Worker::quiet_history,
+// Worker::capture_history_value) and reads its counter-move table, through its
+// friendship with Worker (as in AL1 s198, which keeps the code generation).
+// Included by src/search.cpp and by tests/movepicker_tests.cpp.
+
+#include "forge/search.hpp"
+
+#include <algorithm>
+#include <array>
+#include <cstddef>
+
+namespace forge {
+
+// Material gained by a capture or promotion, in SEE units.
+[[nodiscard]] inline int capture_value(const Board& board, Move move) {
+    int value = SeePieceValue[static_cast<std::size_t>(piece_type(board.captured_piece(move)))];
+    if (move.is_promotion()) value += SeePieceValue[move.promotion] - SeePieceValue[Pawn];
+    return value;
+}
+
+class MovePicker {
+public:
+    enum class Mode { Main, Quiescence };
+
+    MovePicker(const Board& board, const Worker& searcher, const SearchStack* ss, Move tt_move, Mode mode)
+        : board_(board), searcher_(searcher), ss_(ss), tt_move_(tt_move) {
+        const bool in_check = board.in_check();
+        if (!tt_move_.is_none() && !board_.is_pseudo_legal(tt_move_)) tt_move_ = Move::none();
+        if (mode == Mode::Quiescence && !in_check && !tt_move_.is_none() && !tt_move_.is_capture() &&
+            !tt_move_.is_promotion()) {
+            tt_move_ = Move::none();
+        }
+        if (in_check) stage_ = tt_move_.is_none() ? EvasionGenerate : EvasionTT;
+        else if (mode == Mode::Quiescence) stage_ = tt_move_.is_none() ? QsGenerate : QsTT;
+        else stage_ = tt_move_.is_none() ? GenerateCaptures : MainTT;
+        if (ss != nullptr) {
+            killers_ = ss->killers;
+            const SearchStack* previous = ss - 1;
+            if (!previous->current_move.is_none() && previous->moved_piece != Empty) {
+                counter_ = (*searcher_.histories_.counter_moves)[static_cast<std::size_t>(previous->moved_piece)][previous->current_move.to];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Emitted out of line as a COMDAT, which the PE linker places after every
+    // object's .text: pin it to a 64-byte boundary (its AL1 s198 placement) so
+    // its alignment does not depend on the size of other translation units.
+    [[nodiscard, gnu::aligned(64)]] Move next(bool skip_quiets) {
+        while (true) {
+            switch (stage_) {
+            case MainTT:
+            case QsTT:
+            case EvasionTT:
+                stage_ = static_cast<Stage>(stage_ + 1);
+                return tt_move_;
+
+            case GenerateCaptures:
+                generate_captures();
+                stage_ = GoodCaptures;
+                [[fallthrough]];
+            case GoodCaptures:
+                while (index_ < count_) {
+                    const Move move = pick_best();
+                    if (move == tt_move_) continue;
+                    if (!board_.see_ge(move, -move_score_threshold(move))) {
+                        bad_captures_[bad_count_++] = move;
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    return move;
+                }
+                stage_ = Killer1;
+                [[fallthrough]];
+            case Killer1:
+                stage_ = Killer2;
+                if (!skip_quiets && is_valid_quiet(killers_[0])) return killers_[0];
+                [[fallthrough]];
+            case Killer2:
+                stage_ = CounterMove;
+                if (!skip_quiets && is_valid_quiet(killers_[1]) && killers_[1] != killers_[0]) return killers_[1];
+                [[fallthrough]];
+            case CounterMove:
+                stage_ = GenerateQuiets;
+                if (!skip_quiets && is_valid_quiet(counter_) && counter_ != killers_[0] && counter_ != killers_[1]) {
+                    return counter_;
+                }
+                [[fallthrough]];
+            case GenerateQuiets:
+                if (!skip_quiets) generate_quiets();
+                stage_ = QuietMoves;
+                [[fallthrough]];
+            case QuietMoves:
+                if (!skip_quiets) {
+                    while (index_ < count_) {
+                        const Move move = pick_best();
+                        if (move == tt_move_ || move == killers_[0] || move == killers_[1] || move == counter_) continue;
+                        return move;
+                    }
+                }
+                stage_ = BadCaptures;
+                bad_index_ = 0;
+                [[fallthrough]];
+            case BadCaptures:
+                if (bad_index_ < bad_count_) return bad_captures_[bad_index_++];
+                return Move::none();
+
+            case QsGenerate:
+                generate_captures();
+                stage_ = QsCaptures;
+                [[fallthrough]];
+            case QsCaptures:
+                while (index_ < count_) {
+                    const Move move = pick_best();
+                    if (move == tt_move_) continue;
+                    return move;
+                }
+                return Move::none();
+
+            case EvasionGenerate:
+                generate_evasions();
+                stage_ = Evasions;
+                [[fallthrough]];
+            case Evasions:
+                while (index_ < count_) {
+                    const Move move = pick_best();
+                    if (move == tt_move_) continue;
+                    return move;
+                }
+                return Move::none();
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Valid for the move just returned by next(), until the next next() call.
+    // Only Main/GoodCaptures returns after a successful SEE >= 0 test.
+    [[nodiscard]] bool last_move_see_nonnegative() const { return stage_ == GoodCaptures; }
+
+private:
+    enum Stage : int {
+        MainTT, GenerateCaptures, GoodCaptures, Killer1, Killer2, CounterMove, GenerateQuiets, QuietMoves, BadCaptures,
+        QsTT, QsGenerate, QsCaptures,
+        EvasionTT, EvasionGenerate, Evasions
+    };
+    struct Scored {
+        Move move;
+        int score;
+    };
+
+    const Board& board_;
+    const Worker& searcher_;
+    const SearchStack* ss_;
+    Move tt_move_;
+    std::array<Move, 2> killers_{};
+    Move counter_{};
+    Stage stage_;
+#if FORGE_NOZERO_MOVE_BUFFERS
+    UninitArray<Scored, 256> moves_;  // only [0, count_) is ever read
+#else
+    std::array<Scored, 256> moves_{};
+#endif
+    std::size_t index_ = 0;
+    std::size_t count_ = 0;
+#if FORGE_NOZERO_MOVE_BUFFERS
+    UninitArray<Move, 64> bad_captures_;  // only [0, bad_count_) is ever read
+#else
+    std::array<Move, 64> bad_captures_{};
+#endif
+    std::size_t bad_count_ = 0;
+    std::size_t bad_index_ = 0;
+    MoveList buffer_;
+    std::size_t list_start_ = 0;
+    bool sorted_ = false;
+    std::array<Bitboard, 7> check_squares_{};
+
+    // Quiet/evasion generation are mutually exclusive stages, each reached at most once.
+    void init_check_squares() {
+        const Color them = opposite(board_.side_to_move());
+        const int enemy_king = board_.king_square(them);
+        const Bitboard occupancy = board_.occupied();
+        check_squares_[Pawn] = attacks::pawn(them, enemy_king);
+        check_squares_[Knight] = attacks::knight(enemy_king);
+        check_squares_[Bishop] = attacks::bishop(enemy_king, occupancy);
+        check_squares_[Rook] = attacks::rook(enemy_king, occupancy);
+        check_squares_[Queen] = check_squares_[Bishop] | check_squares_[Rook];
+        check_squares_[King] = 0;
+    }
+
+    [[nodiscard]] bool direct_check(Move move) const {
+        const int type = piece_type(board_.moved_piece(move));
+        return (check_squares_[static_cast<std::size_t>(type)] & square_bb(move.to)) != 0;
+    }
+
+    [[nodiscard]] static int move_score_threshold(Move) { return 0; }
+
+    [[nodiscard]] bool is_valid_quiet(Move move) const {
+        return !move.is_none() && move != tt_move_ && !move.is_capture() && !move.is_promotion() &&
+               board_.is_pseudo_legal(move);
+    }
+
+    // Most nodes need only the first few moves: select those on demand and
+    // sort the remainder once if the search gets that far.
+    [[nodiscard]] Move pick_best() {
+        if (!sorted_) {
+            if (index_ - list_start_ < 4) {
+                std::size_t best = index_;
+                for (std::size_t i = index_ + 1; i < count_; ++i) {
+                    if (moves_[i].score > moves_[best].score) best = i;
+                }
+                std::swap(moves_[index_], moves_[best]);
+            } else {
+                std::sort(moves_.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(index_), moves_.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(count_),
+                          [](const Scored& a, const Scored& b) { return a.score > b.score; });
+                sorted_ = true;
+            }
+        }
+        return moves_[index_++].move;
+    }
+
+    void sort_moves() {
+        list_start_ = index_;
+        sorted_ = false;
+    }
+
+    void generate_captures() {
+        buffer_.clear();
+        board_.generate_pseudo_legal(buffer_, true);
+        index_ = 0;
+        count_ = 0;
+        for (const Move move : buffer_) {
+            int score = 10 * capture_value(board_, move) + searcher_.capture_history_value(board_, move);
+            if (move.is_promotion()) score += move.promotion == Queen ? 20000 : -5000;
+            moves_[count_++] = Scored{move, score};
+        }
+        sort_moves();
+    }
+
+    void generate_quiets() {
+        init_check_squares();
+        buffer_.clear();
+        board_.generate_pseudo_legal(buffer_, false);
+        index_ = 0;
+        count_ = 0;
+        for (const Move move : buffer_) {
+            if (move.is_capture() || move.is_promotion()) continue;
+            int score = searcher_.quiet_history(board_, ss_, move);
+            if (direct_check(move)) score += 12000;
+            moves_[count_++] = Scored{move, score};
+        }
+        sort_moves();
+    }
+
+    void generate_evasions() {
+        init_check_squares();
+        buffer_.clear();
+        board_.generate_pseudo_legal(buffer_, false);
+        index_ = 0;
+        count_ = 0;
+        for (const Move move : buffer_) {
+            int score;
+            if (move.is_capture() || move.is_promotion()) {
+                score = 1'000'000 + 10 * capture_value(board_, move) -
+                        SeePieceValue[static_cast<std::size_t>(piece_type(board_.moved_piece(move)))] / 10;
+            } else {
+                score = searcher_.quiet_history(board_, ss_, move) + (direct_check(move) ? 12000 : 0);
+            }
+            moves_[count_++] = Scored{move, score};
+        }
+        sort_moves();
+    }
+};
+
+} // namespace forge

diff --git a/src/search/qsearch.inc b/src/search/qsearch.inc

+// Search module: quiescence search.
+// Compiled as part of src/search.cpp, which includes this file twice (see the
+// head of alphabeta.inc): as Worker::qsearch, the AL1 s198 function, and as
+// Worker::qsearch_cuckoo_body for UCI CuckooRep. FORGE_QSEARCH_FN names the
+// function defined here, FORGE_QSEARCH_CALL the function called for children.
+#if !defined(FORGE_QSEARCH_FN) || !defined(FORGE_QSEARCH_CALL)
+#error "define FORGE_QSEARCH_FN and FORGE_QSEARCH_CALL before including search/qsearch.inc"
+#endif
+
+namespace forge {
+
+int Worker::FORGE_QSEARCH_FN(Board& board, SearchStack* ss, int alpha, int beta) {
+    const bool pv_node = beta - alpha > 1;
+    const int ply = ss->ply;
+    if (((local_nodes_ & 1023U) == 0 || node_limit_specified_) && should_stop()) aborted_ = true;
+    if (aborted_) return 0;
+    ++local_nodes_;
+    seldepth_ = std::max(seldepth_, ply);
+    ss->pv_length = 0;
+
+    if (is_draw(board, ply)) return 0;
+    if (ply >= MaxPly) return board.in_check() ? 0 : static_evaluate(board, ss);
+
+    // UCI IntendedThreefold: the side to move may claim a draw before its next
+    // move (FIDE 9.2.1), so the draw score is a floor here. The value then depends
+    // on the game history: no TT score is used or stored at this node.
+    const bool claim = options::intended_threefold && board.can_claim_threefold_by_next_move();
+    if (claim) {
+        if (beta <= 0) return 0;
+        alpha = std::max(alpha, 0);
+    }
+
+    const bool in_check = board.in_check();
+    ss->in_check = in_check;
+
+    const TTProbe tt_probe = shared_.tt.probe(board.key());
+    const bool tt_hit = tt_probe.hit;
+    const TTEntry& tte = tt_probe.entry;
+    const Move tt_move = tt_hit ? tte.move : Move::none();
+    const int tt_score = !claim && tt_hit && tte.score_usable(board.halfmove_clock())
+                             ? score_from_tt(tte.score, ply) : ValueNone;
+    const Bound tt_bound = tt_hit ? tte.bound() : BoundNone;
+    const bool tt_pv = pv_node || (tt_hit && tte.pv());
+    if (!pv_node && tt_hit && tt_score != ValueNone &&
+        (tt_bound == BoundExact || (tt_bound == BoundLower && tt_score >= beta) ||
+         (tt_bound == BoundUpper && tt_score <= alpha))) {
+        return tt_score;
+    }
+
+    int best_score;
+    int futility_base;
+    if (in_check) {
+        ss->static_eval = ValueNone;
+        best_score = futility_base = -Infinite;
+    } else {
+        int eval;
+        if (tt_hit && tte.eval != ValueNone) ss->raw_eval = tte.eval;
+        else ss->raw_eval = static_evaluate(board, ss);
+        eval = ss->static_eval = corrected_eval(board, ss->raw_eval);
+        if (tt_hit && tt_score != ValueNone &&
+            ((tt_bound == BoundLower && tt_score > eval) || (tt_bound == BoundUpper && tt_score < eval) ||
+             tt_bound == BoundExact)) {
+            eval = tt_score;
+        }
+        best_score = eval;
+        if (best_score >= beta) {
+            if (!tt_hit && !claim) shared_.tt.store(tt_probe, board.key(), score_to_tt(best_score, ply), ss->raw_eval, Move::none(), 0, BoundLower, false, board.halfmove_clock());
+            return best_score;
+        }
+        if (best_score > alpha) alpha = best_score;
+        futility_base = ss->static_eval + tune::QsFutilityMargin;
+    }
+    if (claim && best_score < 0) best_score = 0;
+
+    // HistRework: check evasions score quiet moves with quiet_history, which reads this node's maps.
+    if (in_check && search_options::hist_rework != 0) [[unlikely]]
+        threat_maps_[static_cast<std::size_t>(ply)] = hist_rework::lesser_threats(board);
+    MovePicker picker(board, *this, ss, tt_move, MovePicker::Mode::Quiescence);
+    Move best_move = Move::none();
+    int move_count = 0;
+    Move move;
+    while (!(move = picker.next(false)).is_none()) {
+        if (!board.is_legal(move)) continue;
+        ++move_count;
+        if (!in_check && best_score > -MateInMaxPly) {
+            const bool gives_check = board.gives_check(move);
+            // Futility: even winning the captured piece cannot raise alpha.
+            if (!gives_check && !move.is_promotion() && futility_base + capture_value(board, move) <= alpha) {
+                best_score = std::max(best_score, futility_base + capture_value(board, move));
+                continue;
+            }
+            if (!board.see_ge(move, -tune::QsSeeMargin)) continue;
+        }
+        ss->current_move = move;
+        ss->moved_piece = board.moved_piece(move);
+        ss->continuation = &(*histories_.continuation_history)[static_cast<std::size_t>(ss->moved_piece)][move.to];
+        (ss + 1)->ply = ply + 1;
+        record_move(ss, board, move);
+        Undo undo;
+        (void)board.make_move(move, undo);
+        shared_.tt.prefetch(board.key());
+        const int score = -FORGE_QSEARCH_CALL(board, ss + 1, -beta, -alpha);
+        board.unmake_move(move, undo);
+        if (aborted_) return 0;
+        if (score > best_score) {
+            best_score = score;
+            if (score > alpha) {
+                best_move = move;
+                if (pv_node) {
+                    ss->pv[0] = move;
+                    std::copy_n((ss + 1)->pv.begin(), (ss + 1)->pv_length, ss->pv.begin() + 1);
+                    ss->pv_length = (ss + 1)->pv_length + 1;
+                }
+                if (score >= beta) break;
+                alpha = score;
