+// Stop early when the best move has been stable; spend longer when it keeps changing.
+[[nodiscard]] inline double stability_factor(int stable_iterations, int depth) {
+    double factor = 1.0;
+    if (stable_iterations >= 6) factor = tune::TmStableFactor / 100.0;
+    else if (stable_iterations >= 3) factor = tune::TmSemiStableFactor / 100.0;
+    else if (stable_iterations == 0 && depth >= 6) factor = tune::TmUnstableFactor / 100.0;
+    return factor;
+}
+
+// A best move that absorbed most of the effort (share of the iteration's
+// nodes spent below it) is clearly best; spend longer when the alternatives
+// still draw attention.
+[[nodiscard]] inline double effort_scale(double effort) {
+    return std::clamp(tune::TmEffortBase / 100.0 - effort, 0.6, 1.4);
+}
+
+// A falling evaluation deserves more thought.
+[[nodiscard]] inline bool eval_fell(int previous_score, int score) {
+    return previous_score != ValueNone && !is_mate_score(score) && score < previous_score;
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] inline double falling_eval_scale(int previous_score, int score) {
+    const int drop = std::min(previous_score - score, 150);
+    return 1.0 + drop * tune::TmFallingEvalPercent / 10000.0;
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] inline double factor_floor(double factor) {
+    return std::max(factor, tune::TmMinFactorPercent / 100.0);
+}
+
+} // namespace forge::timeman
diff --git a/src/search/tt.inc b/src/search/tt.inc
@@ -0,0 +1,153 @@
+// Search module: transposition table (interface in forge/tt.hpp).
+// Compiled as part of src/search.cpp (included exactly once).
+
+// UCI LocklessTT dispatch (tt_lockless.inc): a forced tail call where the
+// compiler supports it (GCC 15+), so the default path of store() keeps the
+// lock's frame instead of loading the forwarded arguments first.
+#if defined(__GNUC__) && !defined(__clang__) && __GNUC__ >= 15
+#define FORGE_LTT_TAIL [[gnu::musttail]]
+#else
+#define FORGE_LTT_TAIL
+#endif
+
+namespace forge {
+
+thread_local const TranspositionTable::ExclusiveLease* TranspositionTable::active_lease_ = nullptr;
+
+TranspositionTable::ExclusiveLease::ExclusiveLease(TranspositionTable& table)
+    : table_(table), previous_(active_lease_), gate_(table.exclusive_gate_, std::defer_lock) {
+    if (!table_.has_exclusive_access()) {
+        gate_.lock();
+        table_.exclusive_requested_.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
+        try {
+            // A foreign accessor either completes before its stripe drains,
+            // or observes the request after acquiring that drained stripe.
+            for (std::size_t i = 0; i < StripeCount; ++i) {
+                const std::lock_guard<std::mutex> drain(table_.stripes_[i].mutex);
+            }
+        } catch (...) {
+            table_.exclusive_requested_.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
+            throw;
+        }
+    }
+    active_lease_ = this;
+}
+
+TranspositionTable::ExclusiveLease::~ExclusiveLease() {
+    // Publish completed writes before member destruction releases the gate.
+    active_lease_ = previous_;
+    if (gate_.owns_lock()) table_.exclusive_requested_.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
+}
+
+bool TranspositionTable::has_exclusive_access() const {
+    for (const ExclusiveLease* lease = active_lease_; lease != nullptr; lease = lease->previous_) {
+        if (&lease->table_ == this) return true;
+    }
+    return false;
+}
+
+std::unique_lock<std::mutex> TranspositionTable::lock_stripe(std::size_t index) const {
+    std::mutex& stripe = stripes_[index % StripeCount].mutex;
+    if (has_exclusive_access()) return std::unique_lock<std::mutex>(stripe, std::defer_lock);
+    for (;;) {
+        std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(stripe);
+        if (!exclusive_requested_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return lock;
+        // Never wait for the gate while holding a stripe: the owner drains it.
+        lock.unlock();
+        const std::lock_guard<std::mutex> wait(exclusive_gate_);
+    }
+}
+
+void TranspositionTable::resize(std::size_t mb) {
+    const std::size_t bytes = std::max<std::size_t>(1, mb) * 1024U * 1024U;
+    const std::size_t new_count = std::max<std::size_t>(1, bytes / sizeof(TTCluster));
+    auto new_clusters = std::make_unique<TTCluster[]>(new_count);
+    clusters_ = std::move(new_clusters);
+    cluster_count_ = new_count;
+    generation_ = 0;
+    ++epoch_;
+}
+
+void TranspositionTable::clear() {
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < cluster_count_; ++i) clusters_[i] = TTCluster{};
+    generation_ = 0;
+    ++epoch_;
+}
+
+TTProbe TranspositionTable::probe(std::uint64_t key) const {
+    // (No forced tail call: the result is a structure returned in memory.)
+    if (lockless_) [[unlikely]] return probe_lockless(key);
+    TTCluster& cluster = cluster_for(key);
+    const std::size_t index = static_cast<std::size_t>(&cluster - clusters_.get());
+    const auto lock = lock_stripe(index);
+    auto snapshot = [&](const TTEntry& entry, bool hit) {
+        TTProbe result;
+        result.entry = entry;
+        result.hit = hit;
+        result.owner_ = this;
+        result.cluster_ = index;
+        result.slot_ = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(&entry - cluster.entries.data());
+        result.epoch_ = epoch_;
+        result.key_ = key;
+        return result;
+    };
+    const std::uint32_t key32 = verification_key(key);
+    for (TTEntry& entry : cluster.entries) {
+        if (entry.key32 == key32 && entry.occupied()) {
+            // Reused entries remain recent even when search returns a TT cutoff.
+            if (entry.generation() != generation_) {
+                entry.gen_bound = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(
+                    (static_cast<unsigned>(generation_) << 3U) | (entry.gen_bound & 7U));
+            }
+            return snapshot(entry, true);
+        }
+    }
+    const TTEntry* replace = &cluster.entries[0];
+    auto relative_age = [this](const TTEntry& entry) {
+        return static_cast<int>((32 + generation_ - entry.generation()) & 31U);
+    };
+    for (const TTEntry& entry : cluster.entries) {
+        if (!entry.occupied()) return snapshot(entry, false);
+        if (entry.depth - 4 * relative_age(entry) < replace->depth - 4 * relative_age(*replace)) replace = &entry;
+    }
+    return snapshot(*replace, false);
+}
+
+void TranspositionTable::store(const TTProbe& probe, std::uint64_t key, int score, int eval, Move move, int depth,
+                               Bound bound, bool pv, std::uint16_t halfmove_clock) {
+    // First, before anything else: with the same parameters this compiles to a
+    // conditional tail jump (UCI LocklessTT, tt_lockless.inc).
+    if (lockless_) [[unlikely]] FORGE_LTT_TAIL return store_lockless(probe, key, score, eval, move, depth, bound, pv, halfmove_clock);
+    // Reject default, foreign or lifecycle-stale handles without touching storage.
+    // Lifecycle operations themselves must not run concurrently with search.
+    if (probe.owner_ != this || probe.epoch_ != epoch_ || probe.key_ != key ||
+        probe.cluster_ >= cluster_count_ || probe.slot_ >= 4) return;
+    const auto lock = lock_stripe(probe.cluster_);
+    TTEntry* entry = &clusters_[probe.cluster_].entries[probe.slot_];
+    const std::uint32_t key32 = verification_key(key);
+    const bool same_position = entry->key32 == key32 && entry->occupied();
+    if (!move.is_none() || !same_position) entry->move = move;
+    if (bound == BoundExact || !same_position || depth + 4 > entry->depth || entry->generation() != generation_) {
+        entry->key32 = key32;
+        entry->score = static_cast<std::int16_t>(std::clamp(score, -32767, 32767));
+        entry->rule50_clock = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(std::min<int>(halfmove_clock, 100));
+        entry->eval = static_cast<std::int16_t>(std::clamp(eval, -32767, 32767));
+        entry->depth = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(std::clamp(depth, 0, 255));
+        entry->gen_bound = static_cast<std::uint8_t>((static_cast<unsigned>(generation_) << 3U) | (pv ? 4U : 0U) | static_cast<unsigned>(bound));
+    }
+}
+
+int TranspositionTable::hashfull() const {
+    if (lockless_) FORGE_LTT_TAIL return hashfull_lockless();
+    int count = 0;
+    const std::size_t sample = std::min<std::size_t>(1000, cluster_count_);
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < sample; ++i) {
+        const auto lock = lock_stripe(i);
+        for (const TTEntry& entry : clusters_[i].entries) {
+            if (entry.occupied() && entry.generation() == generation_) ++count;
+        }
+    }
+    return sample == 0 ? 0 : count * 1000 / (4 * static_cast<int>(sample));
+}
+
+} // namespace forge
diff --git a/src/search/tt_lockless.inc b/src/search/tt_lockless.inc
@@ -0,0 +1,210 @@
+// Search module: lockless transposition-table access (UCI LocklessTT, default
+// off; forge/search_options.hpp). Compiled as part of src/search.cpp, included
+// last so that the default functions keep their AL1 s198 code and placement.
+//
+// An entry (forge/tt.hpp, 16 bytes) is stored as two 64-bit words, each read
+// and written with one relaxed atomic access, without the stripe locks:
+//   second = bytes 8-15 of the entry (score, eval, depth, generation/bound,
+//            rule-50 clock, padding), unchanged;
+//   first  = the move (bytes 4-7) in the high half; in the low half the key
+//            tag XOR a 32-bit hash of the move and of the second word.
+// A read that sees the two words of different writes recovers a key tag that
+// is off by the difference of two hashes, so the probe misses (and a store
+// sees another position) instead of returning a torn entry; a false match
+// needs a 32-bit hash collision. Probe, replacement and store rules are those
+// of the locked functions in tt.inc, statement for statement, so a search with
+// one thread is unchanged (bench identical with the option on).
+// Concurrent writes to one slot can leave its two words from different writes;
+// such a slot no longer matches any key and is replaced like any other entry.
+//
+// Placement: every function here is noinline and in section .text.forge_ltt,
+// which the linker places after all .text. probe(), store() and hashfull() in
+// tt.inc only gained one test of lockless_ that tail-jumps into this file;
+// inlined, the lockless bodies would grow the default functions' frames.
+#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
+#define FORGE_LTT_TEXT [[gnu::section(".text.forge_ltt"), gnu::noinline]]
+#else
+#define FORGE_LTT_TEXT
+#endif
+
+namespace forge {
+
+namespace {
+
+static_assert(sizeof(TTEntry) == 16 && offsetof(TTEntry, move) == 4 && offsetof(TTEntry, score) == 8 &&
+                  offsetof(TTEntry, depth) == 12 && offsetof(TTEntry, gen_bound) == 13 && alignof(TTCluster) == 64,
+              "the lockless TT encoding assumes the 16-byte entry layout");
+static_assert(std::endian::native == std::endian::little, "the lockless TT reads entry fields from its words");
+
+// Hash of the 96 data bits (move + second word) that guards the key tag: the
+// high half of one 64-bit product. Two writes with different data give
+// different products (the multiplier is odd), so a mixed slot keeps its tag
+// only if the products' high halves collide (about 2^-32 for TT data).
+[[nodiscard]] inline std::uint32_t lockless_check(std::uint32_t move_bits, std::uint64_t data) {
+    const std::uint64_t spread = (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(move_bits) << 32U) | move_bits;
+    const std::uint64_t h = (data ^ spread) * 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15ULL;
+    return static_cast<std::uint32_t>(h >> 32);
+}
+
+// Fields of the payload word (entry bytes 8-15) used without decoding.
+[[nodiscard]] inline unsigned payload_depth(std::uint64_t second) { return static_cast<unsigned>(second >> 32U) & 0xFFU; }
+[[nodiscard]] inline unsigned payload_gen_bound(std::uint64_t second) { return static_cast<unsigned>(second >> 40U) & 0xFFU; }
+
+// One 64-bit half of a table entry, accessed atomically. Entries sit at 16-byte
+// offsets in 64-byte aligned clusters, so both halves are 8-byte aligned.
+#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
+using TTWord [[gnu::may_alias]] = std::uint64_t;
+
+[[nodiscard]] inline std::uint64_t load_word(const TTEntry& slot, std::size_t half) {
+    return __atomic_load_n(reinterpret_cast<const TTWord*>(&slot) + half, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
+}
+
+inline void store_word(TTEntry& slot, std::size_t half, std::uint64_t value) {
+    __atomic_store_n(reinterpret_cast<TTWord*>(&slot) + half, value, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
+}
+#else
+[[nodiscard]] inline std::uint64_t load_word(const TTEntry& slot, std::size_t half) {
+    auto* words = reinterpret_cast<std::uint64_t*>(const_cast<TTEntry*>(&slot));
+    return std::atomic_ref<std::uint64_t>(words[half]).load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
+}
+
+inline void store_word(TTEntry& slot, std::size_t half, std::uint64_t value) {
+    auto* words = reinterpret_cast<std::uint64_t*>(&slot);
+    std::atomic_ref<std::uint64_t>(words[half]).store(value, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+}
+#endif
+
+[[nodiscard]] inline std::array<std::uint64_t, 2> encode_entry(const TTEntry& entry) {
+    std::uint32_t move_bits = 0;
+    std::memcpy(&move_bits, &entry.move, sizeof move_bits);
+    std::uint64_t second = 0;
+    std::memcpy(&second, reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&entry) + 8, sizeof second);
+    const std::uint32_t tag = entry.key32 ^ lockless_check(move_bits, second);
+    return {(static_cast<std::uint64_t>(move_bits) << 32U) | tag, second};
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] inline TTEntry decode_entry(std::uint64_t first, std::uint64_t second) {
+    TTEntry entry;
+    const auto move_bits = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(first >> 32U);
+    entry.key32 = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(first) ^ lockless_check(move_bits, second);
+    std::memcpy(&entry.move, &move_bits, sizeof move_bits);
+    std::memcpy(reinterpret_cast<unsigned char*>(&entry) + 8, &second, sizeof second);
+    return entry;
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] inline TTEntry load_lockless(const TTEntry& slot) {
+    const std::uint64_t second = load_word(slot, 1);
+    const std::uint64_t first = load_word(slot, 0);
+    return decode_entry(first, second);
+}
+
+inline void save_lockless(TTEntry& slot, const TTEntry& entry) {
+    const auto words = encode_entry(entry);
+    store_word(slot, 0, words[0]);
+    store_word(slot, 1, words[1]);
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
+FORGE_LTT_TEXT std::array<std::uint64_t, 2> TranspositionTable::encode_lockless(const TTEntry& entry) {
+    return encode_entry(entry);
+}
+
+FORGE_LTT_TEXT TTEntry TranspositionTable::decode_lockless(std::uint64_t first, std::uint64_t second) {
+    return decode_entry(first, second);
+}
+
+FORGE_LTT_TEXT void TranspositionTable::set_lockless(bool lockless) {
+    if (lockless == lockless_) return;
+    lockless_ = lockless;
+    clear();
+}
+
+FORGE_LTT_TEXT TTProbe TranspositionTable::probe_lockless(std::uint64_t key) const {
+    TTCluster& cluster = cluster_for(key);
+    const std::size_t index = static_cast<std::size_t>(&cluster - clusters_.get());
+    // Raw words of the four slots. Occupancy, depth and generation sit in the
+    // payload word; the key check is computed only for occupied slots, in slot
+    // order, until one matches (the locked probe's order of decisions).
+    std::array<std::uint64_t, 4> first{};
+    std::array<std::uint64_t, 4> second{};
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
+        second[i] = load_word(cluster.entries[i], 1);
+        first[i] = load_word(cluster.entries[i], 0);
+    }
+    auto snapshot = [&](std::size_t slot, const TTEntry& entry, bool hit) {
+        TTProbe result;
+        result.entry = entry;
+        result.hit = hit;
+        result.owner_ = this;
+        result.cluster_ = index;
+        result.slot_ = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(slot);
+        result.epoch_ = epoch_;
+        result.key_ = key;
+        return result;
+    };
+    const std::uint32_t key32 = verification_key(key);
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
+        if ((payload_gen_bound(second[i]) & 3U) == BoundNone) continue;
+        const auto move_bits = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(first[i] >> 32U);
+        if ((static_cast<std::uint32_t>(first[i]) ^ lockless_check(move_bits, second[i])) != key32) continue;
+        TTEntry entry = decode_entry(first[i], second[i]);
+        // Reused entries remain recent even when search returns a TT cutoff.
+        if (entry.generation() != generation_) {
+            entry.gen_bound = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(
+                (static_cast<unsigned>(generation_) << 3U) | (entry.gen_bound & 7U));
+            save_lockless(cluster.entries[i], entry);
+        }
+        return snapshot(i, entry, true);
+    }
+    auto priority = [this](std::uint64_t payload) {
+        const unsigned gen_bound = payload_gen_bound(payload);
+        const int relative_age = static_cast<int>((32 + generation_ - (gen_bound >> 3U)) & 31U);
+        return static_cast<int>(payload_depth(payload)) - 4 * relative_age;
+    };
+    std::size_t replace = 0;
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
+        if ((payload_gen_bound(second[i]) & 3U) == BoundNone) return snapshot(i, decode_entry(first[i], second[i]), false);
+        if (priority(second[i]) < priority(second[replace])) replace = i;
+    }
+    return snapshot(replace, decode_entry(first[replace], second[replace]), false);
+}
+
+FORGE_LTT_TEXT void TranspositionTable::store_lockless(const TTProbe& probe, std::uint64_t key, int score, int eval, Move move,
+                                        int depth, Bound bound, bool pv, std::uint16_t halfmove_clock) {
+    // Reject default, foreign or lifecycle-stale handles, as store() does.
+    if (probe.owner_ != this || probe.epoch_ != epoch_ || probe.key_ != key ||
+        probe.cluster_ >= cluster_count_ || probe.slot_ >= 4) return;
+    TTEntry& slot = clusters_[probe.cluster_].entries[probe.slot_];
+    TTEntry entry = load_lockless(slot);
+    const TTEntry before = entry;
+    const std::uint32_t key32 = verification_key(key);
+    const bool same_position = entry.key32 == key32 && entry.occupied();
+    if (!move.is_none() || !same_position) entry.move = move;
+    if (bound == BoundExact || !same_position || depth + 4 > entry.depth || entry.generation() != generation_) {
+        entry.key32 = key32;
+        entry.score = static_cast<std::int16_t>(std::clamp(score, -32767, 32767));
+        entry.rule50_clock = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(std::min<int>(halfmove_clock, 100));
+        entry.eval = static_cast<std::int16_t>(std::clamp(eval, -32767, 32767));
+        entry.depth = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(std::clamp(depth, 0, 255));
+        entry.gen_bound = static_cast<std::uint8_t>((static_cast<unsigned>(generation_) << 3U) | (pv ? 4U : 0U) |
+                                                    static_cast<unsigned>(bound));
+    }
+    if (std::memcmp(&entry, &before, sizeof entry) != 0) save_lockless(slot, entry);
+}
+
+FORGE_LTT_TEXT int TranspositionTable::hashfull_lockless() const {
+    int count = 0;
+    const std::size_t sample = std::min<std::size_t>(1000, cluster_count_);
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < sample; ++i) {
+        for (const TTEntry& slot : clusters_[i].entries) {
+            const TTEntry entry = load_lockless(slot);
+            if (entry.occupied() && entry.generation() == generation_) ++count;
+        }
+    }
+    return sample == 0 ? 0 : count * 1000 / (4 * static_cast<int>(sample));
+}
+
+} // namespace forge

+#undef FORGE_LTT_TEXT

+namespace {
+
+struct ReductionTable {
+    std::array<std::array<std::uint8_t, 64>, 64> quiet{};
+    std::array<std::array<std::uint8_t, 64>, 64> noisy{};
+    ReductionTable() { refresh(); }
+    void refresh() {
+        for (int depth = 1; depth < 64; ++depth) {
+            for (int moves = 1; moves < 64; ++moves) {
+                const double product = std::log(static_cast<double>(depth)) * std::log(static_cast<double>(moves));
+                quiet[static_cast<std::size_t>(depth)][static_cast<std::size_t>(moves)] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(
+                    std::max(0.0, tune::LmrQuietBase / 100.0 + product / (tune::LmrQuietDivisor / 100.0)));
+                noisy[static_cast<std::size_t>(depth)][static_cast<std::size_t>(moves)] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(
+                    std::max(0.0, tune::LmrNoisyBase / 100.0 + product / (tune::LmrNoisyDivisor / 100.0)));
+            }
+        }
+    }
+};
+ReductionTable Reductions;
+
+[[nodiscard]] int reduction(bool quiet, int depth, int moves) {
+    const std::size_t d = static_cast<std::size_t>(std::min(depth, 63));
+    const std::size_t m = static_cast<std::size_t>(std::min(moves, 63));
+    return quiet ? Reductions.quiet[d][m] : Reductions.noisy[d][m];
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] int lmp_threshold(int depth, bool improving) {
+    return improving ? tune::LmpBase + depth * depth : (tune::LmpBase + depth * depth) / 2;
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
+void tune::refresh() { Reductions.refresh(); }
+
+} // namespace forge

+// Search module: UCI "info" output. One line per completed (or revised)
+// iteration, written by the emit lambda of the iterative-deepening driver
+// (src/search/iterative.inc) under uci_output_mutex():
+//   info depth D seldepth S score (cp X | mate M) nodes N nps R hashfull H time T pv m1 m2 ...
+// Compiled as part of src/search.cpp (included exactly once).
+
+namespace forge {
+namespace {
+
+// Always inlined: a second call site (SmpBestThread) must not turn it into an
+// out-of-line function placed ahead of the hot search code.
+[[gnu::always_inline]] inline std::string format_score(int score) {
+    if (is_mate_score(score)) {
+        const int distance = MateValue - std::abs(score);
+        const int mate = (distance + 1) / 2;
+        return "mate " + std::to_string(score > 0 ? mate : -mate);
+    }
+    return "cp " + std::to_string(score);
+}
+
+} // namespace
+} // namespace forge

+// HardDeadline (forge/search_options.hpp): state of one `go` shared by the
+// search thread, its watchdog and the UCI loop.
+struct SearchRun {
+    std::mutex mutex;
+    std::condition_variable cv;
+    bool done = false;  // this go is over: its bestmove is out (or the UCI loop ended it)
+    bool ponder_hit = false;
+    std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point ponder_hit_time{};
+    // Claimed by whoever sends this go's single bestmove (search thread or watchdog).
+    std::atomic<bool> answered{false};
+};
+
+// HardDeadline control threads (the UCI input loop and the watchdog) wait almost
+// all the time; at time-critical priority they wake on time even when the
+// engine's search threads are starved of CPU (e.g. a niced engine sharing a core
+// with other work). Heavy commands run at normal priority. Windows only: other
+// systems need privileges to raise a thread's priority.
+void set_control_priority(bool time_critical) {
+#ifdef _WIN32
+    (void)SetThreadPriority(GetCurrentThread(), time_critical ? THREAD_PRIORITY_TIME_CRITICAL : THREAD_PRIORITY_NORMAL);
+#else
+    (void)time_critical;
+#endif
+}
+
+bool contains(const forge::MoveList& moves, forge::Move move) {
+    return std::find(moves.begin(), moves.end(), move) != moves.end();
+}
+
+// The watchdog's answer, with the search's own fallbacks: the published
+// best/ponder pair if it is a root candidate here (a legal move, inside
+// `searchmoves` when given), else the first candidate, else "0000" (no legal
+// move, or a searchmoves list without a legal move).
+std::string forced_bestmove(const forge::Board& board, bool ponder_enabled, bool restricted,
+                            const std::vector<forge::Move>& search_moves) {
+    const auto [best, ponder] = forge::Searcher::published_moves();
+    forge::MoveList candidates;
+    for (const forge::Move move : board.legal_moves()) {
+        if (!restricted || std::find(search_moves.begin(), search_moves.end(), move) != search_moves.end()) {
+            candidates.push_back(move);
+        }
+    }
+    if (candidates.empty()) return "bestmove 0000";
+    if (best.is_none() || !contains(candidates, best)) return "bestmove " + forge::Board::move_to_uci(candidates.front());
+    std::string line = "bestmove " + forge::Board::move_to_uci(best);
+    if (ponder_enabled && !ponder.is_none()) {
+        forge::Board after = board;
+        forge::Undo undo;
+        (void)after.make_move(best, undo);
+        if (contains(after.legal_moves(), ponder)) line += " ponder " + forge::Board::move_to_uci(ponder);
+    }
+    return line;
+}
+
+// HardDeadline watchdog: one persistent thread, started when the option is set,
+// that raises its own priority once (a thread created at search time would first
+// have to be scheduled at normal priority, which a starved engine cannot count
+// on) and then serves every clocked go: it sleeps until the go's deadline (hard
+// limit + HardDeadline ms; ponder searches from ponderhit) and, if the search has
+// not answered by then, stops it and sends the bestmove itself.
+class DeadlineWatchdog {
+public:
+    struct Job {
+        std::shared_ptr<SearchRun> run;
+        forge::Board board;
+        bool ponder = false;
+        std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point origin{};
+        std::chrono::milliseconds allowance{0};
+        bool ponder_enabled = false;
+        bool restricted = false;  // go searchmoves ...
+        std::vector<forge::Move> search_moves;
+    };
+
+    explicit DeadlineWatchdog(forge::Searcher& searcher) : searcher_(searcher) {}
+    ~DeadlineWatchdog() {
+        {
+            const std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
+            quit_ = true;
+        }
+        cv_.notify_all();
+        if (thread_.joinable()) thread_.join();
+    }
+    DeadlineWatchdog(const DeadlineWatchdog&) = delete;
+    DeadlineWatchdog& operator=(const DeadlineWatchdog&) = delete;
+
+    void start() {
+        if (!thread_.joinable()) thread_ = std::thread([this] { loop(); });
+    }
+
+    void arm(Job job) {
+        start();
+        {
+            const std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
+            job_ = std::move(job);
+        }
+        cv_.notify_all();
+    }
+
+    // Call after the run is marked done: returns once the watchdog no longer
+    // serves it, so a forced bestmove can never come after later output.
+    void finish(const SearchRun* run) {
+        std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
+        if (job_ && job_->run.get() == run) job_.reset();
+        cv_.wait(lock, [this, run] { return serving_ != run; });
+    }
+
+private:
+    void loop() {
+        set_control_priority(true);
+        std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
+        while (true) {
+            cv_.wait(lock, [this] { return quit_ || job_.has_value(); });
+            if (quit_) return;
+            Job job = std::move(*job_);
+            job_.reset();
+            serving_ = job.run.get();
+            lock.unlock();
+            serve(job);
+            lock.lock();
+            serving_ = nullptr;
+            cv_.notify_all();
+        }
+    }
+
+    void serve(const Job& job) {
+        SearchRun& run = *job.run;
+        std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(run.mutex);
+        auto origin = job.origin;
+        if (job.ponder) {
+            run.cv.wait(lock, [&run] { return run.done || run.ponder_hit; });
+            if (!run.ponder_hit) return;
+            origin = run.ponder_hit_time;
+        }
+        if (run.cv.wait_until(lock, origin + job.allowance, [&run] { return run.done; })) return;
+        lock.unlock();
+        if (run.answered.exchange(true)) return;  // the search thread is sending its bestmove
+        searcher_.stop();
+        const std::string line = forced_bestmove(job.board, job.ponder_enabled, job.restricted, job.search_moves);
+        const auto late = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
+            std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - origin).count();
+        {
+            const std::lock_guard<std::mutex> output_lock(forge::uci_output_mutex());
+            std::cout << "info string HardDeadline: no answer within " << job.allowance.count()
+                      << " ms, bestmove sent at " << late << " ms" << std::endl;
+            std::cout << line << std::endl;
+        }
+        lock.lock();
+        run.done = true;
+        lock.unlock();
+        run.cv.notify_all();
+    }
+
+    forge::Searcher& searcher_;
+    std::mutex mutex_;
+    std::condition_variable cv_;
+    std::optional<Job> job_;
+    const SearchRun* serving_ = nullptr;
+    bool quit_ = false;
+    std::thread thread_;
+};
+
+#if defined(FORGE_DATAGEN)
+// S32 build switch (default off): "forge datagen ..." runs the self-play generator instead of the
+// UCI loop; without arguments, or with any other first argument, the engine is unchanged.

+    if (argc > 1 && std::string_view(argv[1]) == "datagen") return forge::datagen::run(argc - 2, argv + 2);
+#else
+#endif
